kernel/levels.go:68 // DetectedLevel is the uniform output of every detector (a line or a zone). For
kernel/levels.go:69 // a line, Lo == Hi == Price. HTF marks a higher-timeframe origin (grading input).
kernel/levels.go:70 type DetectedLevel struct {
kernel/levels.go:71 	Kind       LevelKind `json:"kind"`

kernel/levels.go:74 	Hi         float64   `json:"hi"`          // zone top (== Price for a line)
kernel/levels.go:75 	Label      string    `json:"label"`       // display label, e.g. "PDH", "RN 15500", "nPOC·Tue"
kernel/levels.go:76 	OriginDate string    `json:"origin_date"` // YYYY-MM-DD of formation
kernel/levels.go:77 	HTF        bool      `json:"htf"`         // higher-timeframe origin
kernel/levels.go:78 	// TF is the DETECTION timeframe ("1m"…"4h"; "" = the 1m slice). Drives the
kernel/levels.go:79 	// v3 zone evidence tiers (owner-approved 2026-08-24).
kernel/levels.go:80 	TF string `json:"tf,omitempty"`
kernel/levels.go:81 	// ZonePattern classifies an S/D zone as "reversal" (RBD/DBR — strongest) or
kernel/levels.go:82 	// "continuation" (RBR/DBD — weaker). "" = unknown (older detections).
kernel/levels.go:83 	ZonePattern string `json:"zone_pattern,omitempty"`
kernel/levels.go:84 	Info        string `json:"info,omitempty"` // extra (gap size, fill state, ...)
kernel/levels.go:85 	// FormedAtMs is the formation birth instant (bar open time of the candle
kernel/levels.go:86 	// that completed the pattern), in unix ms. 0 = unknown/older detections.
kernel/levels.go:87 	// The W6 wake loop (2026-08-25) diffs this against the plan row's birth
kernel/levels.go:88 	// time to find events the plan never saw.
kernel/levels.go:89 	FormedAtMs int64 `json:"formed_at_ms,omitempty"`
kernel/levels.go:90 }

kernel/levels.go:97 // zoneLevel builds a banded DetectedLevel (Price = midpoint of [lo,hi]).
kernel/levels.go:98 func zoneLevel(kind LevelKind, lo, hi float64, label, origin string) DetectedLevel {
kernel/levels.go:99 	if hi < lo {
kernel/levels.go:100 		lo, hi = hi, lo
kernel/levels.go:101 	}
kernel/levels.go:102 	return DetectedLevel{Kind: kind, Price: (lo + hi) / 2, Lo: lo, Hi: hi, Label: label, OriginDate: origin}
kernel/levels.go:103 }

kernel/levels_score.go:67 // FilterLevelsByMinGrade drops levels graded below minGrade (A > B > C). An empty

kernel/levels_score.go:70 func FilterLevelsByMinGrade(scored []ScoredLevel, minGrade string) []ScoredLevel {
kernel/levels_score.go:71 	min, ok := levelGradeRank[strings.ToUpper(strings.TrimSpace(minGrade))]

kernel/levels_score.go:73 		return scored
kernel/levels_score.go:74 	}
kernel/levels_score.go:75 	out := make([]ScoredLevel, 0, len(scored))
kernel/levels_score.go:76 	for _, l := range scored {
kernel/levels_score.go:77 		// B2 (2026-08-26) — min_grade Tier-1 exception: Tier-1 structural rows
kernel/levels_score.go:78 		// survive ANY min_grade cut (the map must always carry today's anchors).
kernel/levels_score.go:79 		if levelGradeRank[strings.ToUpper(l.Grade)] >= min || isTier1Kind(l.Kind) {
kernel/levels_score.go:80 			out = append(out, l)
kernel/levels_score.go:81 		}
kernel/levels_score.go:82 	}
kernel/levels_score.go:83 	return out
kernel/levels_score.go:84 }

kernel/levels_score.go:162 // zoneTierFor maps a detection timeframe string to one of the four v3 tiers

kernel/levels_score.go:167 func zoneTierFor(tf string) string {

kernel/levels_score.go:171 	case "30m":
kernel/levels_score.go:172 		return "15m"
kernel/levels_score.go:173 	case "2h":
kernel/levels_score.go:174 		return "1h"
kernel/levels_score.go:175 	case "6h", "8h", "12h":
kernel/levels_score.go:176 		return "4h"
kernel/levels_score.go:177 	default:
kernel/levels_score.go:178 		// Keep the known tier names as-is; anything else → 1m (noise floor),
kernel/levels_score.go:179 		// never a missing-map zero.
kernel/levels_score.go:180 		if _, ok := zoneTFMult[strings.ToLower(strings.TrimSpace(tf))]; ok {
kernel/levels_score.go:181 			return strings.ToLower(strings.TrimSpace(tf))
kernel/levels_score.go:182 		}
kernel/levels_score.go:183 		return "1m"
kernel/levels_score.go:184 	}
kernel/levels_score.go:185 }

kernel/levels_score.go:187 // ConfluenceCap (C14, 2026-08-25) — the confluence count is capped before it

kernel/levels_score.go:192 func ConfluenceCap() int {

kernel/levels_score.go:195 			return n
kernel/levels_score.go:196 		}
kernel/levels_score.go:197 	}
kernel/levels_score.go:198 	return 3
kernel/levels_score.go:199 }

kernel/levels_score.go:201 // zoneSizeMult (C13 / A5, 2026-08-25) — the zone-size axis: a tight small base
kernel/levels_score.go:202 // is stronger than an oversized one (S/D literature: a wide zone is a poorly
kernel/levels_score.go:203 // defined decision point). Banded 0.5..1.25 in daily-ATR units; a missing
kernel/levels_score.go:204 // [Lo,Hi] (line levels, not zones) is neutral 1.0.
kernel/levels_score.go:205 func zoneSizeMult(lo, hi, atr float64) float64 {
kernel/levels_score.go:206 	if lo <= 0 || hi < lo || atr <= 0 {
kernel/levels_score.go:207 		return 1.0
kernel/levels_score.go:208 	}
kernel/levels_score.go:209 	size := (hi - lo) / atr
kernel/levels_score.go:210 	switch {
kernel/levels_score.go:211 	case size <= 0.30:
kernel/levels_score.go:212 		return 1.25
kernel/levels_score.go:213 	case size <= 0.60:
kernel/levels_score.go:214 		return 1.10
kernel/levels_score.go:215 	case size <= 1.00:
kernel/levels_score.go:216 		return 1.0
kernel/levels_score.go:217 	case size <= 1.50:
kernel/levels_score.go:218 		return 0.85
kernel/levels_score.go:219 	case size <= 2.50:
kernel/levels_score.go:220 		return 0.70
kernel/levels_score.go:221 	default:
kernel/levels_score.go:222 		return 0.50
kernel/levels_score.go:223 	}
kernel/levels_score.go:224 }

kernel/levels_score.go:296 // withinTier1Proximity reports whether a pattern level sits within
kernel/levels_score.go:297 // Tier1ProximityTicks of a Tier-1 anchor (band-aware: zones measure from their
kernel/levels_score.go:298 // NEAREST band edge, so a wide zone doesn't piggyback on distance).
kernel/levels_score.go:299 func withinTier1Proximity(l DetectedLevel, pool []DetectedLevel, price float64) bool {
kernel/levels_score.go:300 	// MNQ tick = 0.25 (the same constant clusterToleranceFor uses) → 12 ticks =
kernel/levels_score.go:301 	// 3.00 points. `price` is the anchor for the fallback only.
kernel/levels_score.go:302 	_ = price
kernel/levels_score.go:303 	tol := Tier1ProximityTicks * 0.25
kernel/levels_score.go:304 	dist := func(p float64) float64 {
kernel/levels_score.go:305 		switch {
kernel/levels_score.go:306 		case l.Lo < l.Hi && p < l.Lo:
kernel/levels_score.go:307 			return l.Lo - p
kernel/levels_score.go:308 		case l.Lo < l.Hi && p > l.Hi:
kernel/levels_score.go:309 			return p - l.Hi
kernel/levels_score.go:310 		default:
kernel/levels_score.go:311 			return 0
kernel/levels_score.go:312 		}
kernel/levels_score.go:313 	}
kernel/levels_score.go:314 	for _, o := range pool {
kernel/levels_score.go:315 		if !isTier1Kind(o.Kind) {
kernel/levels_score.go:316 			continue
kernel/levels_score.go:317 		}
kernel/levels_score.go:318 		if math.Abs(o.Price-l.Price) <= tol || dist(o.Price) <= tol {
kernel/levels_score.go:319 			return true
kernel/levels_score.go:320 		}
kernel/levels_score.go:321 	}
kernel/levels_score.go:322 	return false
kernel/levels_score.go:323 }

kernel/levels_score.go:356 // freshMult maps a freshness grade to a score multiplier. "" → fresh.
kernel/levels_score.go:357 // P1c design rule: a consumed level is NOT zeroed (that deleted the map's best
kernel/levels_score.go:358 // levels) — it role-flips and stays seated at a reduced score.
kernel/levels_score.go:359 func freshMult(f string) float64 {
kernel/levels_score.go:360 	switch strings.ToLower(strings.TrimSpace(f)) {
kernel/levels_score.go:361 	case "", "a", "fresh":
kernel/levels_score.go:362 		return 1.0
kernel/levels_score.go:363 	case "b":
kernel/levels_score.go:364 		return 0.8
kernel/levels_score.go:365 	case "c", "tested":
kernel/levels_score.go:366 		return 0.6
kernel/levels_score.go:367 	case "done", "consumed":
kernel/levels_score.go:368 		return 0.5 // role-flipped, still on the map
kernel/levels_score.go:369 	default:
kernel/levels_score.go:370 		return 1.0
kernel/levels_score.go:371 	}
kernel/levels_score.go:372 }
kernel/levels_score.go:373
kernel/levels_score.go:374 // zoneFreshMult is the Pack B (2026-08-26) ZONE freshness ladder — 1.0 / 0.6 /
kernel/levels_score.go:375 // 0.3 / 0.15. Zones (S/D/FVG/IFVG/OB) decay hard: a twice-tested base is
kernel/levels_score.go:376 // mostly printed. ANCHORS (line levels) keep the original ladder — "anchors
kernel/levels_score.go:377 // no-decay" per spec, i.e. the volume ladder applies to ZONES ONLY.
kernel/levels_score.go:378 func zoneFreshMult(f string) float64 {
kernel/levels_score.go:379 	switch strings.ToLower(strings.TrimSpace(f)) {
kernel/levels_score.go:380 	case "", "a", "fresh":
kernel/levels_score.go:381 		return 1.0
kernel/levels_score.go:382 	case "b":
kernel/levels_score.go:383 		return 0.6
kernel/levels_score.go:384 	case "c", "tested":
kernel/levels_score.go:385 		return 0.3
kernel/levels_score.go:386 	case "done", "consumed":
kernel/levels_score.go:387 		return 0.15
kernel/levels_score.go:388 	default:
kernel/levels_score.go:389 		return 1.0
kernel/levels_score.go:390 	}
kernel/levels_score.go:391 }
kernel/levels_score.go:392
kernel/levels_score.go:393 // ScoreLevels applies the day-trade lock + confluence grading and returns the
kernel/levels_score.go:394 // seated TOP-N graded levels, nearest-first. `freshness` returns a level's grade

kernel/levels_score.go:396 // proximityK is the resolved day-trade lock half-width in daily-ATR multiples

kernel/levels_score.go:402
kernel/levels_score.go:403 // scoreLevelsPool is the full scorer (lock → grade → collapse → seat → top-N).
kernel/levels_score.go:404 func scoreLevelsPool(levels []DetectedLevel, price, dATR float64, freshness func(DetectedLevel) string, maxLevels int, proximityK float64) []ScoredLevel {
kernel/levels_score.go:405 	if price <= 0 || dATR <= 0 {
kernel/levels_score.go:406 		return nil
kernel/levels_score.go:407 	}
kernel/levels_score.go:408 	if maxLevels <= 0 {
kernel/levels_score.go:409 		maxLevels = DefaultMaxLevels
kernel/levels_score.go:410 	}
kernel/levels_score.go:411 	if proximityK <= 0 {
kernel/levels_score.go:412 		proximityK = ActivationWindowK
kernel/levels_score.go:413 	}
kernel/levels_score.go:414 	band := proximityK * dATR
kernel/levels_score.go:415 	// S1 (mega-research 2026-08-26) — confBand = 0.10 × daily-range proxy:
kernel/levels_score.go:416 	// ±35pt at dATR≈350 — wide enough that distant rows count as "confluence".
kernel/levels_score.go:417 	// Documented, not changed here (the proximity re-tune is config-side).
kernel/levels_score.go:418 	confBand := 0.10 * dATR // cluster tolerance
kernel/levels_score.go:419
kernel/levels_score.go:420 	// Proximity filter (day-trade lock).
kernel/levels_score.go:421 	inBand := make([]DetectedLevel, 0, len(levels))
kernel/levels_score.go:422 	for _, l := range levels {
kernel/levels_score.go:423 		if math.Abs(l.Price-price) <= band {
kernel/levels_score.go:424 			inBand = append(inBand, l)
kernel/levels_score.go:425 		}
kernel/levels_score.go:426 	}
kernel/levels_score.go:427
kernel/levels_score.go:428 	scored := make([]ScoredLevel, 0, len(inBand))
kernel/levels_score.go:429 	for _, l := range inBand {
kernel/levels_score.go:430 		fRaw := ""
kernel/levels_score.go:431 		if freshness != nil {
kernel/levels_score.go:432 			fRaw = freshness(l)
kernel/levels_score.go:433 		}
kernel/levels_score.go:434 		fm := freshMult(fRaw)
kernel/levels_score.go:435 		if isZoneKind(l.Kind) {
kernel/levels_score.go:436 			// Pack B (2026-08-26) — the zone freshness ladder is steeper than
kernel/levels_score.go:437 			// the anchor ladder (1.0/0.6/0.3/0.15 vs 1.0/0.8/0.6/0.5).
kernel/levels_score.go:438 			fm = zoneFreshMult(fRaw)
kernel/levels_score.go:439 		}
kernel/levels_score.go:440 		if fm == 0 {
kernel/levels_score.go:441 			continue // consumed
kernel/levels_score.go:442 		}
kernel/levels_score.go:443 		// B3 (2026-08-26) — confluence counts DISTINCT FAMILIES within the
kernel/levels_score.go:444 		// cluster band, never raw levels: three VWAP-band rows are one family
kernel/levels_score.go:445 		// and must not triple-inflate the grade. Capped (C14, env
kernel/levels_score.go:446 		// CONFLUENCE_CAP, default 3) before it multiplies the score.
kernel/levels_score.go:447 		conf := 0
kernel/levels_score.go:448 		seenFamilies := map[string]bool{}
kernel/levels_score.go:449 		for _, o := range inBand {
kernel/levels_score.go:450 			if o.Price == l.Price && o.Kind == l.Kind && o.Label == l.Label {
kernel/levels_score.go:451 				continue
kernel/levels_score.go:452 			}
kernel/levels_score.go:453 			if math.Abs(o.Price-l.Price) <= confBand {
kernel/levels_score.go:454 				fam := levelFamily(o.Kind)
kernel/levels_score.go:455 				if fam != levelFamily(l.Kind) && !seenFamilies[fam] {
kernel/levels_score.go:456 					seenFamilies[fam] = true
kernel/levels_score.go:457 					conf++
kernel/levels_score.go:458 				}
kernel/levels_score.go:459 			}
kernel/levels_score.go:460 		}
kernel/levels_score.go:461 		// C14 (2026-08-25) — diminishing returns: the confluence count feeding
kernel/levels_score.go:462 		// the score is capped (env CONFLUENCE_CAP, default 3).
kernel/levels_score.go:463 		effConf := float64(conf)
kernel/levels_score.go:464 		if capC := ConfluenceCap(); conf > capC {
kernel/levels_score.go:465 			effConf = float64(capC)
kernel/levels_score.go:466 		}
kernel/levels_score.go:467 		if isZoneKind(l.Kind) && conf == 0 {
kernel/levels_score.go:468 			// P0.1 (2026-08-19) — a zone with an HTF origin seats on its own
kernel/levels_score.go:469 			// merit (grade C): large-account auctions don't need a crowd. Pure
kernel/levels_score.go:470 			// intraday S/D + FVG/OB remain confluence-only, never standalone.
kernel/levels_score.go:471 			if !(l.HTF) {
kernel/levels_score.go:472 				continue
kernel/levels_score.go:473 			}
kernel/levels_score.go:474 		}
kernel/levels_score.go:475 		htf := 1.0
kernel/levels_score.go:476 		if l.HTF {
kernel/levels_score.go:477 			htf = 1.2
kernel/levels_score.go:478 		}
kernel/levels_score.go:479 		var score float64
kernel/levels_score.go:480 		if isZoneKind(l.Kind) {
kernel/levels_score.go:481 			// v3 zone grading: kindBase × size × TFmult × freshness × confluence.
kernel/levels_score.go:482 			score = zoneEvidence(l) * zoneSizeMult(l.Lo, l.Hi, dATR) * fm * (1 + 0.20*effConf) * zoneTFMult[zoneTierFor(l.TF)]
kernel/levels_score.go:483 		} else {
kernel/levels_score.go:484 			score = typeEvidence(l.Kind) * fm * (1 + 0.20*effConf) * htf
kernel/levels_score.go:485 		}
kernel/levels_score.go:486 		grade := gradeFromScore(score)
kernel/levels_score.go:487 		if isZoneKind(l.Kind) {
kernel/levels_score.go:488 			// v3 floors/caps: 1m zones never above C; 15m floor B, cap B
kernel/levels_score.go:489 			// (entry TF, not a zone-defining TF — research R20); 1h floor B,
kernel/levels_score.go:490 			// cap A (1h wave 2026-08-25 — setup/context rung may reach A with
kernel/levels_score.go:491 			// confluence); 4h floor B and may reach A (the professionals' level).
kernel/levels_score.go:492 			switch zoneTierFor(l.TF) {
kernel/levels_score.go:493 			case "15m":
kernel/levels_score.go:494 				if grade != "B" {
kernel/levels_score.go:495 					grade = "B"
kernel/levels_score.go:496 				}
kernel/levels_score.go:497 			case "1h":
kernel/levels_score.go:498 				if grade == "C" {
kernel/levels_score.go:499 					grade = "B"
kernel/levels_score.go:500 				}
kernel/levels_score.go:501 			case "4h":
kernel/levels_score.go:502 				if grade == "C" {
kernel/levels_score.go:503 					grade = "B"
kernel/levels_score.go:504 				}
kernel/levels_score.go:505 			default: // 1m tier
kernel/levels_score.go:506 				if grade != "C" {
kernel/levels_score.go:507 					grade = "C"
kernel/levels_score.go:508 				}
kernel/levels_score.go:509 			}
kernel/levels_score.go:510 			// B2 (2026-08-26) — pattern-above-C only within
kernel/levels_score.go:511 			// TIER1_PROXIMITY_TICKS of a Tier-1 anchor: a pattern (zone) earns
kernel/levels_score.go:512 			// grade B/A ONLY when it sits beside a Tier-1 structural level.
kernel/levels_score.go:513 			// Otherwise the pattern is a context mark, grade C.
kernel/levels_score.go:514 			if grade != "C" && !withinTier1Proximity(l, inBand, price) {
kernel/levels_score.go:515 				grade = "C"
kernel/levels_score.go:516 			}
kernel/levels_score.go:517 		}
kernel/levels_score.go:518 		scored = append(scored, ScoredLevel{
kernel/levels_score.go:519 			DetectedLevel: l,
kernel/levels_score.go:520 			Grade:         grade,
kernel/levels_score.go:521 			Fresh:         freshLabel(fRaw),
kernel/levels_score.go:522 			Score:         score,
kernel/levels_score.go:523 			Confluence:    conf,
kernel/levels_score.go:524 			Distance:      l.Price - price,
kernel/levels_score.go:525 			Role:          RoleFor(l, fRaw),
kernel/levels_score.go:526 		})
kernel/levels_score.go:527 	}
kernel/levels_score.go:528
kernel/levels_score.go:529 	// P0.4 (2026-08-19) — CLUSTER COLLAPSE: levels within LevelClusterTolerance
kernel/levels_score.go:530 	// merge into ONE entry keeping the strongest provenance (highest score,
kernel/levels_score.go:531 	// then today-priority kind, then nearer distance). Before this, an equal-
kernel/levels_score.go:532 	// high/low family 3 points wide shipped as 4 separate "A" rows and the
kernel/levels_score.go:533 	// planner copied every duplicate into the plan (grade inflation + wasted
kernel/levels_score.go:534 	// seats).
kernel/levels_score.go:535 	scored = collapseLevelClusters(scored, clusterToleranceFor(price))
kernel/levels_score.go:536
kernel/levels_score.go:537 	// Seat: today's priority kinds first, then the rest, both by score desc
kernel/levels_score.go:538 	// (deterministic tie-break: nearer distance, then lower price).
kernel/levels_score.go:539 	sort.SliceStable(scored, func(i, j int) bool {
kernel/levels_score.go:540 		pi, pj := isTodayPriority(scored[i].Kind), isTodayPriority(scored[j].Kind)
kernel/levels_score.go:541 		if pi != pj {
kernel/levels_score.go:542 			return pi
kernel/levels_score.go:543 		}
kernel/levels_score.go:544 		if scored[i].Score != scored[j].Score {
kernel/levels_score.go:545 			return scored[i].Score > scored[j].Score
kernel/levels_score.go:546 		}
kernel/levels_score.go:547 		di, dj := math.Abs(scored[i].Distance), math.Abs(scored[j].Distance)
kernel/levels_score.go:548 		if di != dj {
kernel/levels_score.go:549 			return di < dj
kernel/levels_score.go:550 		}
kernel/levels_score.go:551 		return scored[i].Price < scored[j].Price
kernel/levels_score.go:552 	})
kernel/levels_score.go:553
kernel/levels_score.go:554 	// P0.1 (2026-08-19) — BOTH-SIDE SEATING: the plan must always carry levels
kernel/levels_score.go:555 	// on BOTH sides of price. On a gap-down day every today-priority kind sits
kernel/levels_score.go:556 	// above price and used to fill all 8 seats (the 2026-08-18 one-sided map
kernel/levels_score.go:557 	// that left the model with "no trigger" for 110 points of breakdown). When
kernel/levels_score.go:558 	// the pure top-N leaves one side under-supplied while candidates exist,
kernel/levels_score.go:559 	// swap in the best in-band levels from the missing side.
kernel/levels_score.go:560 	// G2/G3/G6 (2026-08-24) — seatHTF first: the model only ever sees the
kernel/levels_score.go:561 	// top-N table, so HTF swing/zone levels must WIN seats to reach the plan.
kernel/levels_score.go:562 	// The P0.1 side-balance pass may still swap a promoted seat if a side ends
kernel/levels_score.go:563 	// under-supplied (the hard rule wins).
kernel/levels_score.go:564 	scored = seatHTF(scored, maxLevels)
kernel/levels_score.go:565 	scored = SeatVolumeFamily(scored, maxLevels) // Pack B (2026-08-26) — E1 volume-family seat
kernel/levels_score.go:566 	scored = seatBothSides(scored, maxLevels)
kernel/levels_score.go:567
kernel/levels_score.go:568 	if len(scored) > maxLevels {
kernel/levels_score.go:569 		scored = scored[:maxLevels]
kernel/levels_score.go:570 	}
kernel/levels_score.go:571
kernel/levels_score.go:572 	// S1/A13 (mega-research 2026-08-26) — the seated table was never logged, so

kernel/levels_score.go:601 func ScoreLevelsMinGradeFull(levels []DetectedLevel, price, dATR float64, freshness func(DetectedLevel) string, maxLevels int, proximityK float64, minGrade string) ([]ScoredLevel, []ScoredLevel) {

kernel/levels_score.go:613 			filtered = filtered[:eff]
kernel/levels_score.go:614 		}
kernel/levels_score.go:615 		return filtered, pool
kernel/levels_score.go:616 	}

kernel/levels_score.go:621 		if pi != pj {

kernel/levels_score.go:623 		}

kernel/levels_score.go:637 		filtered = filtered[:eff]
kernel/levels_score.go:638 	}
kernel/levels_score.go:639 	sort.SliceStable(filtered, func(i, j int) bool {
kernel/levels_score.go:640 		return math.Abs(filtered[i].Distance) < math.Abs(filtered[j].Distance)

kernel/levels_score.go:674 // LevelClusterTicks is the P0.4 cluster-collapse width in ticks (12 MNQ ticks =
kernel/levels_score.go:675 // 3.00 points): levels closer than this are the SAME reference on an intraday

kernel/levels_score.go:679

kernel/levels_score.go:686
kernel/levels_score.go:687 // collapseLevelClusters merges levels within tol of a STRONGER survivor (kept in
kernel/levels_score.go:688 // the same relative position). Kept: highest score, then today-priority kind,
kernel/levels_score.go:689 // then nearer distance, then lower price. The survivor's confluence absorbs the
kernel/levels_score.go:690 // merged member count; duplicates are removed before seating.
kernel/levels_score.go:691 func collapseLevelClusters(scored []ScoredLevel, tol float64) []ScoredLevel {
kernel/levels_score.go:692 	if len(scored) < 2 || tol <= 0 {
kernel/levels_score.go:693 		return scored
kernel/levels_score.go:694 	}
kernel/levels_score.go:695 	// Prefer survivors: sort by strength so the first member of any cluster is
kernel/levels_score.go:696 	// the keeper.
kernel/levels_score.go:697 	order := append([]ScoredLevel(nil), scored...)
kernel/levels_score.go:698 	sort.SliceStable(order, func(i, j int) bool {
kernel/levels_score.go:699 		a, b := order[i], order[j]
kernel/levels_score.go:700 		ai, bi := isTodayPriority(a.Kind), isTodayPriority(b.Kind)
kernel/levels_score.go:701 		if ai != bi {
kernel/levels_score.go:702 			return ai
kernel/levels_score.go:703 		}
kernel/levels_score.go:704 		if a.Score != b.Score {
kernel/levels_score.go:705 			return a.Score > b.Score
kernel/levels_score.go:706 		}
kernel/levels_score.go:707 		di, dj := math.Abs(a.Distance), math.Abs(b.Distance)
kernel/levels_score.go:708 		if di != dj {
kernel/levels_score.go:709 			return di < dj
kernel/levels_score.go:710 		}
kernel/levels_score.go:711 		return a.Price < b.Price
kernel/levels_score.go:712 	})
kernel/levels_score.go:713 	kept := make([]ScoredLevel, 0, len(scored))
kernel/levels_score.go:714 	for _, cand := range order {
kernel/levels_score.go:715 		if isZoneKind(cand.Kind) {
kernel/levels_score.go:716 			// Zones are BANDS with their own semantics (proximal/distal),
kernel/levels_score.go:717 			// never duplicates of a line level — they survive collapse.
kernel/levels_score.go:718 			kept = append(kept, cand)
kernel/levels_score.go:719 			continue
kernel/levels_score.go:720 		}
kernel/levels_score.go:721 		merged := false
kernel/levels_score.go:722 		for i := range kept {
kernel/levels_score.go:723 			if isZoneKind(kept[i].Kind) {
kernel/levels_score.go:724 				continue
kernel/levels_score.go:725 			}
kernel/levels_score.go:726 			if math.Abs(kept[i].Price-cand.Price) <= tol {
kernel/levels_score.go:727 				kept[i].Confluence += cand.Confluence + 1
kernel/levels_score.go:728 				merged = true
kernel/levels_score.go:729 				break
kernel/levels_score.go:730 			}
kernel/levels_score.go:731 		}
kernel/levels_score.go:732 		if !merged {
kernel/levels_score.go:733 			kept = append(kept, cand)
kernel/levels_score.go:734 		}
kernel/levels_score.go:735 	}
kernel/levels_score.go:736 	return kept
kernel/levels_score.go:737 }
kernel/levels_score.go:738

kernel/levels_score.go:835 	}

kernel/levels_score.go:935 func seatHTF(scored []ScoredLevel, maxLevels int) []ScoredLevel {
kernel/levels_score.go:936 	if maxLevels <= 0 {
kernel/levels_score.go:937 		maxLevels = DefaultMaxLevels
kernel/levels_score.go:938 	}

kernel/levels_score.go:941 	}
kernel/levels_score.go:942 	const maxHTFSeats = 2
kernel/levels_score.go:943 	head := append([]ScoredLevel(nil), scored[:maxLevels]...)
kernel/levels_score.go:944 	tail := append([]ScoredLevel(nil), scored[maxLevels:]...)
kernel/levels_score.go:945 	seated := 0
kernel/levels_score.go:946 	for _, l := range head {
kernel/levels_score.go:947 		if isHTFSeatEligible(l) {
kernel/levels_score.go:948 			seated++
kernel/levels_score.go:949 		}
kernel/levels_score.go:950 	}
kernel/levels_score.go:951 	need := maxHTFSeats - seated
kernel/levels_score.go:952 	if need <= 0 {
kernel/levels_score.go:953 		return scored
kernel/levels_score.go:954 	}
kernel/levels_score.go:955 	var cands []ScoredLevel
kernel/levels_score.go:956 	for _, l := range tail {
kernel/levels_score.go:957 		if isHTFSeatEligible(l) {
kernel/levels_score.go:958 			cands = append(cands, l)
kernel/levels_score.go:959 		}
kernel/levels_score.go:960 	}
kernel/levels_score.go:961 	for need > 0 {
kernel/levels_score.go:962 		if len(cands) == 0 {
kernel/levels_score.go:963 			break
kernel/levels_score.go:964 		}
kernel/levels_score.go:965 		dropIdx := -1
kernel/levels_score.go:966 		for i := len(head) - 1; i >= 0; i-- {
kernel/levels_score.go:967 			if isTodayPriority(head[i].Kind) || isHTFSeatEligible(head[i]) {
kernel/levels_score.go:968 				continue
kernel/levels_score.go:969 			}
kernel/levels_score.go:970 			dropIdx = i
kernel/levels_score.go:971 			break
kernel/levels_score.go:972 		}
kernel/levels_score.go:973 		if dropIdx < 0 {
kernel/levels_score.go:974 			break
kernel/levels_score.go:975 		}
kernel/levels_score.go:976 		cand := cands[0]
kernel/levels_score.go:977 		cands = cands[1:]
kernel/levels_score.go:978 		// Remove the candidate from the tail (it now sits in the head) and
kernel/levels_score.go:979 		// move the demoted head entry into the tail.
kernel/levels_score.go:980 		for i, l := range tail {
kernel/levels_score.go:981 			if l.Price == cand.Price && l.Kind == cand.Kind && l.Label == cand.Label && l.HTF == cand.HTF {
kernel/levels_score.go:982 				tail = append(tail[:i], tail[i+1:]...)
kernel/levels_score.go:983 				break
kernel/levels_score.go:984 			}
kernel/levels_score.go:985 		}
kernel/levels_score.go:986 		tail = append(tail, head[dropIdx])
kernel/levels_score.go:987 		head = append(head[:dropIdx], head[dropIdx+1:]...)
kernel/levels_score.go:988 		head = append(head, cand)
kernel/levels_score.go:989 		need--
kernel/levels_score.go:990 	}
kernel/levels_score.go:991 	out := append(head, tail...)
kernel/levels_score.go:992 	// Restore strict seating order (same sort as the pre-pass).
kernel/levels_score.go:993 	sort.SliceStable(out, func(i, j int) bool {
kernel/levels_score.go:994 		pi, pj := isTodayPriority(out[i].Kind), isTodayPriority(out[j].Kind)
kernel/levels_score.go:995 		if pi != pj {
kernel/levels_score.go:996 			return pi
kernel/levels_score.go:997 		}
kernel/levels_score.go:998 		if out[i].Score != out[j].Score {
kernel/levels_score.go:999 			return out[i].Score > out[j].Score
kernel/levels_score.go:1000 		}
kernel/levels_score.go:1001 		di, dj := math.Abs(out[i].Distance), math.Abs(out[j].Distance)
kernel/levels_score.go:1002 		if di != dj {
kernel/levels_score.go:1003 			return di < dj
kernel/levels_score.go:1004 		}
kernel/levels_score.go:1005 		return out[i].Price < out[j].Price
kernel/levels_score.go:1006 	})
kernel/levels_score.go:1007 	return out
kernel/levels_score.go:1008 }

kernel/levels_score.go:1010 func seatBothSides(scored []ScoredLevel, maxLevels int) []ScoredLevel {
kernel/levels_score.go:1011 	if maxLevels <= 0 {
kernel/levels_score.go:1012 		maxLevels = DefaultMaxLevels
kernel/levels_score.go:1013 	}
kernel/levels_score.go:1014 	n := len(scored)
kernel/levels_score.go:1015 	if n <= maxLevels || maxLevels < 2*MinSideLevels {
kernel/levels_score.go:1016 		// Nothing was cut (the cap is not the constraint) or the cap is too

kernel/levels_score.go:1019 	}

kernel/levels_score.go:1022 	for side := 0; side < 2; side++ {
kernel/levels_score.go:1023 		count := func(s []ScoredLevel, below bool) int {
kernel/levels_score.go:1024 			c := 0
kernel/levels_score.go:1025 			for _, l := range s {

kernel/levels_score.go:1031 		}
kernel/levels_score.go:1032 		for _, below := range []bool{true, false} {
kernel/levels_score.go:1033 			if count(seated, below) >= MinSideLevels {
kernel/levels_score.go:1034 				continue
kernel/levels_score.go:1035 			}
kernel/levels_score.go:1036 			need := MinSideLevels - count(seated, below)
kernel/levels_score.go:1037 			// Candidates from the missing side, in seating order (strongest

kernel/levels_score.go:1041 				if (l.Distance < 0) == below {
kernel/levels_score.go:1042 					cands = append(cands, l)
kernel/levels_score.go:1043 				}
kernel/levels_score.go:1044 			}
kernel/levels_score.go:1045 			// Drop the weakest seated levels of the OPPOSITE side to make room.
kernel/levels_score.go:1046 			// Pack B (2026-08-26) — a seated volume-family row is protected from
kernel/levels_score.go:1047 			// this swap (the E1 seat guarantee wins over side rebalance).
kernel/levels_score.go:1048 			for len(cands) > 0 && need > 0 {

kernel/levels_score.go:1050 				for i := len(seated) - 1; i >= 0; i-- {

kernel/levels_score.go:1054 					}
kernel/levels_score.go:1055 				}
kernel/levels_score.go:1056 				if dropIdx < 0 {
kernel/levels_score.go:1057 					break
kernel/levels_score.go:1058 				}
kernel/levels_score.go:1059 				rest = append(rest, seated[dropIdx])
kernel/levels_score.go:1060 				seated = append(seated[:dropIdx], seated[dropIdx+1:]...)
kernel/levels_score.go:1061 				seated = append(seated, cands[0])
kernel/levels_score.go:1062 				cands = cands[1:]
kernel/levels_score.go:1063 				need--
kernel/levels_score.go:1064 			}
kernel/levels_score.go:1065 		}
kernel/levels_score.go:1066 	}
kernel/levels_score.go:1067 	// Restore strict seating order for the final nearest-first pass downstream.
kernel/levels_score.go:1068 	sort.SliceStable(seated, func(i, j int) bool {
kernel/levels_score.go:1069 		pi, pj := isTodayPriority(seated[i].Kind), isTodayPriority(seated[j].Kind)

kernel/levels_score.go:1075 		}

kernel/levels_score.go:1079 		}

kernel/levels_score.go:1114 	}
kernel/levels_score.go:1115 	// W-why-no-trades (2026-08-18): "do not chase price between them" was the
kernel/levels_score.go:1116 	// base-prompt line the model quoted while waiting cycle after cycle (0/3
kernel/levels_score.go:1117 	// stripped-prompt replays produced an entry). Between levels, a confirmed
kernel/levels_score.go:1118 	// momentum/breakout setup stays tradeable.

kernel/levels_assemble.go:192 // dedupeSameKind collapses same-kind duplicates within 1 MNQ tick (register

kernel/levels_assemble.go:196 func dedupeSameKind(levels []DetectedLevel) []DetectedLevel {
kernel/levels_assemble.go:197 	const dedupeTick = 0.25 // MNQ tick (the same constant Tier1ProximityTicks cites)
kernel/levels_assemble.go:198 	out := make([]DetectedLevel, 0, len(levels))
kernel/levels_assemble.go:199 	for _, l := range levels {
kernel/levels_assemble.go:200 		dup := false
kernel/levels_assemble.go:201 		for _, o := range out {
kernel/levels_assemble.go:202 			if o.Kind == l.Kind && math.Abs(o.Price-l.Price) <= dedupeTick {
kernel/levels_assemble.go:203 				dup = true
kernel/levels_assemble.go:204 				break
kernel/levels_assemble.go:205 			}
kernel/levels_assemble.go:206 		}

kernel/levels_assemble.go:209 		}
kernel/levels_assemble.go:210 	}

kernel/levels_assemble.go:214 // DetectHTFLevels (G2/G3, 2026-08-24) — per-TF swing/zone detection on the

kernel/levels_assemble.go:222 func DetectHTFLevels(fetch func(tf string, count int) []market.Kline, timeframes []string, symbol string, now time.Time) []DetectedLevel {

kernel/levels_assemble.go:225 	}

kernel/levels_assemble.go:228 		tick = 0.25
kernel/levels_assemble.go:229 	}
kernel/levels_assemble.go:230 	seen := map[string]bool{}

kernel/levels_assemble.go:285 // DailyRangeProxy estimates the typical daily range from intraday bars by
kernel/levels_assemble.go:286 // averaging each COMPLETED CME session-day's range (the developing day is
kernel/levels_assemble.go:287 // skipped). 0 when no completed day is present — the caller falls back.
kernel/levels_assemble.go:288 // Improves as the cache / durable store warms forward.
kernel/levels_assemble.go:289 // S1 (mega-research 2026-08-26) — RENAMED from DailyATRProxy: this is a
kernel/levels_assemble.go:290 // session-day H−L range mean, NOT an ATR. The name now says so.
kernel/levels_assemble.go:291 func DailyRangeProxy(bars []market.Kline, now time.Time) float64 {
kernel/levels_assemble.go:292 	cb := closedBars(bars, now)
kernel/levels_assemble.go:293 	if len(cb) == 0 {
kernel/levels_assemble.go:294 		return 0
kernel/levels_assemble.go:295 	}
kernel/levels_assemble.go:296 	type dayRange struct{ hi, lo float64 }
kernel/levels_assemble.go:297 	days := map[string]*dayRange{}
kernel/levels_assemble.go:298 	for _, b := range cb {
kernel/levels_assemble.go:299 		key := CMESessionDayKey(time.UnixMilli(b.OpenTime))
kernel/levels_assemble.go:300 		d := days[key]
kernel/levels_assemble.go:301 		if d == nil {
kernel/levels_assemble.go:302 			d = &dayRange{hi: math.Inf(-1), lo: math.Inf(1)}
kernel/levels_assemble.go:303 			days[key] = d
kernel/levels_assemble.go:304 		}
kernel/levels_assemble.go:305 		d.hi = math.Max(d.hi, b.High)
kernel/levels_assemble.go:306 		d.lo = math.Min(d.lo, b.Low)
kernel/levels_assemble.go:307 	}
kernel/levels_assemble.go:308 	nowFut := CMESessionDayKey(now)
kernel/levels_assemble.go:309 	var sum float64
kernel/levels_assemble.go:310 	var n int
kernel/levels_assemble.go:311 	for key, d := range days {
kernel/levels_assemble.go:312 		if key == nowFut {
kernel/levels_assemble.go:313 			continue // developing day is not a completed range
kernel/levels_assemble.go:314 		}
kernel/levels_assemble.go:315 		if d.hi > d.lo {
kernel/levels_assemble.go:316 			sum += d.hi - d.lo
kernel/levels_assemble.go:317 			n++
kernel/levels_assemble.go:318 		}
kernel/levels_assemble.go:319 	}
kernel/levels_assemble.go:320 	if n > 0 {
kernel/levels_assemble.go:321 		return sum / float64(n)
kernel/levels_assemble.go:322 	}
kernel/levels_assemble.go:323 	// Fallback: the developing day's range so far.
kernel/levels_assemble.go:324 	if d := days[nowFut]; d != nil && d.hi > d.lo {
kernel/levels_assemble.go:325 		return d.hi - d.lo
kernel/levels_assemble.go:326 	}
kernel/levels_assemble.go:327 	return 0
kernel/levels_assemble.go:328 }

kernel/levels_intraday.go:12 // P1.2 — intraday computed levels: round numbers, unfilled gaps, opening range +

kernel/levels_intraday.go:16 // RoundNumberLevels emits round-number levels at 100/50/25 spacing within
kernel/levels_intraday.go:17 // proximityK×dATR of price. proximityK is the resolved day-trade lock width
kernel/levels_intraday.go:18 // (proximity_filter_atr, default 1.5); k≤0 → the spec constant. A price that is
kernel/levels_intraday.go:19 // a multiple of a larger step is claimed by that step (100 > 50 > 25), so each
kernel/levels_intraday.go:20 // round price appears once, tagged with its strongest granularity.
kernel/levels_intraday.go:21 func RoundNumberLevels(price, dATR, proximityK float64) []DetectedLevel {
kernel/levels_intraday.go:22 	if price <= 0 || dATR <= 0 {
kernel/levels_intraday.go:23 		return nil
kernel/levels_intraday.go:24 	}
kernel/levels_intraday.go:25 	if proximityK <= 0 {
kernel/levels_intraday.go:26 		proximityK = ActivationWindowK
kernel/levels_intraday.go:27 	}
kernel/levels_intraday.go:28 	band := proximityK * dATR
kernel/levels_intraday.go:29 	lo, hi := price-band, price+band
kernel/levels_intraday.go:30 	steps := []struct {
kernel/levels_intraday.go:31 		step float64
kernel/levels_intraday.go:32 		tag  string
kernel/levels_intraday.go:33 	}{{100, "100"}, {50, "50"}, {25, "25"}}
kernel/levels_intraday.go:34
kernel/levels_intraday.go:35 	seen := map[int64]bool{}
kernel/levels_intraday.go:36 	var out []DetectedLevel
kernel/levels_intraday.go:37 	for _, s := range steps {
kernel/levels_intraday.go:38 		n0 := int64(math.Ceil(lo / s.step))
kernel/levels_intraday.go:39 		for n := n0; float64(n)*s.step <= hi; n++ {
kernel/levels_intraday.go:40 			m := float64(n) * s.step
kernel/levels_intraday.go:41 			key := int64(math.Round(m * 100))
kernel/levels_intraday.go:42 			if seen[key] {
kernel/levels_intraday.go:43 				continue
kernel/levels_intraday.go:44 			}
kernel/levels_intraday.go:45 			seen[key] = true
kernel/levels_intraday.go:46 			out = append(out, lineLevel(KindRound, m, fmt.Sprintf("RN %.0f (%s)", m, s.tag), "", false))
kernel/levels_intraday.go:47 		}
kernel/levels_intraday.go:48 	}
kernel/levels_intraday.go:49 	sort.Slice(out, func(i, j int) bool { return out[i].Price < out[j].Price })
kernel/levels_intraday.go:50 	return out
kernel/levels_intraday.go:51 }

kernel/levels_intraday.go:53 // GapLevels finds UNFILLED price gaps between consecutive closed bars whose size
kernel/levels_intraday.go:54 // ≥ minGapATR×atr, and emits each gap's fill target (the near/prior-session edge
kernel/levels_intraday.go:55 // price toward which price would fill). A gap is filled once a later bar trades
kernel/levels_intraday.go:56 // back through its near edge.
kernel/levels_intraday.go:57 func GapLevels(bars []market.Kline, atr, minGapATR float64, now time.Time) []DetectedLevel {
kernel/levels_intraday.go:58 	if atr <= 0 || minGapATR <= 0 {
kernel/levels_intraday.go:59 		return nil
kernel/levels_intraday.go:60 	}
kernel/levels_intraday.go:61 	nowMs := now.UnixMilli()
kernel/levels_intraday.go:62 	loc := chicago()
kernel/levels_intraday.go:63
kernel/levels_intraday.go:64 	closed := make([]market.Kline, 0, len(bars))
kernel/levels_intraday.go:65 	for i := range bars {
kernel/levels_intraday.go:66 		if bars[i].CloseTime < nowMs {
kernel/levels_intraday.go:67 			closed = append(closed, bars[i])
kernel/levels_intraday.go:68 		}
kernel/levels_intraday.go:69 	}
kernel/levels_intraday.go:70 	if len(closed) < 2 {
kernel/levels_intraday.go:71 		return nil
kernel/levels_intraday.go:72 	}
kernel/levels_intraday.go:73 	minGap := minGapATR * atr
kernel/levels_intraday.go:74
kernel/levels_intraday.go:75 	var out []DetectedLevel
kernel/levels_intraday.go:76 	for i := 1; i < len(closed); i++ {
kernel/levels_intraday.go:77 		p, c := closed[i-1], closed[i]
kernel/levels_intraday.go:78 		var gLo, gHi float64
kernel/levels_intraday.go:79 		var up bool
kernel/levels_intraday.go:80 		switch {
kernel/levels_intraday.go:81 		case c.Low > p.High && c.Low-p.High >= minGap:
kernel/levels_intraday.go:82 			gLo, gHi, up = p.High, c.Low, true
kernel/levels_intraday.go:83 		case c.High < p.Low && p.Low-c.High >= minGap:
kernel/levels_intraday.go:84 			gLo, gHi, up = c.High, p.Low, false
kernel/levels_intraday.go:85 		default:
kernel/levels_intraday.go:86 			continue
kernel/levels_intraday.go:87 		}
kernel/levels_intraday.go:88 		// Fill check: gap-up fills when a later bar's low reaches the near edge
kernel/levels_intraday.go:89 		// (gLo); gap-down fills when a later bar's high reaches gHi.
kernel/levels_intraday.go:90 		filled := false
kernel/levels_intraday.go:91 		for j := i + 1; j < len(closed); j++ {
kernel/levels_intraday.go:92 			if up && closed[j].Low <= gLo {
kernel/levels_intraday.go:93 				filled = true
kernel/levels_intraday.go:94 				break
kernel/levels_intraday.go:95 			}
kernel/levels_intraday.go:96 			if !up && closed[j].High >= gHi {
kernel/levels_intraday.go:97 				filled = true
kernel/levels_intraday.go:98 				break
kernel/levels_intraday.go:99 			}
kernel/levels_intraday.go:100 		}
kernel/levels_intraday.go:101 		if filled {
kernel/levels_intraday.go:102 			continue
kernel/levels_intraday.go:103 		}
kernel/levels_intraday.go:104 		target := gLo
kernel/levels_intraday.go:105 		dir := "↑"
kernel/levels_intraday.go:106 		if !up {
kernel/levels_intraday.go:107 			target = gHi
kernel/levels_intraday.go:108 			dir = "↓"
kernel/levels_intraday.go:109 		}
kernel/levels_intraday.go:110 		origin := time.UnixMilli(c.OpenTime).In(loc).Format("2006-01-02")
kernel/levels_intraday.go:111 		out = append(out, DetectedLevel{
kernel/levels_intraday.go:112 			Kind:       KindGap,
kernel/levels_intraday.go:113 			Price:      target,
kernel/levels_intraday.go:114 			Lo:         gLo,
kernel/levels_intraday.go:115 			Hi:         gHi,
kernel/levels_intraday.go:116 			Label:      fmt.Sprintf("GAP%s fill %.2f", dir, target),
kernel/levels_intraday.go:117 			OriginDate: origin,
kernel/levels_intraday.go:118 			Info:       fmt.Sprintf("size %.2f, unfilled", gHi-gLo),
kernel/levels_intraday.go:119 		})
kernel/levels_intraday.go:120 	}
kernel/levels_intraday.go:121 	return out
kernel/levels_intraday.go:122 }
kernel/levels_intraday.go:123
kernel/levels_intraday.go:124 // OpeningRangeLevels emits today's opening-range (first 5m) high/low and initial
kernel/levels_intraday.go:125 // balance (first 60m, i.e. 9:30–10:30 ET) high/low plus the 1.5× and 2× IB
kernel/levels_intraday.go:126 // extensions. Returns nil before today's RTH open (nothing to measure yet).
kernel/levels_intraday.go:127 func OpeningRangeLevels(bars []market.Kline, reg SessionRegistry, now time.Time) []DetectedLevel {
kernel/levels_intraday.go:128 	loc := chicago()
kernel/levels_intraday.go:129 	nowCT := now.In(loc)
kernel/levels_intraday.go:130 	nowMs := now.UnixMilli()
kernel/levels_intraday.go:131
kernel/levels_intraday.go:132 	// RTH open = 08:30 CT of now's calendar day (from the NY registry row).
kernel/levels_intraday.go:133 	openMin := 8*60 + 30
kernel/levels_intraday.go:134 	if ny, ok := reg.SessionByName(SessionNY); ok {
kernel/levels_intraday.go:135 		if m, ok := parseHHMM(ny.WindowStartCT); ok {
kernel/levels_intraday.go:136 			openMin = m
kernel/levels_intraday.go:137 		}
kernel/levels_intraday.go:138 	}
kernel/levels_intraday.go:139 	rthOpen := time.Date(nowCT.Year(), nowCT.Month(), nowCT.Day(), openMin/60, openMin%60, 0, 0, loc)
kernel/levels_intraday.go:140 	if nowCT.Before(rthOpen) {
kernel/levels_intraday.go:141 		return nil // pre-open: no OR/IB yet
kernel/levels_intraday.go:142 	}
kernel/levels_intraday.go:143 	orEnd := rthOpen.Add(5 * time.Minute)
kernel/levels_intraday.go:144 	ibEnd := rthOpen.Add(60 * time.Minute)
kernel/levels_intraday.go:145
kernel/levels_intraday.go:146 	var orH, orL, ibH, ibL float64 = math.Inf(-1), math.Inf(1), math.Inf(-1), math.Inf(1)
kernel/levels_intraday.go:147 	var hasOR, hasIB bool
kernel/levels_intraday.go:148 	for i := range bars {

kernel/levels_multiday.go:12 // Pure + deterministic: given intraday bars, the session registry, and `now`,

kernel/levels_multiday.go:216 // Coverage minimums for multi-day buckets (P0.4-F, 2026-08-25): a prior-day
kernel/levels_multiday.go:217 // bucket under 15h of closed 1m bars is truncated (boot gap / bot-down window)
kernel/levels_multiday.go:218 // and its low/high/close are unreliable. Prior-week needs ≥3 full days and
kernel/levels_multiday.go:219 // prior-month ≥7 full days — with the ~33h 1m ring these can never pass, which
kernel/levels_multiday.go:220 // is CORRECT: those anchors must come from a multi-day source, not the ring.

kernel/levels_multiday.go:222 	priorDayMinBars   = 900   // 15h of 1m bars
kernel/levels_multiday.go:223 	priorWeekMinBars  = 4320  // 3 days
kernel/levels_multiday.go:224 	priorMonthMinBars = 10080 // 7 days
kernel/levels_multiday.go:225 )
kernel/levels_multiday.go:226
kernel/levels_multiday.go:227 // mostRecentPriorDay returns the latest calendar-day key strictly before today
kernel/levels_multiday.go:228 // that has data AND meets the coverage minimum, or "" if none.
kernel/levels_multiday.go:229 func mostRecentPriorDay(cal map[string]*calDayAgg, todayCal string, minBars int) string {
kernel/levels_multiday.go:230 	best := ""
kernel/levels_multiday.go:231 	for k, a := range cal {
kernel/levels_multiday.go:232 		if k < todayCal && k > best && a.count >= minBars {
kernel/levels_multiday.go:233 			best = k
kernel/levels_multiday.go:234 		}
kernel/levels_multiday.go:235 	}
kernel/levels_multiday.go:236 	return best
kernel/levels_multiday.go:237 }
kernel/levels_multiday.go:238
kernel/levels_multiday.go:239 // pwCovered reports whether the [start, end) week window has ≥ minBars closed
kernel/levels_multiday.go:240 // bars across its calendar-day buckets.
kernel/levels_multiday.go:241 func pwCovered(cal map[string]*calDayAgg, start, end time.Time, minBars int) bool {
kernel/levels_multiday.go:242 	n := 0
kernel/levels_multiday.go:243 	for k, a := range cal {
kernel/levels_multiday.go:244 		d, err := time.ParseInLocation("2006-01-02", k, chicago())
kernel/levels_multiday.go:245 		if err != nil {
kernel/levels_multiday.go:246 			continue
kernel/levels_multiday.go:247 		}
kernel/levels_multiday.go:248 		if !d.Before(start) && d.Before(end) {
kernel/levels_multiday.go:249 			n += a.count
kernel/levels_multiday.go:250 		}
kernel/levels_multiday.go:251 	}
kernel/levels_multiday.go:252 	return n >= minBars
kernel/levels_multiday.go:253 }
kernel/levels_multiday.go:254
kernel/levels_multiday.go:255 // pmCovered reports whether the [start, end) month window has ≥ minBars closed
kernel/levels_multiday.go:256 // bars across its calendar-day buckets.
kernel/levels_multiday.go:257 func pmCovered(cal map[string]*calDayAgg, start, end time.Time, minBars int) bool {
kernel/levels_multiday.go:258 	return pwCovered(cal, start, end, minBars)
kernel/levels_multiday.go:259 }
kernel/levels_multiday.go:260
kernel/levels_multiday.go:261 // startOfWeekCT returns Monday 00:00 of t's ISO week, in t's location.
kernel/levels_multiday.go:262 func startOfWeekCT(t time.Time) time.Time {
kernel/levels_multiday.go:263 	daysSinceMon := (int(t.Weekday()) + 6) % 7 // Sun=0 → 6, Mon=1 → 0, ...
kernel/levels_multiday.go:264 	d := time.Date(t.Year(), t.Month(), t.Day(), 0, 0, 0, 0, t.Location())
kernel/levels_multiday.go:265 	return d.AddDate(0, 0, -daysSinceMon)
kernel/levels_multiday.go:266 }

kernel/levels_volume.go:11 // Pack B — VOLUME FAMILY detectors (owner override 2026-08-26, research
kernel/levels_volume.go:12 // authority). The backward Phase-0 replay is WAIVED (no historical bars
kernel/levels_volume.go:13 // existed when the volume wave was first scoped) — forward validation via the
kernel/levels_volume.go:14 // B4 level_stats table replaces it.
kernel/levels_volume.go:15 //
kernel/levels_volume.go:16 // Phase 0(a) re-confirmed on live frames 2026-08-26: NT8 bar_update/bars_historical
kernel/levels_volume.go:17 // frames carry real per-minute V (1m MNQ: V=460 / 728 / 631 observed), and the
kernel/levels_volume.go:18 // bars table persists them. The volume substrate is REAL, not simulated.
kernel/levels_volume.go:19 // Phase 0(b): session VWAP / POC profile need only 1m OHLCV — computable from
kernel/levels_volume.go:20 // the bars table + BarCache, no tick data required.
kernel/levels_volume.go:21 //
kernel/levels_volume.go:22 // All detectors here are pure and deterministic on the closed 1m slice the
kernel/levels_volume.go:23 // kernel already holds. VWAP is anchored at the CME session-day boundary
kernel/levels_volume.go:24 // (17:00 CT, kernel/cme_calendar.go — the same roll the rest of the system
kernel/levels_volume.go:25 // uses), so it re-emits dynamically every cycle as bars close.
kernel/levels_volume.go:26
kernel/levels_volume.go:27 // ── session VWAP + σ bands ─────────────────────────────────────────────────
kernel/levels_volume.go:28
kernel/levels_volume.go:29 // SessionVWAPLevels (B1) — session-anchored VWAP (17:00 CT roll) plus the
kernel/levels_volume.go:30 // ±1σ fair-value bands, emitted as lines. Dynamic re-emit: recomputed from
kernel/levels_volume.go:31 // the closed bars on EVERY cycle, so the level moves with the auction.
kernel/levels_volume.go:32 // VWAP bands are the institutional mean-reversion envelope (research: the
kernel/levels_volume.go:33 // bulk of session volume trades inside ±1σ; rejection there is the classic
kernel/levels_volume.go:34 // fade signal). Emits nothing until ≥2 closed bars exist in the session.
kernel/levels_volume.go:35 func SessionVWAPLevels(bars []market.Kline, now time.Time) []DetectedLevel {
kernel/levels_volume.go:36 	cb := closedBars(bars, now)
kernel/levels_volume.go:37 	if len(cb) == 0 {
kernel/levels_volume.go:38 		return nil
kernel/levels_volume.go:39 	}
kernel/levels_volume.go:40 	sessStart := CMESessionDayStart(now).UnixMilli()
kernel/levels_volume.go:41 	session := make([]market.Kline, 0, len(cb))
kernel/levels_volume.go:42 	for _, b := range cb {
kernel/levels_volume.go:43 		if b.OpenTime >= sessStart {
kernel/levels_volume.go:44 			session = append(session, b)
kernel/levels_volume.go:45 		}
kernel/levels_volume.go:46 	}
kernel/levels_volume.go:47 	vwap, sd := vwapAndStdev(session)
kernel/levels_volume.go:48 	if vwap <= 0 || len(session) < 2 {
kernel/levels_volume.go:49 		return nil
kernel/levels_volume.go:50 	}
kernel/levels_volume.go:51 	day := time.UnixMilli(sessStart).In(CTLocation()).Format("2006-01-02")
kernel/levels_volume.go:52 	return []DetectedLevel{
kernel/levels_volume.go:53 		lineLevel(KindVWAP, vwap, "VWAP", day, false),
kernel/levels_volume.go:54 		lineLevel(KindVWAP, vwap+sd, "VWAP+1σ", day, false),
kernel/levels_volume.go:55 		lineLevel(KindVWAP, vwap-sd, "VWAP−1σ", day, false),
kernel/levels_volume.go:56 		// Level-truth wave (2026-08-27) — ±2σ bands EMITTED at 0.85 per owner
kernel/levels_volume.go:57 		// ruling (the dispatched spec listed them; the emission was missing).
kernel/levels_volume.go:58 		lineLevel(KindVWAP2S, vwap+2*sd, "VWAP+2σ", day, false),
kernel/levels_volume.go:59 		lineLevel(KindVWAP2S, vwap-2*sd, "VWAP−2σ", day, false),
kernel/levels_volume.go:60 	}
kernel/levels_volume.go:61 }
kernel/levels_volume.go:62
kernel/levels_volume.go:63 // vwapAndStdev computes the session VWAP (typical price = (H+L+C)/3, weighted
kernel/levels_volume.go:64 // by volume) and the volume-weighted standard deviation of typical prices.
kernel/levels_volume.go:65 func vwapAndStdev(bars []market.Kline) (vwap, sd float64) {
kernel/levels_volume.go:66 	var pv, v float64
kernel/levels_volume.go:67 	for _, b := range bars {
kernel/levels_volume.go:68 		tp := (b.High + b.Low + b.Close) / 3
kernel/levels_volume.go:69 		pv += tp * b.Volume
kernel/levels_volume.go:70 		v += b.Volume
kernel/levels_volume.go:71 	}
kernel/levels_volume.go:72 	if v <= 0 {
kernel/levels_volume.go:73 		return 0, 0
kernel/levels_volume.go:74 	}
kernel/levels_volume.go:75 	vwap = pv / v
kernel/levels_volume.go:76 	var acc float64
kernel/levels_volume.go:77 	for _, b := range bars {
kernel/levels_volume.go:78 		tp := (b.High + b.Low + b.Close) / 3
kernel/levels_volume.go:79 		d := tp - vwap
kernel/levels_volume.go:80 		acc += b.Volume * d * d
kernel/levels_volume.go:81 	}
kernel/levels_volume.go:82 	sd = math.Sqrt(acc / v)
kernel/levels_volume.go:83 	return vwap, sd
kernel/levels_volume.go:84 }
kernel/levels_volume.go:85
kernel/levels_volume.go:86 // ── extended VWAP (since-cash-close anchor) ────────────────────────────────
kernel/levels_volume.go:87
kernel/levels_volume.go:88 // EVWAPLevels (B1) — extended VWAP anchored at the most recent 15:00 CT CASH
kernel/levels_volume.go:89 // CLOSE (RTH close), spanning the close hour + the whole overnight + today's
kernel/levels_volume.go:90 // session. Overnight-positioned funds mark eVWAP; the distance between session
kernel/levels_volume.go:91 // VWAP and eVWAP measures overnight inventory.
kernel/levels_volume.go:92 //
kernel/levels_volume.go:93 // A2 (mega-research 2026-08-26) — the old 16:00 CT anchor was a DEGENERATE
kernel/levels_volume.go:94 // DUPLICATE of session VWAP: no bars exist 16:00–17:00 CT (maintenance halt),
kernel/levels_volume.go:95 // so both windows were byte-identical and eVWAP burned a seat for nothing.
kernel/levels_volume.go:96 // 15:00 CT makes it a real, distinct object ("since-cash-close" VWAP).
kernel/levels_volume.go:97 func EVWAPLevels(bars []market.Kline, now time.Time) []DetectedLevel {
kernel/levels_volume.go:98 	cb := closedBars(bars, now)
kernel/levels_volume.go:99 	if len(cb) == 0 {
kernel/levels_volume.go:100 		return nil
kernel/levels_volume.go:101 	}
kernel/levels_volume.go:102 	ct := now.In(CTLocation())
kernel/levels_volume.go:103 	anchorCT := time.Date(ct.Year(), ct.Month(), ct.Day(), 15, 0, 0, 0, CTLocation())
kernel/levels_volume.go:104 	if ct.Hour() < 15 {
kernel/levels_volume.go:105 		anchorCT = anchorCT.AddDate(0, 0, -1)
kernel/levels_volume.go:106 	}
kernel/levels_volume.go:107 	anchorMs := anchorCT.UnixMilli()
kernel/levels_volume.go:108 	win := make([]market.Kline, 0, len(cb))
kernel/levels_volume.go:109 	for _, b := range cb {
kernel/levels_volume.go:110 		if b.OpenTime >= anchorMs {
kernel/levels_volume.go:111 			win = append(win, b)
kernel/levels_volume.go:112 		}
kernel/levels_volume.go:113 	}
kernel/levels_volume.go:114 	ev, _ := vwapAndStdev(win)
kernel/levels_volume.go:115 	if ev <= 0 || len(win) < 2 {
kernel/levels_volume.go:116 		return nil
kernel/levels_volume.go:117 	}
kernel/levels_volume.go:118 	return []DetectedLevel{lineLevel(KindEVWAP, ev, "eVWAP", anchorCT.Format("2006-01-02"), false)}
kernel/levels_volume.go:119 }
kernel/levels_volume.go:120
kernel/levels_volume.go:121 // ── prior-day volume profile (pdPOC / VAH / VAL) ───────────────────────────
kernel/levels_volume.go:122
kernel/levels_volume.go:123 // pdProfileCache memoizes a COMPLETED prior session-day's profile (cached at
kernel/levels_volume.go:124 // roll — spec B1). A prior session-day's bars are immutable once the current
kernel/levels_volume.go:125 // day's 17:00 CT roll passed, so the cache is safe forever.
kernel/levels_volume.go:126 var pdProfileCache sync.Map // dayKey → []DetectedLevel
kernel/levels_volume.go:127
kernel/levels_volume.go:128 // PriorDayProfileLevels (B1) — the prior CME session-day's point of control
kernel/levels_volume.go:129 // (max-volume price) and the 70% value area (VAH/VAL). Cached per day at roll.
kernel/levels_volume.go:130 // POC/VAH/VAL are the auction's accepted-value references: the open inside/
kernel/levels_volume.go:131 // outside value area is the single most robust day-type classifier.
kernel/levels_volume.go:132 func PriorDayProfileLevels(bars []market.Kline, now time.Time) []DetectedLevel {
kernel/levels_volume.go:133 	cb := closedBars(bars, now)
kernel/levels_volume.go:134 	if len(cb) == 0 {
kernel/levels_volume.go:135 		return nil
kernel/levels_volume.go:136 	}
kernel/levels_volume.go:137 	curDay := CMESessionDayStart(now)
kernel/levels_volume.go:138 	priorStart := curDay.AddDate(0, 0, -1).UnixMilli()
kernel/levels_volume.go:139 	priorEnd := curDay.UnixMilli()
kernel/levels_volume.go:140 	dayKey := time.UnixMilli(priorStart).In(CTLocation()).Format("2006-01-02")
kernel/levels_volume.go:141 	if v, ok := pdProfileCache.Load(dayKey); ok {
kernel/levels_volume.go:142 		return v.([]DetectedLevel)
kernel/levels_volume.go:143 	}
kernel/levels_volume.go:144 	var prior []market.Kline
kernel/levels_volume.go:145 	for _, b := range cb {
kernel/levels_volume.go:146 		if b.OpenTime >= priorStart && b.OpenTime < priorEnd {
kernel/levels_volume.go:147 			prior = append(prior, b)
kernel/levels_volume.go:148 		}
kernel/levels_volume.go:149 	}
kernel/levels_volume.go:150 	if len(prior) == 0 {
kernel/levels_volume.go:151 		return nil
kernel/levels_volume.go:152 	}
kernel/levels_volume.go:153 	out := profileLevels(prior, dayKey, "")
kernel/levels_volume.go:154 	if len(out) > 0 {
kernel/levels_volume.go:155 		pdProfileCache.Store(dayKey, out)
kernel/levels_volume.go:156 	}
kernel/levels_volume.go:157 	return out
kernel/levels_volume.go:158 }
kernel/levels_volume.go:159
kernel/levels_volume.go:160 // profileLevels computes POC + 70% value area from a closed 1m series.
kernel/levels_volume.go:161 // prefix labels the rows ("pd" for the prior-day profile, "" for session).
kernel/levels_volume.go:162 func profileLevels(bars []market.Kline, dayKey, prefix string) []DetectedLevel {
kernel/levels_volume.go:163 	if len(bars) == 0 {
kernel/levels_volume.go:164 		return nil
kernel/levels_volume.go:165 	}
kernel/levels_volume.go:166 	const binCount = 120
kernel/levels_volume.go:167 	var hi, lo float64
kernel/levels_volume.go:168 	for i, b := range bars {
kernel/levels_volume.go:169 		if i == 0 || b.High > hi {
kernel/levels_volume.go:170 			hi = b.High
kernel/levels_volume.go:171 		}
kernel/levels_volume.go:172 		if i == 0 || b.Low < lo {
kernel/levels_volume.go:173 			lo = b.Low
kernel/levels_volume.go:174 		}
kernel/levels_volume.go:175 	}
kernel/levels_volume.go:176 	if hi <= lo {
kernel/levels_volume.go:177 		return nil
kernel/levels_volume.go:178 	}
kernel/levels_volume.go:179 	bin := (hi - lo) / binCount
kernel/levels_volume.go:180 	if bin <= 0 {
kernel/levels_volume.go:181 		bin = 0.25
kernel/levels_volume.go:182 	}
kernel/levels_volume.go:183 	vols := make([]float64, binCount)
kernel/levels_volume.go:184 	total := 0.0
kernel/levels_volume.go:185 	for _, b := range bars {
kernel/levels_volume.go:186 		idx := int((b.Close - lo) / bin)
kernel/levels_volume.go:187 		if idx < 0 {
kernel/levels_volume.go:188 			idx = 0
kernel/levels_volume.go:189 		}
kernel/levels_volume.go:190 		if idx >= binCount {
kernel/levels_volume.go:191 			idx = binCount - 1
kernel/levels_volume.go:192 		}
kernel/levels_volume.go:193 		vols[idx] += b.Volume
kernel/levels_volume.go:194 		total += b.Volume
kernel/levels_volume.go:195 	}
kernel/levels_volume.go:196 	if total <= 0 {
kernel/levels_volume.go:197 		return nil
kernel/levels_volume.go:198 	}
kernel/levels_volume.go:199 	pocIdx, pocV := 0, vols[0]
kernel/levels_volume.go:200 	for i, v := range vols {
kernel/levels_volume.go:201 		if v > pocV {
kernel/levels_volume.go:202 			pocIdx, pocV = i, v
kernel/levels_volume.go:203 		}
kernel/levels_volume.go:204 	}
kernel/levels_volume.go:205 	poc := lo + (float64(pocIdx)+0.5)*bin
kernel/levels_volume.go:206 	// 70% value area: walk down/up from the POC bin until ≥70% of volume.
kernel/levels_volume.go:207 	need := 0.70 * total
kernel/levels_volume.go:208 	acc := pocV
kernel/levels_volume.go:209 	loIdx, hiIdx := pocIdx, pocIdx
kernel/levels_volume.go:210 	for acc < need {
kernel/levels_volume.go:211 		below := loIdx - 1
kernel/levels_volume.go:212 		above := hiIdx + 1
kernel/levels_volume.go:213 		up := 0.0
kernel/levels_volume.go:214 		down := 0.0
kernel/levels_volume.go:215 		if above < binCount {
kernel/levels_volume.go:216 			up = vols[above]
kernel/levels_volume.go:217 		}
kernel/levels_volume.go:218 		if below >= 0 {
kernel/levels_volume.go:219 			down = vols[below]
kernel/levels_volume.go:220 		}
kernel/levels_volume.go:221 		if up >= down && above < binCount {
kernel/levels_volume.go:222 			hiIdx = above
kernel/levels_volume.go:223 			acc += up
kernel/levels_volume.go:224 		} else if below >= 0 {
kernel/levels_volume.go:225 			loIdx = below
kernel/levels_volume.go:226 			acc += down
kernel/levels_volume.go:227 		} else if above < binCount {
kernel/levels_volume.go:228 			hiIdx = above
kernel/levels_volume.go:229 			acc += up
kernel/levels_volume.go:230 		} else {
kernel/levels_volume.go:231 			break
kernel/levels_volume.go:232 		}
kernel/levels_volume.go:233 	}
kernel/levels_volume.go:234 	vah := lo + (float64(hiIdx)+1)*bin
kernel/levels_volume.go:235 	val := lo + float64(loIdx)*bin
kernel/levels_volume.go:236 	return []DetectedLevel{
kernel/levels_volume.go:237 		lineLevel(KindPOC, poc, prefix+"POC", dayKey, false),
kernel/levels_volume.go:238 		lineLevel(KindVAH, vah, prefix+"VAH", dayKey, false),
kernel/levels_volume.go:239 		lineLevel(KindVAL, val, prefix+"VAL", dayKey, false),
kernel/levels_volume.go:240 	}
kernel/levels_volume.go:241 }
kernel/levels_volume.go:242
kernel/levels_volume.go:243 // ── naked POC (10-session retire-on-touch) ────────────────────────────────
kernel/levels_volume.go:244
kernel/levels_volume.go:245 // nPOCRetireTick is the retire-on-touch tolerance (register A4: the spec's
kernel/levels_volume.go:246 // ±1-tick rule). A bar retires a naked POC only when it brackets BEYOND
kernel/levels_volume.go:247 // ±1 tick — a 0.01-pt graze no longer consumes the magnet. MNQ tick = 0.25.
kernel/levels_volume.go:248 const nPOCRetireTick = 0.25
kernel/levels_volume.go:249
kernel/levels_volume.go:250 // NakedPOCLevels (B1) — prior session-days' POC that price has NOT traded
kernel/levels_volume.go:251 // back through since birth = naked POC (unfinished business; price revisits).
kernel/levels_volume.go:252 // RETIRE-ON-TOUCH: any later session bar whose range covers the POC ±1 tick
kernel/levels_volume.go:253 // retires it (it has been revisited → no longer naked). Older than 10 sessions
kernel/levels_volume.go:254 // → retired regardless (spec). The live cache covers ~2 sessions; the
kernel/levels_volume.go:255 // 10-session window becomes fully exercisable as the 90-day bars table warms
kernel/levels_volume.go:256 // forward (validation, B4).
kernel/levels_volume.go:257 func NakedPOCLevels(bars []market.Kline, now time.Time) []DetectedLevel {
kernel/levels_volume.go:258 	cb := closedBars(bars, now)
kernel/levels_volume.go:259 	if len(cb) == 0 {
kernel/levels_volume.go:260 		return nil
kernel/levels_volume.go:261 	}
kernel/levels_volume.go:262 	curDay := CMESessionDayStart(now)
kernel/levels_volume.go:263 	type dayPOC struct {
kernel/levels_volume.go:264 		day   string
kernel/levels_volume.go:265 		poc   float64
kernel/levels_volume.go:266 		birth int64 // first bar open of the day (touch comparisons start after)
kernel/levels_volume.go:267 	}
kernel/levels_volume.go:268 	var days []dayPOC
kernel/levels_volume.go:269 	for d := 1; d <= 10; d++ {
kernel/levels_volume.go:270 		dayStart := curDay.AddDate(0, 0, -d)
kernel/levels_volume.go:271 		startMs, endMs := dayStart.UnixMilli(), dayStart.AddDate(0, 0, 1).UnixMilli()
kernel/levels_volume.go:272 		var dayBars []market.Kline
kernel/levels_volume.go:273 		for _, b := range cb {
kernel/levels_volume.go:274 			if b.OpenTime >= startMs && b.OpenTime < endMs {
kernel/levels_volume.go:275 				dayBars = append(dayBars, b)
kernel/levels_volume.go:276 			}
kernel/levels_volume.go:277 		}
kernel/levels_volume.go:278 		if len(dayBars) == 0 {
kernel/levels_volume.go:279 			continue
kernel/levels_volume.go:280 		}
kernel/levels_volume.go:281 		prof := profileLevels(dayBars, "", "")
kernel/levels_volume.go:282 		if len(prof) == 0 {
kernel/levels_volume.go:283 			continue
kernel/levels_volume.go:284 		}
kernel/levels_volume.go:285 		days = append(days, dayPOC{
kernel/levels_volume.go:286 			day:   dayStart.In(CTLocation()).Format("2006-01-02"),
kernel/levels_volume.go:287 			poc:   prof[0].Price,
kernel/levels_volume.go:288 			birth: dayBars[len(dayBars)-1].CloseTime,
kernel/levels_volume.go:289 		})
kernel/levels_volume.go:290 	}
kernel/levels_volume.go:291 	var out []DetectedLevel
kernel/levels_volume.go:292 	for _, d := range days {
kernel/levels_volume.go:293 		touched := false
kernel/levels_volume.go:294 		for _, b := range cb {
kernel/levels_volume.go:295 			if b.OpenTime <= d.birth {
kernel/levels_volume.go:296 				continue
kernel/levels_volume.go:297 			}
kernel/levels_volume.go:298 			// A4/S4 — ±1-tick tolerance: a graze inside the tick band does NOT
kernel/levels_volume.go:299 			// retire the POC; the bar must bracket beyond ±1 tick.
kernel/levels_volume.go:300 			if b.Low <= d.poc-nPOCRetireTick && b.High >= d.poc+nPOCRetireTick {
kernel/levels_volume.go:301 				touched = true
kernel/levels_volume.go:302 				break
kernel/levels_volume.go:303 			}
kernel/levels_volume.go:304 		}
kernel/levels_volume.go:305 		if touched {
kernel/levels_volume.go:306 			continue // retire-on-touch
kernel/levels_volume.go:307 		}
kernel/levels_volume.go:308 		out = append(out, lineLevel(KindNPOC, d.poc, "nPOC·"+d.day, d.day, false))
kernel/levels_volume.go:309 	}
kernel/levels_volume.go:310 	return out
kernel/levels_volume.go:311 }
kernel/levels_volume.go:312

kernel/levels_volume.go:326 	for _, b := range cb {
kernel/levels_volume.go:327 		if b.OpenTime >= startMs && b.OpenTime < endMs {

kernel/levels_volume.go:339 // SETTLevels (B1) — prior settlement, approximated by the prior session-day's
kernel/levels_volume.go:340 // FINAL 1m close (the 16:00 CT CME settle). Settlement is the official
kernel/levels_volume.go:341 // overnight reference — breakouts are measured against it, not midnight.
kernel/levels_volume.go:342 func SETTLevels(bars []market.Kline, now time.Time) []DetectedLevel {

kernel/levels_volume.go:348 	endMs := curDay.UnixMilli()
kernel/levels_volume.go:349 	var sett float64
kernel/levels_volume.go:350 	var day string
kernel/levels_volume.go:351 	for _, b := range cb {
kernel/levels_volume.go:352 		if b.OpenTime < endMs && b.OpenTime >= curDay.AddDate(0, 0, -1).UnixMilli() {
kernel/levels_volume.go:353 			sett = b.Close
kernel/levels_volume.go:354 			day = time.UnixMilli(b.OpenTime).In(CTLocation()).Format("2006-01-02")

kernel/levels_volume.go:362
kernel/levels_volume.go:363 // MIDOLevels (B1) — the overnight range midpoint: (ONH+ONL)/2 for the CURRENT
kernel/levels_volume.go:364 // session-day's overnight window (17:00→08:30 CT). The auction's overnight
kernel/levels_volume.go:365 // balance point; price spends most of RTH revisiting it.
kernel/levels_volume.go:366 func MIDOLevels(bars []market.Kline, now time.Time) []DetectedLevel {
kernel/levels_volume.go:367 	cb := closedBars(bars, now)
kernel/levels_volume.go:368 	if len(cb) == 0 {
kernel/levels_volume.go:369 		return nil
kernel/levels_volume.go:370 	}
kernel/levels_volume.go:371 	ct := now.In(CTLocation())
kernel/levels_volume.go:372 	cutover := time.Date(ct.Year(), ct.Month(), ct.Day(), 8, 30, 0, 0, CTLocation())
kernel/levels_volume.go:373 	if ct.Hour() < 8 || (ct.Hour() == 8 && ct.Minute() < 30) {
kernel/levels_volume.go:374 		// Overnight window still open — use bars so far.
kernel/levels_volume.go:375 		cutover = now
kernel/levels_volume.go:376 	}
kernel/levels_volume.go:377 	sessStart := CMESessionDayStart(now)
kernel/levels_volume.go:378 	hi, lo := math.Inf(-1), math.Inf(1)
kernel/levels_volume.go:379 	for _, b := range cb {
kernel/levels_volume.go:380 		if b.OpenTime >= sessStart.UnixMilli() && b.OpenTime <= cutover.UnixMilli() {
kernel/levels_volume.go:381 			hi = math.Max(hi, b.High)
kernel/levels_volume.go:382 			lo = math.Min(lo, b.Low)
kernel/levels_volume.go:383 		}
kernel/levels_volume.go:384 	}
kernel/levels_volume.go:385 	if math.IsInf(hi, 0) || math.IsInf(lo, 0) {
kernel/levels_volume.go:386 		return nil
kernel/levels_volume.go:387 	}
kernel/levels_volume.go:388 	mid := (hi + lo) / 2
kernel/levels_volume.go:389 	return []DetectedLevel{lineLevel(KindMIDO, mid, "MID-O", sessStart.In(CTLocation()).Format("2006-01-02"), false)}
kernel/levels_volume.go:390 }
kernel/levels_volume.go:391
kernel/levels_volume.go:392 // VolumeLevels (B1) — the full volume family for one cycle (all detectors).
kernel/levels_volume.go:393 func VolumeLevels(bars []market.Kline, now time.Time) []DetectedLevel {
kernel/levels_volume.go:394 	var out []DetectedLevel
kernel/levels_volume.go:395 	out = append(out, SessionVWAPLevels(bars, now)...)
kernel/levels_volume.go:396 	out = append(out, EVWAPLevels(bars, now)...)
kernel/levels_volume.go:397 	out = append(out, PriorDayProfileLevels(bars, now)...)
kernel/levels_volume.go:398 	out = append(out, NakedPOCLevels(bars, now)...)
kernel/levels_volume.go:399 	out = append(out, PDVWAPLevels(bars, now)...)

kernel/levels_zones.go:32 // EqualHighsLows finds swing highs/lows that repeat within `tol` price units —
kernel/levels_zones.go:33 // resting liquidity (EQH above, EQL below). k=2 pivot lookback.
kernel/levels_zones.go:34 func EqualHighsLows(bars []market.Kline, tol float64, now time.Time) []DetectedLevel {
kernel/levels_zones.go:35 	cb := closedBars(bars, now)
kernel/levels_zones.go:36 	if tol <= 0 || len(cb) < 5 {
kernel/levels_zones.go:37 		return nil
kernel/levels_zones.go:38 	}
kernel/levels_zones.go:39 	const k = 2

kernel/levels_zones.go:41 	for i := k; i < len(cb)-k; i++ {
kernel/levels_zones.go:42 		if isStrictPivotHigh(cb, i, k) {
kernel/levels_zones.go:43 			hi = append(hi, cb[i].High)
kernel/levels_zones.go:44 		}
kernel/levels_zones.go:45 		if isStrictPivotLow(cb, i, k) {

kernel/levels_zones.go:47 		}

kernel/levels_zones.go:50 	var out []DetectedLevel
kernel/levels_zones.go:51 	out = append(out, clusterEqual(hi, tol, KindEQH, "EQH", true, origin)...)
kernel/levels_zones.go:52 	out = append(out, clusterEqual(lo, tol, KindEQL, "EQL", false, origin)...)
kernel/levels_zones.go:53 	return out
kernel/levels_zones.go:54 }

kernel/levels_zones.go:56 func isStrictPivotHigh(b []market.Kline, i, k int) bool {
kernel/levels_zones.go:57 	for j := i - k; j <= i+k; j++ {
kernel/levels_zones.go:58 		if j != i && b[j].High >= b[i].High {
kernel/levels_zones.go:59 			return false
kernel/levels_zones.go:60 		}
kernel/levels_zones.go:61 	}
kernel/levels_zones.go:62 	return true
kernel/levels_zones.go:63 }

kernel/levels_zones.go:68 			return false
kernel/levels_zones.go:69 		}

kernel/levels_zones.go:71 	return true

kernel/levels_zones.go:74 // clusterEqual groups pivot prices within tol; a group of ≥2 is an equal-highs/
kernel/levels_zones.go:75 // lows liquidity level (at the group max for highs, min for lows).
kernel/levels_zones.go:76 func clusterEqual(prices []float64, tol float64, kind LevelKind, label string, high bool, origin string) []DetectedLevel {
kernel/levels_zones.go:77 	if len(prices) < 2 {
kernel/levels_zones.go:78 		return nil
kernel/levels_zones.go:79 	}
kernel/levels_zones.go:80 	sort.Float64s(prices)
kernel/levels_zones.go:81 	var out []DetectedLevel
kernel/levels_zones.go:82 	i := 0
kernel/levels_zones.go:83 	for i < len(prices) {
kernel/levels_zones.go:84 		j := i + 1
kernel/levels_zones.go:85 		for j < len(prices) && prices[j]-prices[i] <= tol {
kernel/levels_zones.go:86 			j++
kernel/levels_zones.go:87 		}
kernel/levels_zones.go:88 		if j-i >= 2 {
kernel/levels_zones.go:89 			p := prices[i] // group min (lows)
kernel/levels_zones.go:90 			if high {
kernel/levels_zones.go:91 				p = prices[j-1] // group max (highs)
kernel/levels_zones.go:92 			}
kernel/levels_zones.go:93 			out = append(out, lineLevel(kind, p, label, origin, false))
kernel/levels_zones.go:94 		}
kernel/levels_zones.go:95 		i = j
kernel/levels_zones.go:96 	}
kernel/levels_zones.go:97 	return out
kernel/levels_zones.go:98 }
