kernel/levels_zones.go:100 // SupplyDemandZones finds a small-bodied base (≤6 candles, bodies ≤0.5×ATR)
kernel/levels_zones.go:101 // followed by a departure ≥1.5×ATR: up departure → demand (base below), down →
kernel/levels_zones.go:102 // supply (base above). Zone = the base's [low,high].
kernel/levels_zones.go:103 func SupplyDemandZones(bars []market.Kline, atr float64, now time.Time) []DetectedLevel {
kernel/levels_zones.go:104 	cb := closedBars(bars, now)
kernel/levels_zones.go:105 	if atr <= 0 || len(cb) < 3 {
kernel/levels_zones.go:106 		return nil
kernel/levels_zones.go:107 	}
kernel/levels_zones.go:108 	loc := chicago()
kernel/levels_zones.go:109 	smallBody := 0.5 * atr
kernel/levels_zones.go:110 	departure := 1.5 * atr
kernel/levels_zones.go:111
kernel/levels_zones.go:112 	var out []DetectedLevel
kernel/levels_zones.go:113 	i := 0
kernel/levels_zones.go:114 	for i < len(cb) {
kernel/levels_zones.go:115 		// grow a base of small-bodied candles (1..6)
kernel/levels_zones.go:116 		if math.Abs(cb[i].Close-cb[i].Open) > smallBody {
kernel/levels_zones.go:117 			i++
kernel/levels_zones.go:118 			continue
kernel/levels_zones.go:119 		}
kernel/levels_zones.go:120 		baseLo, baseHi := cb[i].Low, cb[i].High
kernel/levels_zones.go:121 		j := i
kernel/levels_zones.go:122 		for j+1 < len(cb) && j-i < 5 && math.Abs(cb[j+1].Close-cb[j+1].Open) <= smallBody {
kernel/levels_zones.go:123 			j++
kernel/levels_zones.go:124 			baseLo = math.Min(baseLo, cb[j].Low)
kernel/levels_zones.go:125 			baseHi = math.Max(baseHi, cb[j].High)
kernel/levels_zones.go:126 		}
kernel/levels_zones.go:127 		// departure candle right after the base?
kernel/levels_zones.go:128 		if j+1 < len(cb) {
kernel/levels_zones.go:129 			d := cb[j+1]
kernel/levels_zones.go:130 			move := d.Close - d.Open
kernel/levels_zones.go:131 			origin := time.UnixMilli(d.OpenTime).In(loc).Format("2006-01-02")
kernel/levels_zones.go:132 			// v3 (2026-08-24) — pattern classification: the leg BEFORE the base
kernel/levels_zones.go:133 			// vs the departure. Opposite signs → reversal (RBD/DBR, strongest);
kernel/levels_zones.go:134 			// same sign → continuation (RBR/DBD, weaker).
kernel/levels_zones.go:135 			pattern := ""
kernel/levels_zones.go:136 			if i > 0 {
kernel/levels_zones.go:137 				leg := cb[i-1].Close - cb[i-1].Open
kernel/levels_zones.go:138 				if (leg >= 0) != (move >= 0) {
kernel/levels_zones.go:139 					pattern = "reversal"
kernel/levels_zones.go:140 				} else {
kernel/levels_zones.go:141 					pattern = "continuation"
kernel/levels_zones.go:142 				}
kernel/levels_zones.go:143 			}
kernel/levels_zones.go:144 			if move >= departure {
kernel/levels_zones.go:145 				zl := zoneLevel(KindDemand, baseLo, baseHi, "Demand", origin)
kernel/levels_zones.go:146 				zl.ZonePattern = pattern
kernel/levels_zones.go:147 				zl.FormedAtMs = d.OpenTime // W6: birth = departure bar
kernel/levels_zones.go:148 				out = append(out, zl)
kernel/levels_zones.go:149 			} else if -move >= departure {
kernel/levels_zones.go:150 				zl := zoneLevel(KindSupply, baseLo, baseHi, "Supply", origin)
kernel/levels_zones.go:151 				zl.ZonePattern = pattern
kernel/levels_zones.go:152 				zl.FormedAtMs = d.OpenTime // W6: birth = departure bar
kernel/levels_zones.go:153 				out = append(out, zl)
kernel/levels_zones.go:154 			}
kernel/levels_zones.go:155 		}
kernel/levels_zones.go:156 		i = j + 1
kernel/levels_zones.go:157 	}
kernel/levels_zones.go:158 	return out
kernel/levels_zones.go:159 }
kernel/levels_zones.go:160
kernel/levels_zones.go:161 // FVGNoiseFloorPoints (grading audit §4.4, 2026-08-25) — the minimum FVG gap
kernel/levels_zones.go:162 // width in POINTS. Research: requiring a 1×ATR gap has no external support and
kernel/levels_zones.go:163 // kills the published 20–80 pt NQ sweet spot; the floor is instead
kernel/levels_zones.go:164 // max(2×tick, 2.0 pts) — any-gap detection with a noise floor, plus the
kernel/levels_zones.go:165 // size weighting zoneSizeMult already applies at scoring.
kernel/levels_zones.go:166 const FVGNoiseFloorPoints = 2.0
kernel/levels_zones.go:167
kernel/levels_zones.go:168 // fvgMinGapPoints resolves the FVG detection gap floor for a symbol:
kernel/levels_zones.go:169 // max(2 ticks, the noise floor). Ticks below the floor (e.g. MNQ 0.25 → 0.50)
kernel/levels_zones.go:170 // never raise it.
kernel/levels_zones.go:171 func fvgMinGapPoints(symbol string) float64 {
kernel/levels_zones.go:172 	tick := market.FuturesTickSize(symbol)
kernel/levels_zones.go:173 	if tick <= 0 {
kernel/levels_zones.go:174 		tick = 0.25
kernel/levels_zones.go:175 	}
kernel/levels_zones.go:176 	floor := FVGNoiseFloorPoints
kernel/levels_zones.go:177 	if 2*tick > floor {
kernel/levels_zones.go:178 		floor = 2 * tick
kernel/levels_zones.go:179 	}
kernel/levels_zones.go:180 	return floor
kernel/levels_zones.go:181 }
kernel/levels_zones.go:182
kernel/levels_zones.go:183 // FairValueGaps finds 3-candle imbalances: bullish when bar[i-2].High < bar[i].Low
kernel/levels_zones.go:184 // (gap between them), bearish when bar[i-2].Low > bar[i].High. UNFILLED gaps are
kernel/levels_zones.go:185 // emitted as KindFVG. W6 (2026-08-25): a gap whose far edge is later VIOLATED BY
kernel/levels_zones.go:186 // fvgWindowContiguous guards the 3-candle FVG window against session breaks
kernel/levels_zones.go:187 // (register A6, mega-research 2026-08-26): candles separated by more than 3×
kernel/levels_zones.go:188 // the bar interval straddle the 16:00-17:00 CT halt, a weekend, or a data gap
kernel/levels_zones.go:189 // — never a real imbalance. A DST shift keeps 1m bars 60s apart → passes.
kernel/levels_zones.go:190 func fvgWindowContiguous(cb []market.Kline, i int) bool {
kernel/levels_zones.go:191 	if i < 2 || i >= len(cb) {
kernel/levels_zones.go:192 		return false
kernel/levels_zones.go:193 	}
kernel/levels_zones.go:194 	// The series' own interval: the MINIMUM positive delta among the last 10
kernel/levels_zones.go:195 	// bars (the TF interval; duplicate timestamps don't count).
kernel/levels_zones.go:196 	iv := int64(0)
kernel/levels_zones.go:197 	for j := len(cb) - 1; j >= 1 && j >= len(cb)-10; j-- {
kernel/levels_zones.go:198 		d := cb[j].OpenTime - cb[j-1].OpenTime
kernel/levels_zones.go:199 		if d > 0 && (iv == 0 || d < iv) {
kernel/levels_zones.go:200 			iv = d
kernel/levels_zones.go:201 		}
kernel/levels_zones.go:202 	}
kernel/levels_zones.go:203 	if iv <= 0 {
kernel/levels_zones.go:204 		return false
kernel/levels_zones.go:205 	}
kernel/levels_zones.go:206 	return cb[i].OpenTime-cb[i-2].OpenTime <= 3*iv
kernel/levels_zones.go:207 }
kernel/levels_zones.go:208
kernel/levels_zones.go:209 // A CLOSE is no longer dropped — it is emitted as KindIFVG with INVERTED
kernel/levels_zones.go:210 // polarity (filled bullish FVG → bearish iFVG resistance; filled bearish FVG →
kernel/levels_zones.go:211 // bullish iFVG support), keeping the original bounds. Research-grounded: a
kernel/levels_zones.go:212 // stay-through close beyond the gap edge flips the imbalance's role.
kernel/levels_zones.go:213 func FairValueGaps(bars []market.Kline, minGap float64, now time.Time) []DetectedLevel {
kernel/levels_zones.go:214 	cb := closedBars(bars, now)
kernel/levels_zones.go:215 	if len(cb) < 3 {
kernel/levels_zones.go:216 		return nil
kernel/levels_zones.go:217 	}
kernel/levels_zones.go:218 	loc := chicago()
kernel/levels_zones.go:219 	var out []DetectedLevel
kernel/levels_zones.go:220 	for i := 2; i < len(cb); i++ {
kernel/levels_zones.go:221 		// A6 — session-break guard: halt/weekend-straddling triples are noise.
kernel/levels_zones.go:222 		if !fvgWindowContiguous(cb, i) {
kernel/levels_zones.go:223 			continue
kernel/levels_zones.go:224 		}
kernel/levels_zones.go:225 		a, c := cb[i-2], cb[i]
kernel/levels_zones.go:226 		var gLo, gHi float64
kernel/levels_zones.go:227 		var bullish bool
kernel/levels_zones.go:228 		switch {
kernel/levels_zones.go:229 		case a.High < c.Low && c.Low-a.High >= minGap:
kernel/levels_zones.go:230 			gLo, gHi = a.High, c.Low
kernel/levels_zones.go:231 			bullish = true
kernel/levels_zones.go:232 		case a.Low > c.High && a.Low-c.High >= minGap:
kernel/levels_zones.go:233 			gLo, gHi = c.High, a.Low
kernel/levels_zones.go:234 			bullish = false
kernel/levels_zones.go:235 		default:
kernel/levels_zones.go:236 			continue
kernel/levels_zones.go:237 		}
kernel/levels_zones.go:238 		origin := time.UnixMilli(c.OpenTime).In(loc).Format("2006-01-02")
kernel/levels_zones.go:239 		// W6 inversion: a later CLOSE beyond the far edge (below a bullish
kernel/levels_zones.go:240 		// gap's low, above a bearish gap's high) flips the imbalance.
kernel/levels_zones.go:241 		inverted := false
kernel/levels_zones.go:242 		for j := i + 1; j < len(cb); j++ {
kernel/levels_zones.go:243 			if bullish && cb[j].Close < gLo {
kernel/levels_zones.go:244 				inverted = true
kernel/levels_zones.go:245 				break
kernel/levels_zones.go:246 			}
kernel/levels_zones.go:247 			if !bullish && cb[j].Close > gHi {
kernel/levels_zones.go:248 				inverted = true
kernel/levels_zones.go:249 				break
kernel/levels_zones.go:250 			}
kernel/levels_zones.go:251 		}
kernel/levels_zones.go:252 		if inverted {
kernel/levels_zones.go:253 			lvl := zoneLevel(KindIFVG, gLo, gHi, "iFVG", origin)
kernel/levels_zones.go:254 			if bullish {
kernel/levels_zones.go:255 				lvl.Label = "iFVG(bear)" // flipped to resistance
kernel/levels_zones.go:256 			} else {
kernel/levels_zones.go:257 				lvl.Label = "iFVG(bull)" // flipped to support
kernel/levels_zones.go:258 			}
kernel/levels_zones.go:259 			lvl.Info = "filled→inverted"
kernel/levels_zones.go:260 			lvl.FormedAtMs = c.OpenTime
kernel/levels_zones.go:261 			out = append(out, lvl)
kernel/levels_zones.go:262 			continue
kernel/levels_zones.go:263 		}
kernel/levels_zones.go:264 		lvl := zoneLevel(KindFVG, gLo, gHi, "FVG", origin)
kernel/levels_zones.go:265 		lvl.FormedAtMs = c.OpenTime
kernel/levels_zones.go:266 		out = append(out, lvl)
kernel/levels_zones.go:267 	}
kernel/levels_zones.go:268 	return out
kernel/levels_zones.go:269 }
kernel/levels_zones.go:270

kernel/levels_zones.go:273 // opposing candle HOURS back (a stale, meaningless OB); research supports a

kernel/levels_zones.go:283 			return n

kernel/levels_zones.go:292 // OrderBlocks finds the last opposing candle within a bounded lookback before a
kernel/levels_zones.go:293 // displacement ≥1.5×ATR: a big up move → the last DOWN candle before it (bullish
kernel/levels_zones.go:294 // OB); a big down move → the last UP candle before it (bearish OB). Zone = that
kernel/levels_zones.go:295 // candle's [low,high].
kernel/levels_zones.go:296 func OrderBlocks(bars []market.Kline, atr float64, now time.Time) []DetectedLevel {

kernel/levels_zones.go:303 	lookback := obLookbackBars() // bounded scan (§4.5) — never pairs across hours

kernel/levels_zones.go:305 	for i := 1; i < len(cb); i++ {
kernel/levels_zones.go:306 		move := cb[i].Close - cb[i].Open
kernel/levels_zones.go:307 		origin := time.UnixMilli(cb[i].OpenTime).In(loc).Format("2006-01-02")
kernel/levels_zones.go:308 		if move >= displacement {

kernel/levels_zones.go:314 					out = append(out, zl)
kernel/levels_zones.go:315 					break
kernel/levels_zones.go:316 				}
kernel/levels_zones.go:317 			}
kernel/levels_zones.go:318 		} else if -move >= displacement {
kernel/levels_zones.go:319 			for j := i - 1; j >= 0 && i-j <= lookback; j-- {
kernel/levels_zones.go:320 				if cb[j].Close > cb[j].Open {
kernel/levels_zones.go:321 					zl := zoneLevel(KindOB, cb[j].Low, cb[j].High, "OB(bear)", origin)

kernel/levels_swing.go:20 //

kernel/levels_swing.go:35 // SwingPointLevels runs the structure swing detector on the 5m and 15m series
kernel/levels_swing.go:36 // aggregated from the passed bars (typically the 1m slice) and emits the recent
kernel/levels_swing.go:37 // swing highs/lows as DetectedLevel lines. Empty input → nothing.
kernel/levels_swing.go:38 func SwingPointLevels(bars []market.Kline, now time.Time) []DetectedLevel {
kernel/levels_swing.go:39 	if len(bars) == 0 {
kernel/levels_swing.go:40 		return nil
kernel/levels_swing.go:41 	}
kernel/levels_swing.go:42 	var out []DetectedLevel
kernel/levels_swing.go:43 	for _, tfMin := range []int{5, 15} {
kernel/levels_swing.go:44 		agg := aggregateBars(bars, tfMin)
kernel/levels_swing.go:45 		if len(agg) < 2*structureSwingK()+1 {
kernel/levels_swing.go:46 			continue
kernel/levels_swing.go:47 		}
kernel/levels_swing.go:48 		out = append(out, swingPointsFor(agg, tfMin, now)...)
kernel/levels_swing.go:49 	}
kernel/levels_swing.go:50 	return out
kernel/levels_swing.go:51 }
kernel/levels_swing.go:52
kernel/levels_swing.go:53 // swingPointsFor is the per-TF swing emission: fractal extremes with the
kernel/levels_swing.go:54 // structure engine's significance filter, newest-first within the lookback.
kernel/levels_swing.go:55 func swingPointsFor(agg []market.Kline, tfMin int, now time.Time) []DetectedLevel {
kernel/levels_swing.go:56 	iv := int64(tfMin) * 60_000
kernel/levels_swing.go:57 	closed := make([]market.Kline, 0, len(agg))
kernel/levels_swing.go:58 	for _, b := range agg {
kernel/levels_swing.go:59 		if b.CloseTime >= now.UnixMilli() {
kernel/levels_swing.go:60 			continue
kernel/levels_swing.go:61 		}
kernel/levels_swing.go:62 		closed = append(closed, b)
kernel/levels_swing.go:63 	}
kernel/levels_swing.go:64 	n := len(closed)
kernel/levels_swing.go:65 	if n < 2*structureSwingK()+1 {
kernel/levels_swing.go:66 		return nil
kernel/levels_swing.go:67 	}
kernel/levels_swing.go:68 	highs := make([]float64, n)
kernel/levels_swing.go:69 	lows := make([]float64, n)
kernel/levels_swing.go:70 	closes := make([]float64, n)
kernel/levels_swing.go:71 	for i, b := range closed {
kernel/levels_swing.go:72 		highs[i] = b.High
kernel/levels_swing.go:73 		lows[i] = b.Low
kernel/levels_swing.go:74 		closes[i] = b.Close
kernel/levels_swing.go:75 	}
kernel/levels_swing.go:76 	atr := simpleATR14(highs, lows, closes)
kernel/levels_swing.go:77 	if atr <= 0 {
kernel/levels_swing.go:78 		return nil
kernel/levels_swing.go:79 	}
kernel/levels_swing.go:80 	k := structureSwingK()
kernel/levels_swing.go:81 	type pt struct {
kernel/levels_swing.go:82 		high   bool
kernel/levels_swing.go:83 		price  float64
kernel/levels_swing.go:84 		timeMs int64
kernel/levels_swing.go:85 	}
kernel/levels_swing.go:86 	swings := make([]pt, 0, 16)
kernel/levels_swing.go:87 	for i := k; i < n-k; i++ {
kernel/levels_swing.go:88 		isHigh, isLow := true, true
kernel/levels_swing.go:89 		for j := i - k; j <= i+k; j++ {
kernel/levels_swing.go:90 			if j == i {
kernel/levels_swing.go:91 				continue
kernel/levels_swing.go:92 			}
kernel/levels_swing.go:93 			if highs[j] >= highs[i] {
kernel/levels_swing.go:94 				isHigh = false
kernel/levels_swing.go:95 			}
kernel/levels_swing.go:96 			if lows[j] <= lows[i] {
kernel/levels_swing.go:97 				isLow = false
kernel/levels_swing.go:98 			}
kernel/levels_swing.go:99 		}
kernel/levels_swing.go:100 		if !isHigh && !isLow {
kernel/levels_swing.go:101 			continue
kernel/levels_swing.go:102 		}
kernel/levels_swing.go:103 		price, hi := highs[i], true
kernel/levels_swing.go:104 		if isLow {
kernel/levels_swing.go:105 			price, hi = lows[i], false
kernel/levels_swing.go:106 		}
kernel/levels_swing.go:107 		t := closed[i].OpenTime + iv // bar CLOSE instant (repo convention)
kernel/levels_swing.go:108 		if len(swings) > 0 && swings[len(swings)-1].high == hi {
kernel/levels_swing.go:109 			if (hi && price <= swings[len(swings)-1].price) || (!hi && price >= swings[len(swings)-1].price) {
kernel/levels_swing.go:110 				continue
kernel/levels_swing.go:111 			}
kernel/levels_swing.go:112 			swings[len(swings)-1] = pt{price: price, timeMs: t, high: hi}
kernel/levels_swing.go:113 			continue
kernel/levels_swing.go:114 		}
kernel/levels_swing.go:115 		if len(swings) > 0 {
kernel/levels_swing.go:116 			move := price - swings[len(swings)-1].price
kernel/levels_swing.go:117 			if move < 0 {
kernel/levels_swing.go:118 				move = -move
kernel/levels_swing.go:119 			}
kernel/levels_swing.go:120 			if move < structureMinSwingATR()*atr {
kernel/levels_swing.go:121 				continue
kernel/levels_swing.go:122 			}
kernel/levels_swing.go:123 		}
kernel/levels_swing.go:124 		swings = append(swings, pt{price: price, timeMs: t, high: hi})
kernel/levels_swing.go:125 	}
kernel/levels_swing.go:126 	if len(swings) == 0 {
kernel/levels_swing.go:127 		return nil
kernel/levels_swing.go:128 	}
kernel/levels_swing.go:129 	// Recent only: within the lookback, newest first.
kernel/levels_swing.go:130 	cutoff := now.UnixMilli() - int64(lookbackFor(tfMin))*iv
kernel/levels_swing.go:131 	day := CMESessionDayKey(now)
kernel/levels_swing.go:132 	var out []DetectedLevel
kernel/levels_swing.go:133 	addedH, addedL := 0, 0
kernel/levels_swing.go:134 	for i := len(swings) - 1; i >= 0 && (addedH < SwingPointsPerTFSide || addedL < SwingPointsPerTFSide); i-- {
kernel/levels_swing.go:135 		s := swings[i]
kernel/levels_swing.go:136 		if s.timeMs < cutoff {
kernel/levels_swing.go:137 			continue
kernel/levels_swing.go:138 		}
kernel/levels_swing.go:139 		kind := KindSWGH
kernel/levels_swing.go:140 		label := "SWG-H·"
kernel/levels_swing.go:141 		if !s.high {
kernel/levels_swing.go:142 			kind = KindSWGL
kernel/levels_swing.go:143 			label = "SWG-L·"
kernel/levels_swing.go:144 		}
kernel/levels_swing.go:145 		if s.high {
kernel/levels_swing.go:146 			if addedH >= SwingPointsPerTFSide {
kernel/levels_swing.go:147 				continue
kernel/levels_swing.go:148 			}
kernel/levels_swing.go:149 			addedH++
kernel/levels_swing.go:150 		} else {
kernel/levels_swing.go:151 			if addedL >= SwingPointsPerTFSide {
kernel/levels_swing.go:152 				continue
kernel/levels_swing.go:153 			}
kernel/levels_swing.go:154 			addedL++
kernel/levels_swing.go:155 		}
kernel/levels_swing.go:156 		out = append(out, DetectedLevel{
kernel/levels_swing.go:157 			Kind:       kind,
kernel/levels_swing.go:158 			Price:      s.price,
kernel/levels_swing.go:159 			Lo:         s.price,
kernel/levels_swing.go:160 			Hi:         s.price,
kernel/levels_swing.go:161 			Label:      label + tfName(tfMin),
kernel/levels_swing.go:162 			OriginDate: day,
kernel/levels_swing.go:163 			TF:         tfName(tfMin),
kernel/levels_swing.go:164 		})
kernel/levels_swing.go:165 	}
kernel/levels_swing.go:166 	return out
kernel/levels_swing.go:167 }
kernel/levels_swing.go:168
kernel/levels_swing.go:169 func lookbackFor(tfMin int) int {
kernel/levels_swing.go:170 	if tfMin >= 15 {
kernel/levels_swing.go:171 		return Swing15mLookbackBars
kernel/levels_swing.go:172 	}
kernel/levels_swing.go:173 	return Swing5mLookbackBars
kernel/levels_swing.go:174 }
kernel/levels_swing.go:175
kernel/levels_swing.go:176 func tfName(tfMin int) string {
kernel/levels_swing.go:177 	if tfMin >= 15 {
kernel/levels_swing.go:178 		return "15m"
kernel/levels_swing.go:179 	}
kernel/levels_swing.go:180 	return "5m"

kernel/levels_swing.go:182
kernel/levels_swing.go:183 // aggregateBars rolls the passed series (assumed 1m, ascending) into tfMin bars
kernel/levels_swing.go:184 // keyed by OpenTime = start of the tfMin bucket. The closed-bar convention is
kernel/levels_swing.go:185 // preserved downstream (callers filter by CloseTime).
kernel/levels_swing.go:186 func aggregateBars(bars []market.Kline, tfMin int) []market.Kline {
kernel/levels_swing.go:187 	bucket := int64(tfMin) * 60_000
kernel/levels_swing.go:188 	type agg struct{ o, h, l, c, v float64 }
kernel/levels_swing.go:189 	m := map[int64]*agg{}
kernel/levels_swing.go:190 	var order []int64
kernel/levels_swing.go:191 	for _, b := range bars {
kernel/levels_swing.go:192 		key := b.OpenTime - b.OpenTime%bucket
kernel/levels_swing.go:193 		a, ok := m[key]
kernel/levels_swing.go:194 		if !ok {
kernel/levels_swing.go:195 			a = &agg{o: b.Open, h: b.High, l: b.Low, c: b.Close}
kernel/levels_swing.go:196 			m[key] = a
kernel/levels_swing.go:197 			order = append(order, key)
kernel/levels_swing.go:198 			continue
kernel/levels_swing.go:199 		}
kernel/levels_swing.go:200 		if b.High > a.h {
kernel/levels_swing.go:201 			a.h = b.High
kernel/levels_swing.go:202 		}
kernel/levels_swing.go:203 		if b.Low < a.l {
kernel/levels_swing.go:204 			a.l = b.Low
kernel/levels_swing.go:205 		}
kernel/levels_swing.go:206 		a.c = b.Close
kernel/levels_swing.go:207 		a.v += b.Volume
kernel/levels_swing.go:208 	}
kernel/levels_swing.go:209 	out := make([]market.Kline, 0, len(order))
kernel/levels_swing.go:210 	for _, key := range order {
kernel/levels_swing.go:211 		a := m[key]
kernel/levels_swing.go:212 		out = append(out, market.Kline{
kernel/levels_swing.go:213 			OpenTime: key,
kernel/levels_swing.go:214 			Open:     a.o, High: a.h, Low: a.l, Close: a.c, Volume: a.v,
kernel/levels_swing.go:215 			// CloseTime = OpenTime + interval (repo convention — the last
kernel/levels_swing.go:216 			// instant INSIDE the bar).
kernel/levels_swing.go:217 			CloseTime: key + bucket,
kernel/levels_swing.go:218 		})
kernel/levels_swing.go:219 	}
kernel/levels_swing.go:220 	return out
kernel/levels_swing.go:221 }

kernel/levels_role.go:164 func ComputeBiasContext(bars []market.Kline, scored []ScoredLevel, now time.Time) BiasContext {
kernel/levels_role.go:165 	bc := BiasContext{}
kernel/levels_role.go:166 	cb := closedBars(bars, now)

kernel/levels_role.go:169 	}

kernel/levels_role.go:172 	}

kernel/levels_role.go:175 		case KindPDC:
kernel/levels_role.go:176 			if bc.PDC <= 0 {

kernel/levels_role.go:178 			}

kernel/levels_role.go:182 			}

kernel/levels_role.go:186 			}
kernel/levels_role.go:187 		}
kernel/levels_role.go:188 	}
kernel/levels_role.go:189 	prof := profileLevels(cb, "", "")
kernel/levels_role.go:190 	if len(prof) >= 3 {

kernel/levels_role.go:192 		if bc.VAH < bc.VAL {

kernel/levels_role.go:194 		}
kernel/levels_role.go:195 	}

kernel/levels_role.go:198 	}
kernel/levels_role.go:199 	// Nearest magnet / liquidity from the scored pool by role, nearest-first.
kernel/levels_role.go:200 	type cand struct {

kernel/levels_role.go:203 	}

kernel/levels_role.go:212 		}
kernel/levels_role.go:213 	}

kernel/levels_role.go:217 		}

kernel/levels_role.go:219 		return fmt.Sprintf("%s (%+.1f)", cs[0].label, cs[0].dist)
kernel/levels_role.go:220 	}

kernel/levels_role.go:222 	bc.NearestLiquidity = nearest(liquid)

kernel/levels_role.go:389 // RoleMismatches (addendum 1, validator WARN — never a fail) — scans the plan's
kernel/levels_role.go:390 // scenarios against the ROLE of the plan level each confirm{} ref_price cites:
kernel/levels_role.go:391 // a magnet_meanrevert level used as a breakout/acceptance trigger, or a
kernel/levels_role.go:392 // liquidity_break level faded with a plain reject, are role-vs-scenario
kernel/levels_role.go:393 // mismatches. Returns human-readable lines for the journal.
kernel/levels_role.go:394 func RoleMismatches(doc *PlanDoc) []string {
kernel/levels_role.go:395 	if doc == nil || len(doc.Scenarios) == 0 || len(doc.Levels) == 0 {
kernel/levels_role.go:396 		return nil
kernel/levels_role.go:397 	}
kernel/levels_role.go:398 	roleAt := func(price float64) (LevelRole, string) {
kernel/levels_role.go:399 		best := -1
kernel/levels_role.go:400 		bd := math.MaxFloat64
kernel/levels_role.go:401 		for i, l := range doc.Levels {
kernel/levels_role.go:402 			d := math.Abs(l.Price - price)
kernel/levels_role.go:403 			if d < bd {
kernel/levels_role.go:404 				bd, best = d, i
kernel/levels_role.go:405 			}
kernel/levels_role.go:406 		}
kernel/levels_role.go:407 		if best < 0 || bd > 3.0 {
kernel/levels_role.go:408 			return "", ""
kernel/levels_role.go:409 		}
kernel/levels_role.go:410 		return RoleForLabel(doc.Levels[best].Label), doc.Levels[best].Label
kernel/levels_role.go:411 	}
kernel/levels_role.go:412 	var out []string
kernel/levels_role.go:413 	for _, sc := range doc.Scenarios {
kernel/levels_role.go:414 		// FVG ENTRY MODEL (2026-08-26) — an fvg_entry anchored to a
kernel/levels_role.go:415 		// liquidity_break origin gets the SAME sweep-reclaim caution WARN as
kernel/levels_role.go:416 		// other plays (no exemption): the origin is a liquidity pool, the gap
kernel/levels_role.go:417 		// entry is a fade of that pool.
kernel/levels_role.go:418 		if strings.EqualFold(strings.TrimSpace(sc.Condition), "fvg_entry") && sc.Fvg != nil {
kernel/levels_role.go:419 			if RoleForLabel(sc.Fvg.OriginLevel) == RoleLiquidityBreak {
kernel/levels_role.go:420 				out = append(out, fmt.Sprintf("S%s fvg_entry anchored on %s (liquidity_break) — sweep-reclaim caution: enter only on a confirmed reclaim close, never the wick alone",
kernel/levels_role.go:421 					sc.ID, sc.Fvg.OriginLevel))
kernel/levels_role.go:422 			}
kernel/levels_role.go:423 			continue
kernel/levels_role.go:424 		}
kernel/levels_role.go:425 		if sc.Confirm == nil || sc.Confirm.RefPrice <= 0 {
kernel/levels_role.go:426 			continue
kernel/levels_role.go:427 		}
kernel/levels_role.go:428 		role, label := roleAt(sc.Confirm.RefPrice)
kernel/levels_role.go:429 		if role == "" {
kernel/levels_role.go:430 			continue
kernel/levels_role.go:431 		}
kernel/levels_role.go:432 		cond := strings.ToLower(strings.TrimSpace(sc.Condition))
kernel/levels_role.go:433 		switch {
kernel/levels_role.go:434 		case role == RoleMagnetMeanRevert && (cond == "breakout_retest" || cond == "acceptance"):
kernel/levels_role.go:435 			out = append(out, fmt.Sprintf("S%s cites %s (%s) but uses condition %q — a magnet is a reversion reference, not a breakout trigger",
kernel/levels_role.go:436 				sc.ID, label, role, sc.Condition))
kernel/levels_role.go:437 		case role == RoleLiquidityBreak && cond == "reject":
kernel/levels_role.go:438 			out = append(out, fmt.Sprintf("S%s fades %s (%s) with a plain reject — liquidity fades need sweep_reclaim (wick through + close back)",
kernel/levels_role.go:439 				sc.ID, label, role))
kernel/levels_role.go:440 		case role == RolePivot && (cond == "breakout_retest" || cond == "acceptance"):
kernel/levels_role.go:441 			out = append(out, fmt.Sprintf("S%s cites %s (%s) with condition %q — pivots are bias references; breakout triggers belong to liquidity levels",
kernel/levels_role.go:442 				sc.ID, label, role, sc.Condition))
kernel/levels_role.go:443 		case role == RoleTargetOnly:
kernel/levels_role.go:444 			out = append(out, fmt.Sprintf("S%s uses %s (%s) as its confirm reference — target_only levels are never entry triggers",
kernel/levels_role.go:445 				sc.ID, label, role))
kernel/levels_role.go:446 		}
kernel/levels_role.go:447 	}
kernel/levels_role.go:448 	return out
kernel/levels_role.go:449 }

kernel/detector_recorder.go:35 func BuildCandidatePool(all []DetectedLevel, seated []ScoredLevel, price, dATR, proximityK float64, maxLevels int) []CandidateRecord {

kernel/detector_recorder.go:37 		return nil
kernel/detector_recorder.go:38 	}
kernel/detector_recorder.go:39 	if proximityK <= 0 {
kernel/detector_recorder.go:40 		proximityK = ActivationWindowK
kernel/detector_recorder.go:41 	}
kernel/detector_recorder.go:42 	if maxLevels <= 0 {
kernel/detector_recorder.go:43 		maxLevels = DefaultMaxLevels
kernel/detector_recorder.go:44 	}
kernel/detector_recorder.go:45 	band := proximityK * dATR
kernel/detector_recorder.go:46 	rankOf := map[string]int{}
kernel/detector_recorder.go:47 	scoreOf := map[string]float64{}
kernel/detector_recorder.go:48 	gradeOf := map[string]string{}
kernel/detector_recorder.go:49 	key := func(p float64, kind, label string) string { return fmt.Sprintf("%.4f|%s|%s", p, kind, label) }
kernel/detector_recorder.go:50 	for i, s := range seated {
kernel/detector_recorder.go:51 		k := key(s.Price, string(s.Kind), s.Label)
kernel/detector_recorder.go:52 		rankOf[k] = i + 1
kernel/detector_recorder.go:53 		scoreOf[k] = s.Score
kernel/detector_recorder.go:54 		gradeOf[k] = s.Grade
kernel/detector_recorder.go:55 	}
kernel/detector_recorder.go:56 	// The seating threshold, observed rather than assumed: the weakest score
kernel/detector_recorder.go:57 	// that made it in. With nothing seated there is no threshold to report.
kernel/detector_recorder.go:58 	threshold := 0.0
kernel/detector_recorder.go:59 	if len(seated) > 0 {
kernel/detector_recorder.go:60 		threshold = seated[0].Score
kernel/detector_recorder.go:61 		for _, s := range seated {
kernel/detector_recorder.go:62 			if s.Score < threshold {
kernel/detector_recorder.go:63 				threshold = s.Score
kernel/detector_recorder.go:64 			}
kernel/detector_recorder.go:65 		}
kernel/detector_recorder.go:66 	}
kernel/detector_recorder.go:67 	out := make([]CandidateRecord, 0, len(all))
kernel/detector_recorder.go:68 	for _, l := range all {
kernel/detector_recorder.go:69 		k := key(l.Price, string(l.Kind), l.Label)
kernel/detector_recorder.go:70 		rec := CandidateRecord{
kernel/detector_recorder.go:71 			Price: l.Price, Kind: string(l.Kind), Label: l.Label,
kernel/detector_recorder.go:72 			Threshold: threshold, Components: "{}",
kernel/detector_recorder.go:73 		}
kernel/detector_recorder.go:74 		if r, ok := rankOf[k]; ok {
kernel/detector_recorder.go:75 			rec.Rank, rec.Seated, rec.Score, rec.Grade = r, true, scoreOf[k], gradeOf[k]
kernel/detector_recorder.go:76 			out = append(out, rec)
kernel/detector_recorder.go:77 			continue
kernel/detector_recorder.go:78 		}
kernel/detector_recorder.go:79 		// Cut. The reason is derived from the constructor's own predicates, in
kernel/detector_recorder.go:80 		// the order it applies them, so the record explains the decision rather
kernel/detector_recorder.go:81 		// than guessing at it.
kernel/detector_recorder.go:82 		switch {
kernel/detector_recorder.go:83 		case dATR > 0 && math.Abs(l.Price-price) > band:
kernel/detector_recorder.go:84 			rec.CutReason = fmt.Sprintf("proximity: %.2f pts from price > %.1f×dATR (%.2f)", math.Abs(l.Price-price), proximityK, band)
kernel/detector_recorder.go:85 		case len(seated) >= maxLevels:
kernel/detector_recorder.go:86 			rec.CutReason = fmt.Sprintf("max_levels: %d seated, cap %d", len(seated), maxLevels)
kernel/detector_recorder.go:87 		default:
kernel/detector_recorder.go:88 			rec.CutReason = "not seated (cluster collapse or grade floor)"
kernel/detector_recorder.go:89 		}
kernel/detector_recorder.go:90 		out = append(out, rec)
kernel/detector_recorder.go:91 	}
kernel/detector_recorder.go:92 	return out
kernel/detector_recorder.go:93 }
kernel/detector_recorder.go:94
kernel/detector_recorder.go:95 // NewEpisodesSince filters a detector run to the episodes not yet recorded, so
kernel/detector_recorder.go:96 // a per-read hook is safe to call repeatedly: only episodes opening AFTER the
kernel/detector_recorder.go:97 // last stored one are new. Restart-safe, because the watermark comes from the
kernel/detector_recorder.go:98 // store rather than memory.
kernel/detector_recorder.go:99 func NewEpisodesSince(eps []TouchOutcome, lastOpenedMs int64) []TouchOutcome {
kernel/detector_recorder.go:100 	out := make([]TouchOutcome, 0, len(eps))
kernel/detector_recorder.go:101 	for _, e := range eps {
kernel/detector_recorder.go:102 		if e.OpenedAtMs > lastOpenedMs && e.ClosedAtMs != 0 {
kernel/detector_recorder.go:103 			out = append(out, e)
kernel/detector_recorder.go:104 		}
kernel/detector_recorder.go:105 	}
kernel/detector_recorder.go:106 	return out
kernel/detector_recorder.go:107 }

kernel/detector_d1prime.go:13 // ── D1′ — THE CALIBRATED TOUCH DETECTOR (2026-09-03) ─────────────────────────
kernel/detector_d1prime.go:14 //
kernel/detector_d1prime.go:15 // Replaces two instruments that could not be wrong in the direction that
kernel/detector_d1prime.go:16 // mattered:
kernel/detector_d1prime.go:17 //   · touch_telemetry.go called a touch a "rejection" when the close was still
kernel/detector_d1prime.go:18 //     on its starting side — ≈0.69 on IID noise BY CONSTRUCTION.
kernel/detector_d1prime.go:19 //   · level_stats_calc.go:70 counted ANY ≥reactPts move away from the level as
kernel/detector_d1prime.go:20 //     a reaction, so a blast-through scored as a reaction.
kernel/detector_d1prime.go:21 // Every reaction rate ever published from them (84%, 70.3%, 75.1%) is an
kernel/detector_d1prime.go:22 // artifact of the predicate, not a property of the tape.
kernel/detector_d1prime.go:23 //
kernel/detector_d1prime.go:24 // D1′ is a gambler's-ruin design: barriers are anchored to the LEVEL at
kernel/detector_d1prime.go:25 // L ± k·Δ, so they are equidistant from the start point and a driftless walk
kernel/detector_d1prime.go:26 // is a coin flip. Calibrated on IID-shuffled tape at p(hold) = 0.5067
kernel/detector_d1prime.go:27 // (k=3, exit_on=close, H=12) — the report's stated conclusion and the operating
kernel/detector_d1prime.go:28 // point BOTH replays (1m and 1h) ran on, so the live instrument matches the
kernel/detector_d1prime.go:29 // baselines it will be compared against.
kernel/detector_d1prime.go:30 //
kernel/detector_d1prime.go:31 // OWNER RULING 2026-09-03 on chosen_k.json: that artifact says k=6/range. Its
kernel/detector_d1prime.go:32 // selection rule minimized |p−0.5| and IGNORED ambiguity share — k=6 discards
kernel/detector_d1prime.go:33 // 50.6% of episodes (n 56,922 → 21,948) to buy 0.0015, which is inside the
kernel/detector_d1prime.go:34 // confidence interval. The report's k=3 stands; k=3/range is computed as a
kernel/detector_d1prime.go:35 // SENSITIVITY variant and presented beside it, never used for decisions.
kernel/detector_d1prime.go:36 //
kernel/detector_d1prime.go:37 // Reference: ~/nofx-analysis/detector-redesign/detectors.py detect_symmetric_v2.
kernel/detector_d1prime.go:38
kernel/detector_d1prime.go:39 // TouchOutcome is one episode. Ambiguous outcomes are RECORDED and excluded

kernel/detector_d1prime.go:93 // MeanAbsIncrement is Δ: the mean |close-to-close increment| over the supplied
kernel/detector_d1prime.go:94 // bars. This is the tape's OWN scale, re-derived per period — never a constant.
kernel/detector_d1prime.go:95 // Fewer than 2 bars → 0, and a 0 Δ means the detector cannot run (no band).
kernel/detector_d1prime.go:96 func MeanAbsIncrement(bars []market.Kline) float64 {
kernel/detector_d1prime.go:97 	if len(bars) < 2 {
kernel/detector_d1prime.go:98 		return 0
kernel/detector_d1prime.go:99 	}
kernel/detector_d1prime.go:100 	sum, n := 0.0, 0
kernel/detector_d1prime.go:101 	for i := 1; i < len(bars); i++ {
kernel/detector_d1prime.go:102 		sum += math.Abs(bars[i].Close - bars[i-1].Close)
kernel/detector_d1prime.go:103 		n++
kernel/detector_d1prime.go:104 	}
kernel/detector_d1prime.go:105 	if n == 0 {
kernel/detector_d1prime.go:106 		return 0
kernel/detector_d1prime.go:107 	}
kernel/detector_d1prime.go:108 	return sum / float64(n)
kernel/detector_d1prime.go:109 }
kernel/detector_d1prime.go:110
kernel/detector_d1prime.go:111 // DetectTouchOutcomes is the ONE live detector. A port of detect_symmetric_v2,
kernel/detector_d1prime.go:112 // deliberately line-for-line so the parity fixture can be byte-equal.
kernel/detector_d1prime.go:113 //
kernel/detector_d1prime.go:114 // An episode OPENS on a genuine touch of the level — the bar's range contains
kernel/detector_d1prime.go:115 // L and the PREVIOUS bar's did not — not on entry to a band edge. The entry
kernel/detector_d1prime.go:116 // side is the side the previous bar closed on. It CLOSES when a close (or, in
kernel/detector_d1prime.go:117 // range mode, a bar's range) passes a barrier: exiting the entry side is a
kernel/detector_d1prime.go:118 // HOLD, the far side is a BREAK, both in one bar is ambiguous_span, and running
kernel/detector_d1prime.go:119 // out of horizon is ambiguous_horizon.
kernel/detector_d1prime.go:120 func DetectTouchOutcomes(bars []market.Kline, level, k, delta float64, horizon int, exitOn string) []TouchOutcome {
kernel/detector_d1prime.go:121 	if level <= 0 || k <= 0 || delta <= 0 || horizon <= 0 || len(bars) < 2 {
kernel/detector_d1prime.go:122 		return nil
kernel/detector_d1prime.go:123 	}
kernel/detector_d1prime.go:124 	up, lo := level+k*delta, level-k*delta
kernel/detector_d1prime.go:125 	var eps []TouchOutcome
kernel/detector_d1prime.go:126 	ordinal := 0
kernel/detector_d1prime.go:127 	n := len(bars)
kernel/detector_d1prime.go:128 	for i := 1; i < n; {
kernel/detector_d1prime.go:129 		b, pb := bars[i], bars[i-1]
kernel/detector_d1prime.go:130 		touch := pb.Low <= level && level <= pb.High
kernel/detector_d1prime.go:131 		cur := b.Low <= level && level <= b.High
kernel/detector_d1prime.go:132 		if !(cur && !touch) {
kernel/detector_d1prime.go:133 			i++
kernel/detector_d1prime.go:134 			continue
kernel/detector_d1prime.go:135 		}
kernel/detector_d1prime.go:136 		entry := "above"
kernel/detector_d1prime.go:137 		if pb.Close < level {
kernel/detector_d1prime.go:138 			entry = "below"
kernel/detector_d1prime.go:139 		}
kernel/detector_d1prime.go:140 		ordinal++
kernel/detector_d1prime.go:141 		outcome, exitSide := "", ""
kernel/detector_d1prime.go:142 		mfe, mae := 0.0, 0.0
kernel/detector_d1prime.go:143 		j := i + 1
kernel/detector_d1prime.go:144 		for ; j < n && (j-i) <= horizon; j++ {
kernel/detector_d1prime.go:145 			c := bars[j]
kernel/detector_d1prime.go:146 			if entry == "below" {
kernel/detector_d1prime.go:147 				mfe = math.Max(mfe, c.High-level)
kernel/detector_d1prime.go:148 				mae = math.Min(mae, c.Low-level)
kernel/detector_d1prime.go:149 			} else {
kernel/detector_d1prime.go:150 				mfe = math.Max(mfe, level-c.Low)
kernel/detector_d1prime.go:151 				mae = math.Min(mae, level-c.High)
kernel/detector_d1prime.go:152 			}
kernel/detector_d1prime.go:153 			var cu, cd bool
kernel/detector_d1prime.go:154 			if exitOn == "close" {
kernel/detector_d1prime.go:155 				cu, cd = c.Close > up, c.Close < lo
kernel/detector_d1prime.go:156 			} else {
kernel/detector_d1prime.go:157 				cu, cd = c.High > up, c.Low < lo
kernel/detector_d1prime.go:158 			}
kernel/detector_d1prime.go:159 			if cu && cd {
kernel/detector_d1prime.go:160 				outcome = "ambiguous_span"
kernel/detector_d1prime.go:161 				break
kernel/detector_d1prime.go:162 			}
kernel/detector_d1prime.go:163 			if cu {
kernel/detector_d1prime.go:164 				outcome, exitSide = breakOrHold(entry, "above"), "above"
kernel/detector_d1prime.go:165 				break
kernel/detector_d1prime.go:166 			}
kernel/detector_d1prime.go:167 			if cd {
kernel/detector_d1prime.go:168 				outcome, exitSide = breakOrHold(entry, "below"), "below"
kernel/detector_d1prime.go:169 				break
kernel/detector_d1prime.go:170 			}
kernel/detector_d1prime.go:171 		}
kernel/detector_d1prime.go:172 		if outcome == "" {
kernel/detector_d1prime.go:173 			outcome = "ambiguous_horizon"
kernel/detector_d1prime.go:174 		}
kernel/detector_d1prime.go:175 		closedAt := int64(0)
kernel/detector_d1prime.go:176 		if j < n {
kernel/detector_d1prime.go:177 			closedAt = bars[j].OpenTime
kernel/detector_d1prime.go:178 		}
kernel/detector_d1prime.go:179 		eps = append(eps, TouchOutcome{
kernel/detector_d1prime.go:180 			Ordinal: ordinal, OpenedAtMs: b.OpenTime, ClosedAtMs: closedAt,
kernel/detector_d1prime.go:181 			Entry: entry, Exit: exitSide, Outcome: outcome,
kernel/detector_d1prime.go:182 			BarsToExit: j - i, MFE: mfe, MAE: mae,
kernel/detector_d1prime.go:183 		})
kernel/detector_d1prime.go:184 		i = j + 1
kernel/detector_d1prime.go:185 	}
kernel/detector_d1prime.go:186 	return eps
kernel/detector_d1prime.go:187 }
kernel/detector_d1prime.go:188
kernel/detector_d1prime.go:189 // breakOrHold: exiting the side you came FROM is a hold; the far side a break.
kernel/detector_d1prime.go:190 func breakOrHold(entry, exit string) string {
kernel/detector_d1prime.go:191 	if entry == exit {
kernel/detector_d1prime.go:192 		return "hold"
kernel/detector_d1prime.go:193 	}
kernel/detector_d1prime.go:194 	return "break"
kernel/detector_d1prime.go:195 }
kernel/detector_d1prime.go:196
kernel/detector_d1prime.go:197 // HoldRate returns hold/(hold+break) with n, and the ambiguous count that was
kernel/detector_d1prime.go:198 // EXCLUDED. A rate without its n is not evidence (A24), so callers get both.
kernel/detector_d1prime.go:199 func HoldRate(eps []TouchOutcome) (p float64, n, ambiguous int) {
kernel/detector_d1prime.go:200 	hold, brk := 0, 0
kernel/detector_d1prime.go:201 	for _, e := range eps {
kernel/detector_d1prime.go:202 		switch {
kernel/detector_d1prime.go:203 		case e.IsAmbiguous():
kernel/detector_d1prime.go:204 			ambiguous++
kernel/detector_d1prime.go:205 		case e.Outcome == "hold":
kernel/detector_d1prime.go:206 			hold++
kernel/detector_d1prime.go:207 		case e.Outcome == "break":
kernel/detector_d1prime.go:208 			brk++
kernel/detector_d1prime.go:209 		}
kernel/detector_d1prime.go:210 	}
kernel/detector_d1prime.go:211 	n = hold + brk
kernel/detector_d1prime.go:212 	if n == 0 {
kernel/detector_d1prime.go:213 		return 0, 0, ambiguous
kernel/detector_d1prime.go:214 	}
kernel/detector_d1prime.go:215 	return float64(hold) / float64(n), n, ambiguous
kernel/detector_d1prime.go:216 }
kernel/detector_d1prime.go:217
kernel/detector_d1prime.go:218 // WilsonInterval is the 95% score interval for p over n. Returns (0,0) at n=0.
kernel/detector_d1prime.go:219 func WilsonInterval(p float64, n int) (lo, hi float64) {
kernel/detector_d1prime.go:220 	if n <= 0 {
kernel/detector_d1prime.go:221 		return 0, 0
kernel/detector_d1prime.go:222 	}
kernel/detector_d1prime.go:223 	const z = 1.959963984540054
kernel/detector_d1prime.go:224 	nf := float64(n)
kernel/detector_d1prime.go:225 	den := 1 + z*z/nf
kernel/detector_d1prime.go:226 	centre := (p + z*z/(2*nf)) / den
kernel/detector_d1prime.go:227 	half := z * math.Sqrt(p*(1-p)/nf+z*z/(4*nf*nf)) / den
kernel/detector_d1prime.go:228 	return centre - half, centre + half
kernel/detector_d1prime.go:229 }
kernel/detector_d1prime.go:230
kernel/detector_d1prime.go:231 // DetectorRateFloor is the n below which a rate is DESCRIPTIVE ONLY.
kernel/detector_d1prime.go:232 const DetectorRateFloor = 200
kernel/detector_d1prime.go:233
kernel/detector_d1prime.go:234 // FormatHoldRate renders a rate that can never be quoted without its base.
kernel/detector_d1prime.go:235 func FormatHoldRate(eps []TouchOutcome) string {
kernel/detector_d1prime.go:236 	p, n, amb := HoldRate(eps)
kernel/detector_d1prime.go:237 	if n == 0 {
kernel/detector_d1prime.go:238 		return "p(hold)=n/a (n=0" + ambSuffix(amb) + ")"
kernel/detector_d1prime.go:239 	}
kernel/detector_d1prime.go:240 	lo, hi := WilsonInterval(p, n)
kernel/detector_d1prime.go:241 	s := "p(hold)=" + strconv.FormatFloat(p, 'f', 3, 64) +
kernel/detector_d1prime.go:242 		" [" + strconv.FormatFloat(lo, 'f', 3, 64) + ", " + strconv.FormatFloat(hi, 'f', 3, 64) + "]" +
kernel/detector_d1prime.go:243 		" n=" + strconv.Itoa(n) + ambSuffix(amb)
kernel/detector_d1prime.go:244 	if n < DetectorRateFloor {
kernel/detector_d1prime.go:245 		s += " — n<" + strconv.Itoa(DetectorRateFloor) + ", DESCRIPTIVE ONLY"
kernel/detector_d1prime.go:246 	}
kernel/detector_d1prime.go:247 	return s
kernel/detector_d1prime.go:248 }
kernel/detector_d1prime.go:249
kernel/detector_d1prime.go:250 func ambSuffix(amb int) string {
kernel/detector_d1prime.go:251 	if amb == 0 {
kernel/detector_d1prime.go:252 		return ""
kernel/detector_d1prime.go:253 	}
kernel/detector_d1prime.go:254 	return " · ambiguous=" + strconv.Itoa(amb) + " excluded"
kernel/detector_d1prime.go:255 }
kernel/detector_d1prime.go:256
kernel/detector_d1prime.go:257 // MeanAbsIncrementOf is Δ from a bare increment series (the calibration path).
kernel/detector_d1prime.go:258 func MeanAbsIncrementOf(incs []float64) float64 {
kernel/detector_d1prime.go:259 	if len(incs) == 0 {
kernel/detector_d1prime.go:260 		return 0
kernel/detector_d1prime.go:261 	}
kernel/detector_d1prime.go:262 	sum := 0.0
kernel/detector_d1prime.go:263 	for _, d := range incs {
kernel/detector_d1prime.go:264 		sum += math.Abs(d)
kernel/detector_d1prime.go:265 	}
kernel/detector_d1prime.go:266 	return sum / float64(len(incs))
kernel/detector_d1prime.go:267 }

kernel/detector_d1prime.go:274 // web/src/guide/content and the Go tree: no surface renders a RATE from the
kernel/detector_d1prime.go:275 // retired instruments. The single Guide hit — "first-touch reactions with
kernel/detector_d1prime.go:276 // confirmation" in the REACT ZONE card — is a play DESCRIPTION, not a measured
kernel/detector_d1prime.go:277 // rate, so it is not a retirement target. TestNoSurfaceRendersARetiredRate

kernel/session_registry.go:13 // is the single source of truth for each session's clock: window, planner read
kernel/session_registry.go:14 // time, session-flat time, killzones, and whether it is enabled. It follows the

kernel/session_registry.go:16 // NEVER local machine time — so DST is handled by the tz database and the ~3

kernel/session_registry.go:21 // as JSON in system_config under SessionRegistryConfigKey; empty/absent → the

kernel/session_registry.go:126 // LoadSessionRegistry parses a stored registry JSON. Empty input → the default
kernel/session_registry.go:127 // registry. On a parse error it returns the default AND the error so the caller
kernel/session_registry.go:128 // can log and never run with an empty registry (fail-safe, never silently off).
kernel/session_registry.go:129 func LoadSessionRegistry(raw string) (SessionRegistry, error) {
kernel/session_registry.go:130 	if strings.TrimSpace(raw) == "" {
kernel/session_registry.go:131 		return DefaultSessionRegistry(), nil
kernel/session_registry.go:132 	}
kernel/session_registry.go:133 	var r SessionRegistry
kernel/session_registry.go:134 	if err := json.Unmarshal([]byte(raw), &r); err != nil {
kernel/session_registry.go:135 		return DefaultSessionRegistry(), err
kernel/session_registry.go:136 	}
kernel/session_registry.go:137 	if len(r.Sessions) == 0 {
kernel/session_registry.go:138 		return DefaultSessionRegistry(), nil
kernel/session_registry.go:139 	}
kernel/session_registry.go:140 	return r, nil
kernel/session_registry.go:141 }
kernel/session_registry.go:142
kernel/session_registry.go:143 // ValidateSessionRegistry rejects a registry that would break the gates — unlike
kernel/session_registry.go:144 // LoadSessionRegistry (which fail-safes to the default on bad input), an EDIT must
kernel/session_registry.go:145 // be refused, not silently defaulted. Requires ≥1 session, each with a name and
kernel/session_registry.go:146 // four parseable HH:MM CT anchors (window start/end, read, flat); killzone bounds
kernel/session_registry.go:147 // must parse too. Returns nil when safe to persist.
kernel/session_registry.go:148 func ValidateSessionRegistry(r SessionRegistry) error {
kernel/session_registry.go:149 	if len(r.Sessions) == 0 {
kernel/session_registry.go:150 		return fmt.Errorf("registry must have at least one session")
kernel/session_registry.go:151 	}
kernel/session_registry.go:152 	for i := range r.Sessions {
kernel/session_registry.go:153 		s := r.Sessions[i]
kernel/session_registry.go:154 		if strings.TrimSpace(s.Name) == "" {
kernel/session_registry.go:155 			return fmt.Errorf("session %d: name required", i)
kernel/session_registry.go:156 		}
kernel/session_registry.go:157 		for field, v := range map[string]string{
kernel/session_registry.go:158 			"window_start_ct": s.WindowStartCT, "window_end_ct": s.WindowEndCT,
kernel/session_registry.go:159 			"read_ct": s.ReadCT, "flat_ct": s.FlatCT,
kernel/session_registry.go:160 		} {
kernel/session_registry.go:161 			if _, ok := parseHHMM(v); !ok {
kernel/session_registry.go:162 				return fmt.Errorf("session %q: %s %q is not HH:MM", s.Name, field, v)
kernel/session_registry.go:163 			}
kernel/session_registry.go:164 		}
kernel/session_registry.go:165 		for _, kz := range s.Killzones {
kernel/session_registry.go:166 			if _, ok := parseHHMM(kz.StartCT); !ok {
kernel/session_registry.go:167 				return fmt.Errorf("session %q killzone %q: start %q is not HH:MM", s.Name, kz.Name, kz.StartCT)
kernel/session_registry.go:168 			}
kernel/session_registry.go:169 			if _, ok := parseHHMM(kz.EndCT); !ok {
kernel/session_registry.go:170 				return fmt.Errorf("session %q killzone %q: end %q is not HH:MM", s.Name, kz.Name, kz.EndCT)
kernel/session_registry.go:171 			}
kernel/session_registry.go:172 		}
kernel/session_registry.go:173 	}
kernel/session_registry.go:174 	// P4 (ledger-close 2026-08-19) — validate HalfDays too: keys are session-day
kernel/session_registry.go:175 	// dates (YYYY-MM-DD), values early-close CT (HH:MM). Garbage was previously
kernel/session_registry.go:176 	// persistable through the API door and silently ignored at consumption.
kernel/session_registry.go:177 	for k, v := range r.HalfDays {
kernel/session_registry.go:178 		if _, err := time.Parse("2006-01-02", k); err != nil {
kernel/session_registry.go:179 			return fmt.Errorf("half_days key %q is not YYYY-MM-DD", k)
kernel/session_registry.go:180 		}
kernel/session_registry.go:181 		if _, ok := parseHHMM(v); !ok {
kernel/session_registry.go:182 			return fmt.Errorf("half_days[%q]: %q is not HH:MM (CT)", k, v)
kernel/session_registry.go:183 		}
kernel/session_registry.go:184 	}
kernel/session_registry.go:185 	return nil
kernel/session_registry.go:186 }

kernel/session_registry.go:188 // SessionByName returns the named session (case-insensitive), or (nil, false).
kernel/session_registry.go:189 func (r SessionRegistry) SessionByName(name string) (*SessionDef, bool) {
kernel/session_registry.go:190 	for i := range r.Sessions {
kernel/session_registry.go:191 		if strings.EqualFold(r.Sessions[i].Name, name) {
kernel/session_registry.go:192 			return &r.Sessions[i], true
kernel/session_registry.go:193 		}
kernel/session_registry.go:194 	}

kernel/session_registry.go:208 }

kernel/session_registry.go:210 // IsNightMode (P3.6-D) reports whether now is in NIGHT state: there is no ACTIVE

kernel/session_registry.go:261 // IsReadTime reports whether now's CT wall-clock minute equals the session's

kernel/session_registry.go:268 	}

kernel/session_registry.go:276 	return ct.Hour()*60 + ct.Minute()
kernel/session_registry.go:277 }

kernel/plan_doc.go:97 // PlanArmSpec is the machine-manageable arming contract for one scenario.

kernel/plan_doc.go:128 // ArmSpecValid checks the arming contract of one scenario. ok=false with a
kernel/plan_doc.go:129 // reason when the contract is malformed or the condition is not armable.
kernel/plan_doc.go:130 func ArmSpecValid(sc PlanScenario) error {
kernel/plan_doc.go:131 	if sc.Arm == nil || !sc.Arm.Enabled {
kernel/plan_doc.go:132 		return nil // not armed — nothing to validate
kernel/plan_doc.go:133 	}
kernel/plan_doc.go:134 	// Autopsy-response wave (2026-08-27): sweep_reclaim becomes armable ONLY
kernel/plan_doc.go:135 	// as a CHAINED arm (wait_confirm) — the arm rests until the scenario's own
kernel/plan_doc.go:136 	// confirm{} is machine-MET, then the retrace entry goes live.
kernel/plan_doc.go:137 	// E4 (2026-08-30): the SPLIT entry replaces the single chain — the legacy
kernel/plan_doc.go:138 	// wait_confirm requirement applies to single arms only.
kernel/plan_doc.go:139 	if strings.EqualFold(strings.TrimSpace(sc.Condition), "sweep_reclaim") {
kernel/plan_doc.go:140 		if len(sc.Arm.Legs) == 0 && !sc.Arm.WaitConfirm {
kernel/plan_doc.go:141 			return fmt.Errorf("sweep_reclaim arm on %s requires wait_confirm:true (the retrace arm must chain on its confirm)", sc.ID)
kernel/plan_doc.go:142 		}
kernel/plan_doc.go:143 		if sc.Confirm == nil {
kernel/plan_doc.go:144 			return fmt.Errorf("sweep_reclaim arm on %s requires a confirm{} object to chain on", sc.ID)
kernel/plan_doc.go:145 		}
kernel/plan_doc.go:146 	} else if IsBreakdownCondition(sc.Condition) {
kernel/plan_doc.go:147 		// Waterfall-class arms (2026-08-28): the resting limit sits AT the broken
kernel/plan_doc.go:148 		// level and chains on confirm leg 1 — exactly the pullback-that-fails
kernel/plan_doc.go:149 		// entry the play describes. Immediate-mode entries cannot rest.
kernel/plan_doc.go:150 		if sc.Breakdown == nil {
kernel/plan_doc.go:151 			return fmt.Errorf("%s arm requires the breakdown{} facts object", sc.ID)
kernel/plan_doc.go:152 		}
kernel/plan_doc.go:153 		if !strings.EqualFold(strings.TrimSpace(sc.Breakdown.EntryMode), "pullback") {
kernel/plan_doc.go:154 			return fmt.Errorf("%s arm requires entry_mode=pullback (immediate-mode entries are AI-path only)", sc.ID)
kernel/plan_doc.go:155 		}
kernel/plan_doc.go:156 		if !sc.Arm.WaitConfirm {
kernel/plan_doc.go:157 			return fmt.Errorf("%s arm requires wait_confirm:true (it chains on confirm leg 1 before resting at the level)", sc.ID)
kernel/plan_doc.go:158 		}
kernel/plan_doc.go:159 		if sc.Confirm == nil {
kernel/plan_doc.go:160 			return fmt.Errorf("%s arm requires a confirm{} (leg 1) to chain on", sc.ID)
kernel/plan_doc.go:161 		}
kernel/plan_doc.go:162 	} else if !ArmableCondition(sc.Condition) {
kernel/plan_doc.go:163 		return fmt.Errorf("arm enabled on non-armable condition %q (fvg_entry | reject | breakdown_continue | breakup_continue; sweep_reclaim via wait_confirm; breakout_retest is a normal AI play and never arms — GAR-F4)", sc.Condition)
kernel/plan_doc.go:164 	}
kernel/plan_doc.go:165 	a := sc.Arm
kernel/plan_doc.go:166 	// E4 (2026-08-30) — split-entry legs. sweep_reclaim ONLY for now; exactly
kernel/plan_doc.go:167 	// two; leg 0 = the touch leg resting AT the sweep ref (no chain), leg 1 =
kernel/plan_doc.go:168 	// the momentum leg chained on 1m_mss (1x5m_close accepted).
kernel/plan_doc.go:169 	if len(a.Legs) > 0 {
kernel/plan_doc.go:170 		if !strings.EqualFold(strings.TrimSpace(sc.Condition), "sweep_reclaim") {
kernel/plan_doc.go:171 			return fmt.Errorf("arm legs on %s — arm_legs_sweep_reclaim_only %s", sc.Condition, ArmLegsSplitContract)
kernel/plan_doc.go:172 		}
kernel/plan_doc.go:173 		if len(a.Legs) != 2 {
kernel/plan_doc.go:174 			return fmt.Errorf("arm on %s needs EXACTLY 2 legs (split contract), got %d", sc.ID, len(a.Legs))
kernel/plan_doc.go:175 		}
kernel/plan_doc.go:176 		if sc.Confirm == nil || sc.Confirm.Rule != "touch" {
kernel/plan_doc.go:177 			return fmt.Errorf("arm on %s split requires confirm=touch at the sweep ref (leg 1 rests AT the level)", sc.ID)
kernel/plan_doc.go:178 		}
kernel/plan_doc.go:179 		if sc.Confirm2 == nil {
kernel/plan_doc.go:180 			return fmt.Errorf("arm on %s split requires confirm2 (the leg-2 chain: 1m_mss or 1x5m_close)", sc.ID)
kernel/plan_doc.go:181 		}
kernel/plan_doc.go:182 		l0, l1 := a.Legs[0], a.Legs[1]
kernel/plan_doc.go:183 		if l0.WaitConfirm {
kernel/plan_doc.go:184 			return fmt.Errorf("arm on %s leg 1 must rest at the sweep ref (wait_confirm false) — it fills ON the touch", sc.ID)
kernel/plan_doc.go:185 		}
kernel/plan_doc.go:186 		if !l1.WaitConfirm {
kernel/plan_doc.go:187 			return fmt.Errorf("arm on %s leg 2 must chain (wait_confirm true) on its confirm rule", sc.ID)
kernel/plan_doc.go:188 		}
kernel/plan_doc.go:189 		if l1.Rule != "1m_mss" && l1.Rule != "1x5m_close" {
kernel/plan_doc.go:190 			return fmt.Errorf("arm on %s leg 2 rule %q — sweep_leg2_requires_mss_or_1x5m", sc.ID, l1.Rule)
kernel/plan_doc.go:191 		}
kernel/plan_doc.go:192 		if l1.Rule != sc.Confirm2.Rule {
kernel/plan_doc.go:193 			return fmt.Errorf("arm on %s leg 2 rule %q must match confirm2.rule %q (the chain is the machine confirm)", sc.ID, l1.Rule, sc.Confirm2.Rule)
kernel/plan_doc.go:194 		}
kernel/plan_doc.go:195 		if l0.Kind != "" && l0.Kind != "limit" {
kernel/plan_doc.go:196 			return fmt.Errorf("arm on %s leg 1 must be a limit (touch leg), got kind %q", sc.ID, l0.Kind)
kernel/plan_doc.go:197 		}
kernel/plan_doc.go:198 		if l1.Kind != "" && l1.Kind != "limit" && l1.Kind != "stop_entry" {
kernel/plan_doc.go:199 			return fmt.Errorf("arm on %s leg 2 kind %q invalid (limit|stop_entry)", sc.ID, l1.Kind)
kernel/plan_doc.go:200 		}
kernel/plan_doc.go:201 		// The top-level fields mirror leg 1 for legacy readers.
kernel/plan_doc.go:202 		if a.Entry != l0.Entry || a.Stop != l0.Stop || a.Target != l0.Target {
kernel/plan_doc.go:203 			return fmt.Errorf("arm on %s top-level entry/stop/target must equal leg 1's (legacy readers read the top-level)", sc.ID)
kernel/plan_doc.go:204 		}
kernel/plan_doc.go:205 	}
kernel/plan_doc.go:206 	if a.Entry <= 0 || a.Stop <= 0 || a.Target <= 0 {
kernel/plan_doc.go:207 		return fmt.Errorf("arm on %s needs exact entry/stop/target > 0 (got %.2f/%.2f/%.2f)", sc.ID, a.Entry, a.Stop, a.Target)
kernel/plan_doc.go:208 	}
kernel/plan_doc.go:209 	dir := strings.ToLower(strings.TrimSpace(sc.Direction))
kernel/plan_doc.go:210 	if dir == "long" {
kernel/plan_doc.go:211 		if !(a.Stop < a.Entry && a.Entry < a.Target) {
kernel/plan_doc.go:212 			return fmt.Errorf("arm on %s long: stop %.2f < entry %.2f < target %.2f required", sc.ID, a.Stop, a.Entry, a.Target)
kernel/plan_doc.go:213 		}
kernel/plan_doc.go:214 		for i, l := range a.Legs {
kernel/plan_doc.go:215 			if !(l.Stop < l.Entry && l.Entry < l.Target) {
kernel/plan_doc.go:216 				return fmt.Errorf("arm on %s long leg %d: stop %.2f < entry %.2f < target %.2f required", sc.ID, i+1, l.Stop, l.Entry, l.Target)
kernel/plan_doc.go:217 			}
kernel/plan_doc.go:218 		}
kernel/plan_doc.go:219 	} else if dir == "short" {
kernel/plan_doc.go:220 		if !(a.Target < a.Entry && a.Entry < a.Stop) {
kernel/plan_doc.go:221 			return fmt.Errorf("arm on %s short: target %.2f < entry %.2f < stop %.2f required", sc.ID, a.Target, a.Entry, a.Stop)
kernel/plan_doc.go:222 		}
kernel/plan_doc.go:223 		for i, l := range a.Legs {
kernel/plan_doc.go:224 			if !(l.Target < l.Entry && l.Entry < l.Stop) {
kernel/plan_doc.go:225 				return fmt.Errorf("arm on %s short leg %d: target %.2f < entry %.2f < stop %.2f required", sc.ID, i+1, l.Target, l.Entry, l.Stop)

kernel/plan_doc.go:367 // resolvePlanCaps turns resolved config values into effective caps: ≤0 → shipped
kernel/plan_doc.go:368 // defaults; above the hard ceilings → clamped DOWN to them (a bad config value
kernel/plan_doc.go:369 // can never widen the schema past what the UI offers).
kernel/plan_doc.go:370 func resolvePlanCaps(maxLevels, maxScenarios int) (maxL, maxS int) {
kernel/plan_doc.go:371 	maxL = planMaxLevels
kernel/plan_doc.go:372 	if maxLevels > 0 {
kernel/plan_doc.go:373 		maxL = maxLevels
kernel/plan_doc.go:374 	}
kernel/plan_doc.go:375 	if maxL > PlanHardMaxLevels {
kernel/plan_doc.go:376 		maxL = PlanHardMaxLevels
kernel/plan_doc.go:377 	}
kernel/plan_doc.go:378 	maxS = planMaxScenarios
kernel/plan_doc.go:379 	if maxScenarios > 0 {
kernel/plan_doc.go:380 		maxS = maxScenarios
kernel/plan_doc.go:381 	}
kernel/plan_doc.go:382 	if maxS > PlanHardMaxScenarios {
kernel/plan_doc.go:383 		maxS = PlanHardMaxScenarios
kernel/plan_doc.go:384 	}
kernel/plan_doc.go:385 	return maxL, maxS
kernel/plan_doc.go:386 }
kernel/plan_doc.go:387
kernel/plan_doc.go:388 // CollapsePlanLevels merges plan levels closer than tol points into ONE entry
kernel/plan_doc.go:389 // (the P0.4 cluster rule, applied to the model's own output — 2026-08-24 ASIA
kernel/plan_doc.go:390 // v2 fail-closed because the model wrote two levels 2.13 pts apart and the
kernel/plan_doc.go:391 // duplicate-seat validation burned all retries). The survivor is the higher
kernel/plan_doc.go:392 // grade; ties keep the first. Consumed ORs across the merge. Pure.
kernel/plan_doc.go:393 func CollapsePlanLevels(levels []PlanLevel, tol float64) ([]PlanLevel, int) {
kernel/plan_doc.go:394 	if len(levels) < 2 || tol <= 0 {
kernel/plan_doc.go:395 		return levels, 0
kernel/plan_doc.go:396 	}
kernel/plan_doc.go:397 	out := make([]PlanLevel, 0, len(levels))
kernel/plan_doc.go:398 	merged := 0
kernel/plan_doc.go:399 	for _, l := range levels {
kernel/plan_doc.go:400 		hit := -1
kernel/plan_doc.go:401 		for i, o := range out {
kernel/plan_doc.go:402 			if math.Abs(o.Price-l.Price) <= tol {
kernel/plan_doc.go:403 				hit = i
kernel/plan_doc.go:404 				break
kernel/plan_doc.go:405 			}
kernel/plan_doc.go:406 		}
kernel/plan_doc.go:407 		if hit < 0 {
kernel/plan_doc.go:408 			out = append(out, l)
kernel/plan_doc.go:409 			continue
kernel/plan_doc.go:410 		}
kernel/plan_doc.go:411 		merged++
kernel/plan_doc.go:412 		old := out[hit]
kernel/plan_doc.go:413 		if planGradeRank(l.Grade) > planGradeRank(old.Grade) {
kernel/plan_doc.go:414 			out[hit] = l
kernel/plan_doc.go:415 		}
kernel/plan_doc.go:416 	}
kernel/plan_doc.go:417 	return out, merged
kernel/plan_doc.go:418 }
kernel/plan_doc.go:419
kernel/plan_doc.go:420 // planGradeRank ranks a level grade A > B > C (unknown → 0).
kernel/plan_doc.go:421 func planGradeRank(g string) int {
kernel/plan_doc.go:422 	switch strings.ToUpper(strings.TrimSpace(g)) {
kernel/plan_doc.go:423 	case "A":
kernel/plan_doc.go:424 		return 3
kernel/plan_doc.go:425 	case "B":
kernel/plan_doc.go:426 		return 2
kernel/plan_doc.go:427 	case "C":
kernel/plan_doc.go:428 		return 1
kernel/plan_doc.go:429 	}
kernel/plan_doc.go:430 	return 0
kernel/plan_doc.go:431 }

kernel/plan_doc.go:466 // NormalizePlanDocRules (F1b, LONDON-FORENSICS 2026-08-28) canonicalizes every
kernel/plan_doc.go:467 // confirm/flip/death rule spelling the model has been observed producing. The
kernel/plan_doc.go:468 // audit (journal, 2026-08-23→28) found flip.rule "2x5m_close" (15 rejects) and
kernel/plan_doc.go:469 // confirm.rule "5m_close" (2 rejects); the wider alias set mirrors the
kernel/plan_doc.go:470 // scenario-facts vocabulary so future spellings normalize too. Unknown
kernel/plan_doc.go:471 // spellings pass through unchanged — validation still rejects them honestly.
kernel/plan_doc.go:472 func NormalizePlanDocRules(d *PlanDoc) {
kernel/plan_doc.go:473 	if d == nil {
kernel/plan_doc.go:474 		return
kernel/plan_doc.go:475 	}

kernel/plan_doc.go:478 			d.Scenarios[i].Confirm.Rule = NormalizeConfirmRule(d.Scenarios[i].Confirm.Rule)

kernel/plan_doc.go:481 	if d.FlipStructured != nil {

kernel/plan_doc.go:485 		d.DeathStructured.Rule = NormalizeConditionRule(d.DeathStructured.Rule)
kernel/plan_doc.go:486 	}

kernel/plan_doc.go:489 	// WEEKLY doc's vocabulary (kernel/weekly_prompt.go), and aliasing a day-plan

kernel/plan_doc.go:498 // NormalizeBiasDirection canonicalizes a day-plan bias direction to the set the

kernel/plan_doc.go:500 // unchanged so validation still rejects them honestly (class 11's law).
kernel/plan_doc.go:501 func NormalizeBiasDirection(dir string) string {
kernel/plan_doc.go:502 	switch strings.ToLower(strings.TrimSpace(dir)) {
kernel/plan_doc.go:503 	case "bearish", "bear", "down", "downside", "sell":
kernel/plan_doc.go:504 		return "short"
kernel/plan_doc.go:505 	case "bullish", "bull", "up", "upside", "buy":
kernel/plan_doc.go:506 		return "long"
kernel/plan_doc.go:507 	}
kernel/plan_doc.go:508 	// DELIBERATELY NOT ALIASED: "flat" / "sideways" / "range" → neutral. Those
kernel/plan_doc.go:509 	// are SEMANTIC judgements, not spellings of a known token, and "neutral" is
kernel/plan_doc.go:510 	// already writable directly. "sideways" also serves as the invalid-direction
kernel/plan_doc.go:511 	// sentinel in two existing fixtures (plan_doc_test, w4_overlay_executor_test)
kernel/plan_doc.go:512 	// — aliasing it would have silently weakened both. An alias table fixes
kernel/plan_doc.go:513 	// SPELLING; it must never decide meaning.
kernel/plan_doc.go:514 	return dir
kernel/plan_doc.go:515 }

kernel/plan_doc.go:548 // ArmFeasibilityWarnings (F4, LONDON-FORENSICS 2026-08-28) reports arms that
kernel/plan_doc.go:549 // the gate-at-arm chain would refuse EVERY cycle: R:R below the arm minimum or
kernel/plan_doc.go:550 // a stop tighter than minSLMult × ATR5m. Advisory only — the write succeeds
kernel/plan_doc.go:551 // (the executor's hard gate is the enforcement); the planner learns instead of
kernel/plan_doc.go:552 // burning 120 refusal lines a night.
kernel/plan_doc.go:553 func ArmFeasibilityWarnings(d *PlanDoc, atr5m, minRR, minSLMult float64) []string {
kernel/plan_doc.go:554 	if d == nil || atr5m <= 0 {
kernel/plan_doc.go:555 		return nil
kernel/plan_doc.go:556 	}
kernel/plan_doc.go:557 	var out []string
kernel/plan_doc.go:558 	for _, sc := range d.Scenarios {
kernel/plan_doc.go:559 		a := sc.Arm
kernel/plan_doc.go:560 		if a == nil || !a.Enabled {
kernel/plan_doc.go:561 			continue
kernel/plan_doc.go:562 		}
kernel/plan_doc.go:563 		dist := a.Entry - a.Stop
kernel/plan_doc.go:564 		if strings.EqualFold(sc.Direction, "short") {
kernel/plan_doc.go:565 			dist = a.Stop - a.Entry
kernel/plan_doc.go:566 		}
kernel/plan_doc.go:567 		rr := 0.0
kernel/plan_doc.go:568 		if dist > 0 && a.Entry > 0 {
kernel/plan_doc.go:569 			if strings.EqualFold(sc.Direction, "short") {
kernel/plan_doc.go:570 				rr = (a.Entry - a.Target) / dist
kernel/plan_doc.go:571 			} else {

kernel/plan_doc.go:768 // structuralPrefix returns the leading structural token of a label (e.g.

kernel/plan_doc.go:771 func structuralPrefix(label string) string {

kernel/plan_doc.go:773 	if i := strings.Index(l, "·"); i > 0 {
kernel/plan_doc.go:774 		l = l[:i]

kernel/plan_doc.go:777 		return l
kernel/plan_doc.go:778 	}

kernel/plan_doc.go:850 func ValidatePlanDocWithFactsMachine(d *PlanDoc, facts PlanFacts, machine map[float64]string, maxLevels, maxScenarios int) error {
kernel/plan_doc.go:851 	if err := ValidatePlanDocWithCaps(d, maxLevels, maxScenarios); err != nil {
kernel/plan_doc.go:852 		return err
kernel/plan_doc.go:853 	}
kernel/plan_doc.go:854 	if facts.Price <= 0 {
kernel/plan_doc.go:855 		return nil // no facts → schema-only (legacy callers/tests)
kernel/plan_doc.go:856 	}
kernel/plan_doc.go:857 	// P0.4 — duplicate-level rejection (the planner copied an EQ family 4×).
kernel/plan_doc.go:858 	for i := 0; i < len(d.Levels); i++ {
kernel/plan_doc.go:859 		for j := i + 1; j < len(d.Levels); j++ {
kernel/plan_doc.go:860 			if math.Abs(d.Levels[i].Price-d.Levels[j].Price) <= LevelClusterTicks*0.25 {
kernel/plan_doc.go:861 				return fmt.Errorf("levels[%d] and [%d] are %.2f apart — duplicates within the cluster tolerance; collapse them into one entry",
kernel/plan_doc.go:862 					i, j, math.Abs(d.Levels[i].Price-d.Levels[j].Price))
kernel/plan_doc.go:863 			}
kernel/plan_doc.go:864 		}
kernel/plan_doc.go:865 	}
kernel/plan_doc.go:866 	// P0.1-relax — both-side minimum (see doc above).

kernel/plan_doc.go:868 	for _, l := range d.Levels {

kernel/plan_doc.go:870 		case l.Price < facts.Price:
kernel/plan_doc.go:871 			below++
kernel/plan_doc.go:872 		case l.Price > facts.Price:
kernel/plan_doc.go:873 			above++
kernel/plan_doc.go:874 		}
kernel/plan_doc.go:875 	}
kernel/plan_doc.go:876 	if machine != nil && len(machine) == 0 {

kernel/plan_doc.go:1219 		if strings.EqualFold(strings.TrimSpace(sc.Condition), "sweep_reclaim") {

kernel/plan_doc.go:1221 		}

kernel/plan_doc.go:1224 		single.Legs = nil

kernel/plan_doc.go:1226 		if err := ArmSpecValid(trial); err != nil {
kernel/plan_doc.go:1227 			continue // step 4 — unchanged; the original reason surfaces downstream
kernel/plan_doc.go:1228 		}
kernel/plan_doc.go:1229 		rec := ArmNormalization{
kernel/plan_doc.go:1230 			Scenario: sc.ID, Condition: sc.Condition,
kernel/plan_doc.go:1231 			DroppedLegs: append([]PlanArmLeg(nil), sc.Arm.Legs...), // a COPY of what was authored — never a new leg
kernel/plan_doc.go:1232 			KeptEntry:   sc.Arm.Entry, KeptStop: sc.Arm.Stop, KeptTarget: sc.Arm.Target, KeptWait: sc.Arm.WaitConfirm,
kernel/plan_doc.go:1233 		}
kernel/plan_doc.go:1234 		if sc.Confirm != nil {

kernel/plan_doc.go:1236 		}
kernel/plan_doc.go:1237 		sc.Arm.Legs = nil // step 1, in place — the top-level arm IS the single arm
kernel/plan_doc.go:1238 		d.ArmNormalizations = append(d.ArmNormalizations, rec)
kernel/plan_doc.go:1239 	}
kernel/plan_doc.go:1240 }

kernel/plan_doc.go:1242 // ArmNormalizationWarn renders the class-39 write-site WARN (D2): the

kernel/planner_prompt.go:129 // RenderBiasTree (A1, planner-contract wave 2026-08-26) — the machine-computed
kernel/planner_prompt.go:130 // bias decision tree. Facts only + the branch the facts CURRENTLY match; the
kernel/planner_prompt.go:131 // planner states which branch it took (contract requirement). Premium/discount
kernel/planner_prompt.go:132 // = position within the value-area range; the draw = nearest opposing
kernel/planner_prompt.go:133 // liquidity pool (the runner target).
kernel/planner_prompt.go:134 func RenderBiasTree(price float64, levels []ScoredLevel, bc BiasContext) string {
kernel/planner_prompt.go:135 	// S-dispatch (2026-08-27) — the structured bias facts win: post-roll the
kernel/planner_prompt.go:136 	// seated table can drop PDH/PDL (out of the proximity band) and the tree
kernel/planner_prompt.go:137 	// rendered "no PDH/PDL anchor". Fall back to the seated scan for legacy
kernel/planner_prompt.go:138 	// callers/tests that only pass levels.
kernel/planner_prompt.go:139 	pdh, pdl, pdc := bc.PDH, bc.PDL, bc.PDC
kernel/planner_prompt.go:140 	for _, l := range levels {
kernel/planner_prompt.go:141 		switch l.Kind {
kernel/planner_prompt.go:142 		case KindPDH:
kernel/planner_prompt.go:143 			if pdh <= 0 {
kernel/planner_prompt.go:144 				pdh = l.Price
kernel/planner_prompt.go:145 			}
kernel/planner_prompt.go:146 		case KindPDL:
kernel/planner_prompt.go:147 			if pdl <= 0 {
kernel/planner_prompt.go:148 				pdl = l.Price
kernel/planner_prompt.go:149 			}
kernel/planner_prompt.go:150 		case KindPDC:
kernel/planner_prompt.go:151 			if pdc <= 0 {
kernel/planner_prompt.go:152 				pdc = l.Price
kernel/planner_prompt.go:153 			}
kernel/planner_prompt.go:154 		}
kernel/planner_prompt.go:155 	}
kernel/planner_prompt.go:156 	var b strings.Builder
kernel/planner_prompt.go:157 	b.WriteString("## BIAS-TREE (machine branches — your reasoning MUST state the branch you took, e.g. \"bias-tree: inside-day long LOW\")\n")
kernel/planner_prompt.go:158 	b.WriteString("  1. close > PDH → bull-continuation, conviction HIGH\n")
kernel/planner_prompt.go:159 	b.WriteString("  2. sweep of PDH + close back inside → bear, conviction MEDIUM  (mirror: sweep PDL + close inside → bull MEDIUM)\n")
kernel/planner_prompt.go:160 	b.WriteString("  3. inside the day (between PDH/PDL) → direction of close vs PDC (prior close), conviction LOW\n")
kernel/planner_prompt.go:161 	b.WriteString("  4. closed OUTSIDE the prior day's range but now inside → NO bias (write neutral; trade the structure, not a thesis)\n")
kernel/planner_prompt.go:162 	b.WriteString("  5. premium/discount: longs ONLY below the 50% mark of the dealing range, shorts ONLY above it\n")
kernel/planner_prompt.go:163 	b.WriteString("  6. draw-on-liquidity: the runner target is the DRAW — the nearest opposing liquidity pool beyond the first target\n")
kernel/planner_prompt.go:164 	fmt.Fprintf(&b, "  computed: PDH %.2f · PDL %.2f · PDC %.2f", pdh, pdl, pdc)
kernel/planner_prompt.go:165 	// S-dispatch (2026-08-27) — the branch-5 premium/discount anchor is the
kernel/planner_prompt.go:166 	// DEALING RANGE (prior-day swing hi/lo = PDH/PDL), not the value area. The
kernel/planner_prompt.go:167 	// VA can be a few points wide (the 17:46 read printed "376% of range" off
kernel/planner_prompt.go:168 	// a ~30pt VA); a beyond-range price now renders "beyond range (extended)"
kernel/planner_prompt.go:169 	// instead of a >100% percentage. Value area stays as the fallback when
kernel/planner_prompt.go:170 	// the day anchors are unknown.
kernel/planner_prompt.go:171 	lo, hi := pdl, pdh
kernel/planner_prompt.go:172 	if lo <= 0 || hi <= 0 || hi <= lo {
kernel/planner_prompt.go:173 		lo, hi = bc.VAL, bc.VAH
kernel/planner_prompt.go:174 		if lo > hi {
kernel/planner_prompt.go:175 			lo, hi = hi, lo
kernel/planner_prompt.go:176 		}
kernel/planner_prompt.go:177 	}
kernel/planner_prompt.go:178 	if hi > lo && price > 0 {
kernel/planner_prompt.go:179 		fmt.Fprintf(&b, " · dealing range %.2f–%.2f", lo, hi)
kernel/planner_prompt.go:180 		pos := (price - lo) / (hi - lo)
kernel/planner_prompt.go:181 		switch {
kernel/planner_prompt.go:182 		case pos > 1:
kernel/planner_prompt.go:183 			b.WriteString(" · price BEYOND range high (extended) — longs disallowed by branch 5 (premium)")
kernel/planner_prompt.go:184 		case pos < 0:
kernel/planner_prompt.go:185 			b.WriteString(" · price BEYOND range low (extended) — shorts disallowed by branch 5 (discount)")
kernel/planner_prompt.go:186 		case pos >= 0.5:
kernel/planner_prompt.go:187 			fmt.Fprintf(&b, " · price at %.0f%% of the range (PREMIUM — longs disallowed by branch 5)", pos*100)
kernel/planner_prompt.go:188 		default:
kernel/planner_prompt.go:189 			fmt.Fprintf(&b, " · price at %.0f%% of the range (DISCOUNT — shorts disallowed by branch 5)", pos*100)
kernel/planner_prompt.go:190 		}
kernel/planner_prompt.go:191 	}
kernel/planner_prompt.go:192 	if price > 0 && pdh > 0 && price > pdh {
kernel/planner_prompt.go:193 		b.WriteString(" · facts match branch 1 (close > PDH)")
kernel/planner_prompt.go:194 	} else if price > 0 && pdl > 0 && price < pdl {
kernel/planner_prompt.go:195 		b.WriteString(" · facts match branch 1 mirror (close < PDL)")
kernel/planner_prompt.go:196 	} else if price > 0 && pdh > 0 && pdl > 0 && pdc > 0 && price < pdh && price > pdl {
kernel/planner_prompt.go:197 		dir := "flat"
kernel/planner_prompt.go:198 		if price > pdc {
kernel/planner_prompt.go:199 			dir = "long"
kernel/planner_prompt.go:200 		} else if price < pdc {
kernel/planner_prompt.go:201 			dir = "short"
kernel/planner_prompt.go:202 		}
kernel/planner_prompt.go:203 		fmt.Fprintf(&b, " · facts match branch 3 (inside day; close %s PDC → %s LOW)", dir, dir)
kernel/planner_prompt.go:204 	}
kernel/planner_prompt.go:205 	if bc.NearestLiquidity != "" {
kernel/planner_prompt.go:206 		b.WriteString(" · draw/liquidity: " + bc.NearestLiquidity)
kernel/planner_prompt.go:207 	}
kernel/planner_prompt.go:208 	b.WriteString("\n\n")
kernel/planner_prompt.go:209 	return b.String()
kernel/planner_prompt.go:210 }
kernel/planner_prompt.go:211
kernel/planner_prompt.go:212 // ── CLASS 50b (live-bias replay ruling 53498adb, 2026-09-02) ────────────────
kernel/planner_prompt.go:213 // The plan bias is a LABEL, not a direction: the AI-authored direction, the
kernel/planner_prompt.go:214 // machine bias-tree call and the regime call are surfaced on one line so no
kernel/planner_prompt.go:215 // single source reads as truth. These three helpers are the one shape; no MUST
kernel/planner_prompt.go:216 // attaches to either leg anywhere.
kernel/planner_prompt.go:217
kernel/planner_prompt.go:218 // TreeCallWord reduces the documented bias-tree branches (RenderBiasTree) to
kernel/planner_prompt.go:219 // one word for the label line: branch 1 → long, branch 1 mirror → short,
kernel/planner_prompt.go:220 // branch 3 → close vs PDC, branch 5 premium/discount veto → neutral. Mirrors
kernel/planner_prompt.go:221 // the reconstruction in the live-bias replay P2 (53498adb).
kernel/planner_prompt.go:222 func TreeCallWord(price, pdh, pdl, pdc float64) string {
kernel/planner_prompt.go:223 	if price <= 0 || pdh <= 0 || pdl <= 0 || pdc <= 0 {
kernel/planner_prompt.go:224 		return "neutral"
kernel/planner_prompt.go:225 	}
kernel/planner_prompt.go:226 	if price > pdh {
kernel/planner_prompt.go:227 		return "long"
kernel/planner_prompt.go:228 	}
kernel/planner_prompt.go:229 	if price < pdl {
kernel/planner_prompt.go:230 		return "short"
kernel/planner_prompt.go:231 	}
kernel/planner_prompt.go:232 	if pdh > pdl {
kernel/planner_prompt.go:233 		pos := (price - pdl) / (pdh - pdl)
kernel/planner_prompt.go:234 		call := "neutral"
kernel/planner_prompt.go:235 		switch {
kernel/planner_prompt.go:236 		case price > pdc:
kernel/planner_prompt.go:237 			call = "long"
kernel/planner_prompt.go:238 		case price < pdc:
kernel/planner_prompt.go:239 			call = "short"
kernel/planner_prompt.go:240 		}
kernel/planner_prompt.go:241 		// branch 5: premium (≥50% of the dealing range) disallows longs,
kernel/planner_prompt.go:242 		// discount disallows shorts.
kernel/planner_prompt.go:243 		if pos >= 0.5 && call == "long" {
kernel/planner_prompt.go:244 			return "neutral"
kernel/planner_prompt.go:245 		}
kernel/planner_prompt.go:246 		if pos < 0.5 && call == "short" {
kernel/planner_prompt.go:247 			return "neutral"
kernel/planner_prompt.go:248 		}
kernel/planner_prompt.go:249 		return call
kernel/planner_prompt.go:250 	}
kernel/planner_prompt.go:251 	return "neutral"
kernel/planner_prompt.go:252 }
kernel/planner_prompt.go:253
kernel/planner_prompt.go:254 // RegimeCallWord reduces the regime block to one word for the label line:

kernel/planner_prompt.go:256 // live-bias replay P2 definition).

kernel/planner_prompt.go:263 		strings.EqualFold(strings.TrimSpace(r.Trend1h), "down") {

kernel/planner_prompt.go:282 // FantasyTargetWarnings (autopsy-response wave, 2026-08-27) — advisory WARN,
kernel/planner_prompt.go:283 // never a fail: an armed scenario whose PLANNED R:R exceeds 6 is a
kernel/planner_prompt.go:284 // fantasy-target flag (the 3.28–22.88 R losers in the refusal autopsy).
kernel/planner_prompt.go:285 func FantasyTargetWarnings(doc PlanDoc) []string {
kernel/planner_prompt.go:286 	var out []string
kernel/planner_prompt.go:287 	for _, s := range doc.Scenarios {
kernel/planner_prompt.go:288 		a := s.Arm
kernel/planner_prompt.go:289 		if a == nil || !a.Enabled || a.Entry <= 0 || a.Stop <= 0 || a.Target <= 0 {
kernel/planner_prompt.go:290 			continue
kernel/planner_prompt.go:291 		}
kernel/planner_prompt.go:292 		risk := a.Entry - a.Stop
kernel/planner_prompt.go:293 		reward := a.Target - a.Entry
kernel/planner_prompt.go:294 		if strings.EqualFold(strings.TrimSpace(s.Direction), "short") {
kernel/planner_prompt.go:295 			risk = a.Stop - a.Entry
kernel/planner_prompt.go:296 			reward = a.Entry - a.Target
kernel/planner_prompt.go:297 		}
kernel/planner_prompt.go:298 		if risk > 0 && reward/risk > 6.0 {
kernel/planner_prompt.go:299 			out = append(out, fmt.Sprintf("%s: planned R %.1f (entry %.2f stop %.2f target %.2f) — fantasy-target flag", s.ID, reward/risk, a.Entry, a.Stop, a.Target))
kernel/planner_prompt.go:300 		}
kernel/planner_prompt.go:301 	}
kernel/planner_prompt.go:302 	return out
kernel/planner_prompt.go:303 }
kernel/planner_prompt.go:304
kernel/planner_prompt.go:305 // FvgDemandWarnings (grand-audit response F5, 2026-08-28) — WARN-only demand

kernel/planner_prompt.go:733 		"Every armed scenario at quality A or B SHOULD carry arm{} — enabled:true + EXACT entry/stop/target (breakout_retest stays a normal AI play: the machine never arms it — GAR-F4). CHAINED ARMS: when a sweep_reclaim you believe in confirms, its RETRACE entry should already be resting — author that retrace as its own arm with wait_confirm:true, or add wait_confirm:true to the sweep scenario's arm: the system holds the arm dormant until the scenario's confirm{} is machine-MET, then places it (the sweep fast path). NEVER arm acceptance or a raw sweep WITHOUT the wait_confirm chain. Keep targets REAL: a planned R:R above ~6 is a fantasy target and gets WARN-flagged at write. The system places arms within a tick band, manages them tick-level, and cancels on veto/dormant/session-end. FEASIBILITY CONTRACT: an arm{} MUST be gate-feasible or it is REFUSED every cycle and learns nothing — R:R = |target\u2212entry| \u00f7 |stop\u2212entry| must be \u2265 2.0 (ARM_MIN_RR) AND the stop distance must be \u2265 " + fmt.Sprintf("%.1f", MinSLATRMult()) + "\u00d7 the current 5m ATR (the facts list the session ATR5m — cite the live value; a 10-point stop when ATR5m is ~16 is an instant refuse). WATERFALL ARMS (F1): a breakdown_continue / breakup_continue at quality A or B SHOULD carry arm{} with wait_confirm:true + entry_mode=pullback — the resting limit sits AT the broken level and chains on confirm leg 1, so the pullback-that-fails FILLS it (immediate-mode waterfall plays stay on the AI path). kernel/plan_render.go:204 		rule = "2x5m"
kernel/plan_render.go:205 	}
kernel/plan_render.go:206 	lookback := 3
kernel/plan_render.go:207 	// Activation window (P3.6): only levels within 1.5×dATR are live candidates.
