kernel/plan_render.go:211 		fmt.Fprintf(&b, "(%d level(s) outside the %.1f×dATR activation window — re-arm when price returns)\n", hidden, ActivationWindowK)
kernel/plan_render.go:212 	}
kernel/plan_render.go:213 	fresh := levelFreshnessFn(traderID, symbol) // W11b — persisted cross-session state (nil → none)
kernel/plan_render.go:214 	for _, l := range active {
kernel/plan_render.go:215 		dir := DirAbove
kernel/plan_render.go:216 		if l.Price < price {
kernel/plan_render.go:217 			dir = DirBelow
kernel/plan_render.go:218 		}
kernel/plan_render.go:219 		f := EvaluateLevelFacts(BarsSince(bars, birthMs), l.Price, dir, rule, lookback, now)
kernel/plan_render.go:220 		sweep := "F"
kernel/plan_render.go:221 		if f.Swept {
kernel/plan_render.go:222 			sweep = "T"
kernel/plan_render.go:223 		}

kernel/plan_render.go:230 		// DistancePoints = 0 − level. Distance is a snapshot fact — use the
kernel/plan_render.go:231 		// caller's current price (nonzero; engine_analysis computes it from the
kernel/plan_render.go:232 		// closed bars), falling back to the evaluator value only when it is 0.
kernel/plan_render.go:233 		dist := f.DistancePoints
kernel/plan_render.go:234 		if price > 0 {

kernel/plan_render.go:236 		}

kernel/plan_render.go:238 			l.Price, l.Label, dist, sweep, maxInt(f.ClosesBeyondUp, f.ClosesBeyondDown), f.AcceptHave, f.AcceptNeed, valid)
kernel/plan_render.go:239 		// W11b — append persisted cross-session state so a level burned in an EARLIER

kernel/plan_render.go:250 }
kernel/plan_render.go:251
kernel/plan_render.go:252 // planStateLabel maps a persisted freshness grade to a PLAN-STATUS annotation.
kernel/plan_render.go:253 // "" / A / fresh → "" (no annotation, keeps the fresh line unchanged).
kernel/plan_render.go:254 // P1c design rule: a consumed level ROLE-FLIPS and stays on the map — the label
kernel/plan_render.go:255 // says "flipped", never "burned", because a consumed level is an OPPORTUNITY
kernel/plan_render.go:256 // (the trap S1 trades), not a scar.
kernel/plan_render.go:257 func planStateLabel(grade string) string {
kernel/plan_render.go:258 	switch strings.ToLower(strings.TrimSpace(grade)) {
kernel/plan_render.go:259 	case "", "a", "fresh":
kernel/plan_render.go:260 		return ""
kernel/plan_render.go:261 	case "b":
kernel/plan_render.go:262 		return "state=B(tested)"
kernel/plan_render.go:263 	case "c", "tested":
kernel/plan_render.go:264 		return "state=C(tested×2)"
kernel/plan_render.go:265 	case "done", "consumed":
kernel/plan_render.go:266 		// W-why-no-trades: "watch the trap" made the model AVOID the level — a
kernel/plan_render.go:267 		// consumed level is a role-flipped opportunity, not a hazard to flee.
kernel/plan_render.go:268 		return "flipped(consumed — tradeable both directions)"
kernel/plan_render.go:269 	default:
kernel/plan_render.go:270 		return "state=" + grade
kernel/plan_render.go:271 	}
kernel/plan_render.go:272 }
kernel/plan_render.go:273
kernel/plan_render.go:274 // PlanCitationResult classifies an executor's plan citation (P3.5 advisory).
kernel/plan_render.go:275 type PlanCitationResult struct {
kernel/plan_render.go:276 	Cited   string // the resolved scenario id, or "off-plan"
kernel/plan_render.go:277 	OffPlan bool
kernel/plan_render.go:278 	Matched bool // cited a real scenario whose direction aligns with the action
kernel/plan_render.go:279 }
kernel/plan_render.go:280
kernel/plan_render.go:281 // ClassifyCitation resolves the executor's cited_scenario against the active
kernel/plan_render.go:282 // plan: empty/"off-plan"/unknown-id → off-plan; a known scenario → matched iff
kernel/plan_render.go:283 // the action's direction aligns with the scenario's. Advisory only — it never
kernel/plan_render.go:284 // gates the trade.
kernel/plan_render.go:285 // CitationStructure (B3/F6, fail-register wave) judges the ENTRY against the
kernel/plan_render.go:286 // cited scenario's stated structure: entry inside the scenario anchor's
kernel/plan_render.go:287 // activation band (1.5×dATR), SL on the protective side of the anchor, TP
kernel/plan_render.go:288 // direction-correct toward the target chain. "" (unknown/no anchor) is treated
kernel/plan_render.go:289 // as ok — fail-open, forward-only grading.
kernel/plan_render.go:290 func CitationStructure(action, cited string, doc PlanDoc, entry, sl, tp, dATR float64) string {
kernel/plan_render.go:291 	c := strings.TrimSpace(cited)
kernel/plan_render.go:292 	if c == "" || strings.EqualFold(c, "off-plan") || entry <= 0 || dATR <= 0 {
kernel/plan_render.go:293 		return ""
kernel/plan_render.go:294 	}
kernel/plan_render.go:295 	for _, s := range doc.Scenarios {
kernel/plan_render.go:296 		if !strings.EqualFold(s.ID, c) {
kernel/plan_render.go:297 			continue
kernel/plan_render.go:298 		}
kernel/plan_render.go:299 		anchor, ok := ScenarioAnchor(s, doc.Levels)
kernel/plan_render.go:300 		if !ok {
kernel/plan_render.go:301 			return "" // unevaluable scenario — no structural verdict
kernel/plan_render.go:302 		}
kernel/plan_render.go:303 		if absF(entry-anchor) > ActivationWindowK*dATR {
kernel/plan_render.go:304 			return "off_band"
kernel/plan_render.go:305 		}
kernel/plan_render.go:306 		long := action == "open_long"
kernel/plan_render.go:307 		if sl > 0 {
kernel/plan_render.go:308 			// protective side: a long's stop below the anchor zone, a short's above.
kernel/plan_render.go:309 			if long && sl >= anchor+ActivationWindowK*dATR {
kernel/plan_render.go:310 				return "struct"
kernel/plan_render.go:311 			}
kernel/plan_render.go:312 			if !long && sl <= anchor-ActivationWindowK*dATR {
kernel/plan_render.go:313 				return "struct"
kernel/plan_render.go:314 			}
kernel/plan_render.go:315 		}
kernel/plan_render.go:316 		if tp > 0 && len(s.TargetChain) > 0 {
kernel/plan_render.go:317 			// TP must point the trade's way relative to entry.
kernel/plan_render.go:318 			if long && tp <= entry {
kernel/plan_render.go:319 				return "struct"
kernel/plan_render.go:320 			}
kernel/plan_render.go:321 			if !long && tp >= entry {
kernel/plan_render.go:322 				return "struct"
kernel/plan_render.go:323 			}
kernel/plan_render.go:324 		}
kernel/plan_render.go:325 		return "ok"
kernel/plan_render.go:326 	}
kernel/plan_render.go:327 	return ""
kernel/plan_render.go:328 }
kernel/plan_render.go:329
kernel/plan_render.go:330 func ClassifyCitation(action, cited string, doc PlanDoc) PlanCitationResult {
kernel/plan_render.go:331 	c := strings.TrimSpace(cited)
kernel/plan_render.go:332 	if c == "" || strings.EqualFold(c, "off-plan") || strings.EqualFold(c, "offplan") {
kernel/plan_render.go:333 		return PlanCitationResult{Cited: "off-plan", OffPlan: true}
kernel/plan_render.go:334 	}
kernel/plan_render.go:335 	for _, s := range doc.Scenarios {
kernel/plan_render.go:336 		if strings.EqualFold(s.ID, c) {
kernel/plan_render.go:337 			return PlanCitationResult{Cited: s.ID, Matched: actionMatchesDirection(action, s.Direction)}
kernel/plan_render.go:338 		}
kernel/plan_render.go:339 	}
kernel/plan_render.go:340 	return PlanCitationResult{Cited: "off-plan", OffPlan: true} // unknown id → off-plan
kernel/plan_render.go:341 }
kernel/plan_render.go:342
kernel/plan_render.go:343 func actionMatchesDirection(action, dir string) bool {
kernel/plan_render.go:344 	switch action {
kernel/plan_render.go:345 	case "open_long":
kernel/plan_render.go:346 		return dir == "long"
kernel/plan_render.go:347 	case "open_short":
kernel/plan_render.go:348 		return dir == "short"
kernel/plan_render.go:349 	}
kernel/plan_render.go:350 	return false
kernel/plan_render.go:351 }
kernel/plan_render.go:352

kernel/plan_render.go:355 		return "—"

kernel/plan_render.go:358 	for i, x := range xs {
kernel/plan_render.go:359 		parts[i] = fmt.Sprintf("%.2f", x)
kernel/plan_render.go:360 	}

kernel/svp.go:1 package kernel
kernel/svp.go:2
kernel/svp.go:3 // Session Volume Profile (SVP) engine — Part B.
kernel/svp.go:4 //
kernel/svp.go:5 // Builds a volume-at-price histogram for the CME equity-index RTH session, then
kernel/svp.go:6 // derives the Point of Control (POC) and the 70% Value Area (VAH/VAL) using the
kernel/svp.go:7 // CLASSIC two-row expansion method (locked by a mandatory test vector in
kernel/svp.go:8 // svp_test.go). It is STATELESS: each call recomputes from the 1-minute bars
kernel/svp.go:9 // currently in the cache, which makes the "developing" profile grow bar-by-bar,
kernel/svp.go:10 // reset automatically at the RTH open, and stay correct across a mid-session
kernel/svp.go:11 // restart (a thin re-seed simply raises the `partial` flag).
kernel/svp.go:12 //
kernel/svp.go:13 // Session clock: the RTH window is anchored to the ONE session authority in this
kernel/svp.go:14 // package (cme_calendar.go — isCMEHoliday + weekday), so SVP, the AI gate, and
kernel/svp.go:15 // the guardrails never disagree about what day/session it is.
kernel/svp.go:16
kernel/svp.go:17 import (
kernel/svp.go:18 	"fmt"
kernel/svp.go:19 	"math"
kernel/svp.go:20 	"sort"
kernel/svp.go:21 	"time"
kernel/svp.go:22
kernel/svp.go:23 	"nofx/market"
kernel/svp.go:24 )
kernel/svp.go:25
kernel/svp.go:26 // ---- Constants (single source of truth) ------------------------------------
kernel/svp.go:27
kernel/svp.go:28 const (
kernel/svp.go:29 	// SVPTickSize is the MNQ/ES index-futures tick (points per tick).
kernel/svp.go:30 	SVPTickSize = 0.25
kernel/svp.go:31 	// SVPRowHeightTicks is the profile row granularity in ticks. 5 ticks → each
kernel/svp.go:32 	// price row spans 1.25 points.
kernel/svp.go:33 	SVPRowHeightTicks = 5
kernel/svp.go:34 	// SVPRowHeight is the price height of one histogram row (1.25 points).
kernel/svp.go:35 	SVPRowHeight = SVPTickSize * SVPRowHeightTicks
kernel/svp.go:36 	// SVPValueAreaPercent is the fraction of total session volume enclosed by the
kernel/svp.go:37 	// Value Area (classic 70%).
kernel/svp.go:38 	SVPValueAreaPercent = 0.70
kernel/svp.go:39
kernel/svp.go:40 	// AISVPBarInterval / AISVPBarCount are the bars the AI computes its SVP prompt
kernel/svp.go:41 	// line from (kernel/engine_analysis.go). This is INDEPENDENT of the chart: the
kernel/svp.go:42 	// chart is a display indicator that profiles whatever candle data it is showing
kernel/svp.go:43 	// at its selected timeframe (see api/handler_svp.go), while the AI always
kernel/svp.go:44 	// profiles 1m bars for its own reasoning. Keep this AI value fixed — changing
kernel/svp.go:45 	// it alters the AI prompt (a trading-behavior change).
kernel/svp.go:46 	AISVPBarInterval = "1m"
kernel/svp.go:47 	AISVPBarCount    = 2000
kernel/svp.go:48
kernel/svp.go:49 	// The SVP session is the CME futures trading day: it opens at 17:00 CT and
kernel/svp.go:50 	// RESETS every 17:00 CT (the daily-break roll). This is anchored to
kernel/svp.go:51 	// CMESessionDayStart in cme_calendar.go — NOT the 08:30 RTH open — so the
kernel/svp.go:52 	// profile matches the full futures session the bot actually trades.
kernel/svp.go:53
kernel/svp.go:54 	// svpPartialToleranceMs: if the earliest cached bar starts more than this
kernel/svp.go:55 	// after the session open, we could not see the whole session → partial=true.
kernel/svp.go:56 	svpPartialToleranceMs = 2 * 60 * 1000 // 2 minutes
kernel/svp.go:57 )
kernel/svp.go:58
kernel/svp.go:59 // ---- Output types (also the JSON contract for the chart, Part C) -----------
kernel/svp.go:60
kernel/svp.go:61 // SVPBin is one price row: Price is the row MIDPOINT; Vol its accumulated total
kernel/svp.go:62 // volume; UpVol/DownVol the split by candle direction (close>=open → up, else
kernel/svp.go:63 // down); InVA whether the row is inside the Value Area. Invariant: Vol ==
kernel/svp.go:64 // UpVol + DownVol.
kernel/svp.go:65 type SVPBin struct {
kernel/svp.go:66 	Price   float64 `json:"price"`
kernel/svp.go:67 	Vol     float64 `json:"vol"`     // total volume in the row (= UpVol + DownVol)
kernel/svp.go:68 	UpVol   float64 `json:"upVol"`   // volume from up candles (close>=open)
kernel/svp.go:69 	DownVol float64 `json:"downVol"` // volume from down candles (close<open)
kernel/svp.go:70 	InVA    bool    `json:"inVA"`
kernel/svp.go:71 }
kernel/svp.go:72
kernel/svp.go:73 // SVPSession is a single CME futures trading day's profile. VAH is the TOP edge
kernel/svp.go:74 // of the top VA row, VAL the BOTTOM edge of the bottom VA row, POC the mid of
kernel/svp.go:75 // the max-volume row. Frozen is true once the session has closed (every session
kernel/svp.go:76 // except the current live one). SessionStart is the 17:00 CT session open as a
kernel/svp.go:77 // UTC-seconds timestamp — the chart x-anchor for this session's histogram.
kernel/svp.go:78 type SVPSession struct {
kernel/svp.go:79 	SessionStart int64    `json:"sessionStart"` // UTC SECONDS — chart x-anchor
kernel/svp.go:80 	Date         string   `json:"date"`         // YYYY-MM-DD (session-day start, Chicago)
kernel/svp.go:81 	Partial      bool     `json:"partial"`
kernel/svp.go:82 	POC          float64  `json:"poc"`
kernel/svp.go:83 	VAH          float64  `json:"vah"`
kernel/svp.go:84 	VAL          float64  `json:"val"`
kernel/svp.go:85 	Bins         []SVPBin `json:"bins"`
kernel/svp.go:86 	Frozen       bool     `json:"frozen"`
kernel/svp.go:87 }
kernel/svp.go:88
kernel/svp.go:89 // SVPProfile is the full multi-session profile: one SVPSession per CME futures
kernel/svp.go:90 // trading day present in the bar range, ascending by time (like TradingView's
kernel/svp.go:91 // Session Volume Profile). RowHeight sizes the bars for the renderer. Sessions
kernel/svp.go:92 // is NEVER nil (empty → []SVPSession{}).
kernel/svp.go:93 type SVPProfile struct {
kernel/svp.go:94 	RowHeight float64      `json:"rowHeight"`
kernel/svp.go:95 	Sessions  []SVPSession `json:"sessions"`
kernel/svp.go:96 }
kernel/svp.go:97
kernel/svp.go:98 // FormatSVPLine renders the MOST RECENT session as the single AI-prompt context
kernel/svp.go:99 // line:
kernel/svp.go:100 //
kernel/svp.go:101 //	SVP (today's session, since 17:00 CT open): POC X VAH Y VAL Z (partial)
kernel/svp.go:102 //
kernel/svp.go:103 // The "(partial)" tag appears only when that session could not be seen from its
kernel/svp.go:104 // open. Returns "" when there are no sessions, or the last session has no bars —
kernel/svp.go:105 // the prompt gate then injects nothing (keeping the OFF/insufficient case clean).
kernel/svp.go:106 func FormatSVPLine(p SVPProfile) string {
kernel/svp.go:107 	if len(p.Sessions) == 0 {
kernel/svp.go:108 		return ""
kernel/svp.go:109 	}
kernel/svp.go:110 	last := p.Sessions[len(p.Sessions)-1]
kernel/svp.go:111 	if len(last.Bins) == 0 {
kernel/svp.go:112 		return ""
kernel/svp.go:113 	}
kernel/svp.go:114 	s := fmt.Sprintf("SVP (today's session, since 17:00 CT open): POC %.2f VAH %.2f VAL %.2f",
kernel/svp.go:115 		last.POC, last.VAH, last.VAL)
kernel/svp.go:116 	if last.Partial {
kernel/svp.go:117 		s += " (partial)"
kernel/svp.go:118 	}
kernel/svp.go:119 	return s
kernel/svp.go:120 }
kernel/svp.go:121
kernel/svp.go:122 // ---- Histogram (B1) --------------------------------------------------------
kernel/svp.go:123
kernel/svp.go:124 // svpBinIndex maps a price to its ABSOLUTE-grid row index: floor(price/row).
kernel/svp.go:125 // The grid is anchored at 0, not at the session — so a given price always lands
kernel/svp.go:126 // in the same row regardless of the session, which lets levels line up across
kernel/svp.go:127 // days.
kernel/svp.go:128 func svpBinIndex(price float64) int {
kernel/svp.go:129 	return int(math.Floor(price / SVPRowHeight))
kernel/svp.go:130 }
kernel/svp.go:131
kernel/svp.go:132 // svpAddBar distributes one bar's volume across the rows its [low,high] range
kernel/svp.go:133 // touches, weighted by the fraction of the range that overlaps each row
kernel/svp.go:134 // (uniform-within-bar assumption). A doji (low==high) drops all volume in one
kernel/svp.go:135 // row.
kernel/svp.go:136 func svpAddBar(hist map[int]float64, low, high, vol float64) {
kernel/svp.go:137 	if high < low {
kernel/svp.go:138 		low, high = high, low
kernel/svp.go:139 	}
kernel/svp.go:140 	if high == low {
kernel/svp.go:141 		hist[svpBinIndex(low)] += vol
kernel/svp.go:142 		return
kernel/svp.go:143 	}
kernel/svp.go:144 	span := high - low
kernel/svp.go:145 	loBin := svpBinIndex(low)
kernel/svp.go:146 	hiBin := svpBinIndex(high)
kernel/svp.go:147 	for b := loBin; b <= hiBin; b++ {
kernel/svp.go:148 		binLow := float64(b) * SVPRowHeight
kernel/svp.go:149 		binHigh := binLow + SVPRowHeight
kernel/svp.go:150 		overlap := math.Min(high, binHigh) - math.Max(low, binLow)
kernel/svp.go:151 		if overlap <= 0 {
kernel/svp.go:152 			continue
kernel/svp.go:153 		}
kernel/svp.go:154 		hist[b] += vol * (overlap / span)
kernel/svp.go:155 	}
kernel/svp.go:156 }
kernel/svp.go:157
kernel/svp.go:158 // svpDirBins holds the per-row up/down volume split for one session.
kernel/svp.go:159 type svpDirBins struct {
kernel/svp.go:160 	up   map[int]float64
kernel/svp.go:161 	down map[int]float64
kernel/svp.go:162 }
kernel/svp.go:163
kernel/svp.go:164 func newSvpDirBins() *svpDirBins {
kernel/svp.go:165 	return &svpDirBins{up: map[int]float64{}, down: map[int]float64{}}
kernel/svp.go:166 }
kernel/svp.go:167
kernel/svp.go:168 // addBar distributes one bar's volume into the UP or DOWN histogram (by candle
kernel/svp.go:169 // direction: close>=open → up, else down) using the same uniform row-overlap
kernel/svp.go:170 // logic as svpAddBar.
kernel/svp.go:171 func (s *svpDirBins) addBar(b market.Kline) {
kernel/svp.go:172 	hist := s.up
kernel/svp.go:173 	if b.Close < b.Open {
kernel/svp.go:174 		hist = s.down
kernel/svp.go:175 	}
kernel/svp.go:176 	svpAddBar(hist, b.Low, b.High, b.Volume)
kernel/svp.go:177 }
kernel/svp.go:178
kernel/svp.go:179 // totals returns the combined UP+DOWN volume per occupied row — the input to
kernel/svp.go:180 // svpValueArea (POC/VA are computed on TOTAL volume).
kernel/svp.go:181 func (s *svpDirBins) totals() map[int]float64 {
kernel/svp.go:182 	tot := make(map[int]float64, len(s.up)+len(s.down))
kernel/svp.go:183 	for b, v := range s.up {
kernel/svp.go:184 		tot[b] += v
kernel/svp.go:185 	}
kernel/svp.go:186 	for b, v := range s.down {
kernel/svp.go:187 		tot[b] += v
kernel/svp.go:188 	}
kernel/svp.go:189 	return tot
kernel/svp.go:190 }
kernel/svp.go:191
kernel/svp.go:192 // ---- POC + Value Area (B2, classic two-row) --------------------------------
kernel/svp.go:193
kernel/svp.go:194 // svpValueArea computes the POC row and the Value-Area row span [vaLow,vaHigh]
kernel/svp.go:195 // (inclusive, absolute row indices) using the CLASSIC two-row method:
kernel/svp.go:196 //
kernel/svp.go:197 //	POC   = the max-volume row (tie → nearest to the middle of the occupied
kernel/svp.go:198 //	        range, then the LOWER row).
kernel/svp.go:199 //	VA    = seed with the POC's volume; each step compare the SUM of the TWO
kernel/svp.go:200 //	        rows ABOVE the current VA against the SUM of the TWO rows BELOW, and
kernel/svp.go:201 //	        annex the larger PAIR as a unit (equal → expand UP). If a side is
kernel/svp.go:202 //	        exhausted, take the other. Stop once VA volume ≥ 70% of the total.
kernel/svp.go:203 //
kernel/svp.go:204 // Annexing the pair as a UNIT (checking the target only AFTER both rows are
kernel/svp.go:205 // added) is what separates this from the greedy one-row method — the mandatory
kernel/svp.go:206 // test vector fails greedy and passes only this.
kernel/svp.go:207 func svpValueArea(hist map[int]float64, vaPercent float64) (poc, vaLow, vaHigh int, vaVol, total float64) {
kernel/svp.go:208 	if len(hist) == 0 {
kernel/svp.go:209 		return 0, 0, 0, 0, 0
kernel/svp.go:210 	}
kernel/svp.go:211 	minB, maxB := math.MaxInt, math.MinInt
kernel/svp.go:212 	for b, v := range hist {
kernel/svp.go:213 		total += v
kernel/svp.go:214 		if b < minB {
kernel/svp.go:215 			minB = b
kernel/svp.go:216 		}
kernel/svp.go:217 		if b > maxB {
kernel/svp.go:218 			maxB = b
kernel/svp.go:219 		}
kernel/svp.go:220 	}
kernel/svp.go:221
kernel/svp.go:222 	// POC: max-volume row; tie broken toward the middle of the occupied range,
kernel/svp.go:223 	// then toward the LOWER row. Ascending iteration + strict "<" makes the lower
kernel/svp.go:224 	// row win any equidistant tie.
kernel/svp.go:225 	maxVol := math.Inf(-1)
kernel/svp.go:226 	for _, v := range hist {
kernel/svp.go:227 		if v > maxVol {
kernel/svp.go:228 			maxVol = v
kernel/svp.go:229 		}
kernel/svp.go:230 	}
kernel/svp.go:231 	mid := float64(minB+maxB) / 2.0
kernel/svp.go:232 	poc = minB
kernel/svp.go:233 	bestDist := math.Inf(1)
kernel/svp.go:234 	for b := minB; b <= maxB; b++ {
kernel/svp.go:235 		if hist[b] != maxVol {
kernel/svp.go:236 			continue
kernel/svp.go:237 		}
kernel/svp.go:238 		if d := math.Abs(float64(b) - mid); d < bestDist {
kernel/svp.go:239 			bestDist = d
kernel/svp.go:240 			poc = b
kernel/svp.go:241 		}
kernel/svp.go:242 	}
kernel/svp.go:243
kernel/svp.go:244 	// Value area expansion.
kernel/svp.go:245 	vaLow, vaHigh = poc, poc
kernel/svp.go:246 	vaVol = hist[poc]
kernel/svp.go:247 	target := total * vaPercent
kernel/svp.go:248 	for vaVol < target {
kernel/svp.go:249 		aboveAvail := vaHigh+1 <= maxB
kernel/svp.go:250 		belowAvail := vaLow-1 >= minB
kernel/svp.go:251 		if !aboveAvail && !belowAvail {
kernel/svp.go:252 			break
kernel/svp.go:253 		}
kernel/svp.go:254 		above := hist[vaHigh+1] + hist[vaHigh+2] // absent rows read as 0
kernel/svp.go:255 		below := hist[vaLow-1] + hist[vaLow-2]
kernel/svp.go:256
kernel/svp.go:257 		takeAbove := false
kernel/svp.go:258 		switch {
kernel/svp.go:259 		case !belowAvail:
kernel/svp.go:260 			takeAbove = true
kernel/svp.go:261 		case !aboveAvail:
kernel/svp.go:262 			takeAbove = false
kernel/svp.go:263 		case above > below:
kernel/svp.go:264 			takeAbove = true
kernel/svp.go:265 		case below > above:
kernel/svp.go:266 			takeAbove = false
kernel/svp.go:267 		default:
kernel/svp.go:268 			takeAbove = true // equal → expand UP (documented)
kernel/svp.go:269 		}
kernel/svp.go:270
kernel/svp.go:271 		// Annex the whole pair (up to two rows) on the chosen side, THEN check
kernel/svp.go:272 		// the target — never between the two rows of the pair.
kernel/svp.go:273 		if takeAbove {
kernel/svp.go:274 			vaHigh++
kernel/svp.go:275 			vaVol += hist[vaHigh]
kernel/svp.go:276 			if vaHigh+1 <= maxB {
kernel/svp.go:277 				vaHigh++
kernel/svp.go:278 				vaVol += hist[vaHigh]
kernel/svp.go:279 			}
kernel/svp.go:280 		} else {
kernel/svp.go:281 			vaLow--
kernel/svp.go:282 			vaVol += hist[vaLow]
kernel/svp.go:283 			if vaLow-1 >= minB {
kernel/svp.go:284 				vaLow--
kernel/svp.go:285 				vaVol += hist[vaLow]
kernel/svp.go:286 			}
kernel/svp.go:287 		}
kernel/svp.go:288 	}
kernel/svp.go:289 	return poc, vaLow, vaHigh, vaVol, total
kernel/svp.go:290 }
kernel/svp.go:291
kernel/svp.go:292 // ---- Assembly (anchored to the CME session-day, cme_calendar.go) -----------
kernel/svp.go:293
kernel/svp.go:294 // BuildSVPProfile computes a MULTI-SESSION Session Volume Profile — one
kernel/svp.go:295 // SVPSession per CME futures trading day present in the supplied 1-minute bars
kernel/svp.go:296 // (CLOSED bars only). Bars are grouped by their CME session-day (the most-recent
kernel/svp.go:297 // 17:00 CT boundary, via CMESessionDayStart): two bars share a session iff their
kernel/svp.go:298 // CMESessionDayStart is equal, so each futures day (17:00 CT → 16:00 CT next
kernel/svp.go:299 // day, overnight included) is its own profile — matching TradingView's SVP.
kernel/svp.go:300 //
kernel/svp.go:301 // The current live session (the one containing `now` while IsCMEOpen(now)) is
kernel/svp.go:302 // Frozen=false; every other session is Frozen=true. Sessions are returned
kernel/svp.go:303 // ascending by SessionStart and the slice is NEVER nil.
kernel/svp.go:304 func BuildSVPProfile(bars []market.Kline, now time.Time) SVPProfile {
kernel/svp.go:305 	nowMs := now.UnixMilli()
kernel/svp.go:306
kernel/svp.go:307 	// The live session's anchor (only meaningful while the market is open): the
kernel/svp.go:308 	// session containing `now` is the developing one.
kernel/svp.go:309 	var liveStartMs int64 = math.MinInt64
kernel/svp.go:310 	if IsCMEOpen(now) {
kernel/svp.go:311 		liveStartMs = CMESessionDayStart(now).UnixMilli()
kernel/svp.go:312 	}
kernel/svp.go:313
kernel/svp.go:314 	// Group CLOSED bars by session-day-start (ms).
kernel/svp.go:315 	type sessAgg struct {
kernel/svp.go:316 		start     time.Time
kernel/svp.go:317 		startMs   int64
kernel/svp.go:318 		firstOpen int64
kernel/svp.go:319 		dir       *svpDirBins
kernel/svp.go:320 	}
kernel/svp.go:321 	groups := make(map[int64]*sessAgg)
kernel/svp.go:322 	for i := range bars {
kernel/svp.go:323 		b := bars[i]
kernel/svp.go:324 		if b.CloseTime >= nowMs {
kernel/svp.go:325 			continue // in-progress / future bar (only CLOSED bars count)
kernel/svp.go:326 		}
kernel/svp.go:327 		start := CMESessionDayStart(time.UnixMilli(b.OpenTime))
kernel/svp.go:328 		startMs := start.UnixMilli()
kernel/svp.go:329 		g := groups[startMs]
kernel/svp.go:330 		if g == nil {
kernel/svp.go:331 			g = &sessAgg{start: start, startMs: startMs, firstOpen: b.OpenTime, dir: newSvpDirBins()}
kernel/svp.go:332 			groups[startMs] = g
kernel/svp.go:333 		} else if b.OpenTime < g.firstOpen {
kernel/svp.go:334 			g.firstOpen = b.OpenTime
kernel/svp.go:335 		}
kernel/svp.go:336 		g.dir.addBar(b)
kernel/svp.go:337 	}
kernel/svp.go:338
kernel/svp.go:339 	sessions := make([]SVPSession, 0, len(groups))
kernel/svp.go:340 	for _, g := range groups {
kernel/svp.go:341 		sessions = append(sessions, svpFinalizeSession(g.start, g.startMs, g.firstOpen, g.dir, g.startMs == liveStartMs))
kernel/svp.go:342 	}
kernel/svp.go:343 	sort.Slice(sessions, func(i, j int) bool {
kernel/svp.go:344 		return sessions[i].SessionStart < sessions[j].SessionStart
kernel/svp.go:345 	})
kernel/svp.go:346
kernel/svp.go:347 	return SVPProfile{
kernel/svp.go:348 		RowHeight: SVPRowHeight,
kernel/svp.go:349 		Sessions:  sessions,
kernel/svp.go:350 	}
kernel/svp.go:351 }
kernel/svp.go:352
kernel/svp.go:353 // svpFinalizeSession turns one session's accumulated up/down histogram into an
kernel/svp.go:354 // SVPSession: POC/VA from svpValueArea on the TOTAL volume, then per-row bins
kernel/svp.go:355 // carrying the up/down split. `live` marks the current developing session
kernel/svp.go:356 // (Frozen=false); every other session is frozen.
kernel/svp.go:357 func svpFinalizeSession(sessStart time.Time, startMs, firstOpen int64, dir *svpDirBins, live bool) SVPSession {
kernel/svp.go:358 	sess := SVPSession{
kernel/svp.go:359 		SessionStart: sessStart.Unix(),
kernel/svp.go:360 		Date:         sessStart.Format("2006-01-02"),
kernel/svp.go:361 		Frozen:       !live,
kernel/svp.go:362 		// partial: we could not see the session from its 17:00 CT open.
kernel/svp.go:363 		Partial: firstOpen > startMs+svpPartialToleranceMs,
kernel/svp.go:364 		Bins:    []SVPBin{},
kernel/svp.go:365 	}
kernel/svp.go:366
kernel/svp.go:367 	totals := dir.totals()
kernel/svp.go:368 	poc, vaLow, vaHigh, _, _ := svpValueArea(totals, SVPValueAreaPercent)
kernel/svp.go:369 	sess.POC = (float64(poc) + 0.5) * SVPRowHeight // row midpoint
kernel/svp.go:370 	sess.VAH = float64(vaHigh+1) * SVPRowHeight    // top edge of top VA row
kernel/svp.go:371 	sess.VAL = float64(vaLow) * SVPRowHeight       // bottom edge of bottom VA row
kernel/svp.go:372
kernel/svp.go:373 	// Emit occupied rows ascending by price, tagged inVA, with the up/down split.
kernel/svp.go:374 	idx := make([]int, 0, len(totals))
kernel/svp.go:375 	for b := range totals {
kernel/svp.go:376 		idx = append(idx, b)
kernel/svp.go:377 	}
kernel/svp.go:378 	sort.Ints(idx)
kernel/svp.go:379 	sess.Bins = make([]SVPBin, 0, len(idx))
kernel/svp.go:380 	for _, b := range idx {
kernel/svp.go:381 		up := dir.up[b]
kernel/svp.go:382 		down := dir.down[b]
kernel/svp.go:383 		sess.Bins = append(sess.Bins, SVPBin{
kernel/svp.go:384 			Price:   (float64(b) + 0.5) * SVPRowHeight,
kernel/svp.go:385 			Vol:     up + down,
kernel/svp.go:386 			UpVol:   up,
kernel/svp.go:387 			DownVol: down,
kernel/svp.go:388 			InVA:    b >= vaLow && b <= vaHigh,
kernel/svp.go:389 		})
kernel/svp.go:390 	}
kernel/svp.go:391 	return sess
kernel/svp.go:392 }

trader/auto_trader_planner.go:190 func (at *AutoTrader) maybeRunSessionReadsAt(now time.Time) []SessionReadFired {
trader/auto_trader_planner.go:191 	if !at.dayPlanEnabled() || at.store == nil {

trader/auto_trader_planner.go:193 	}
trader/auto_trader_planner.go:194 	reg := at.sessionRegistry(now) // W8 — admin registry from system_config (fallback default)
trader/auto_trader_planner.go:195 	fired := make([]SessionReadFired, 0, 1)
trader/auto_trader_planner.go:196 	for i := range reg.Sessions {
trader/auto_trader_planner.go:197 		s := &reg.Sessions[i]
trader/auto_trader_planner.go:198 		// W1 — fire the read ONLY inside this session's own read window. The
trader/auto_trader_planner.go:199 		// market-open test is made against the SESSION INSTANCE'S OPEN (P0-B):
trader/auto_trader_planner.go:200 		// the ASIA read is designed for 16:30 CT (owner ruling 2026-08-31), inside

trader/auto_trader_planner.go:358 //

trader/auto_trader_planner.go:361 // report. It is deliberately NOT attempted here: getting it wrong would move an
trader/auto_trader_planner.go:362 // owner's edit onto a DIFFERENT level, which is worse than dropping it.
trader/auto_trader_planner.go:363 //

trader/auto_trader_planner.go:367 // edit is the one at risk, and this is what will tell them.

trader/auto_trader_planner.go:645 // warnFlipDeathSanity (P0.4-F, 2026-08-25) — advisory checks at plan write:

trader/auto_trader_planner.go:647 // (death preempts it), flip==death degeneracy, or a flip/death anchored on a
trader/auto_trader_planner.go:648 // level absent from the plan's own list. These are WARN-only by design — the

trader/auto_trader_planner.go:651 func (at *AutoTrader) warnFlipDeathSanity(d *kernel.PlanDoc) {
trader/auto_trader_planner.go:652 	if d == nil {
trader/auto_trader_planner.go:653 		return
trader/auto_trader_planner.go:654 	}
trader/auto_trader_planner.go:655 	levelPrice := func(p float64) bool {
trader/auto_trader_planner.go:656 		for _, l := range d.Levels {
trader/auto_trader_planner.go:657 			if math.Abs(l.Price-p) <= 3.0 {
trader/auto_trader_planner.go:658 				return true
trader/auto_trader_planner.go:659 			}
trader/auto_trader_planner.go:660 		}
trader/auto_trader_planner.go:661 		return false
trader/auto_trader_planner.go:662 	}
trader/auto_trader_planner.go:663 	if d.DeathStructured != nil && d.DeathStructured.Price > 0 && !levelPrice(d.DeathStructured.Price) {
trader/auto_trader_planner.go:664 		at.logWarnf("⚠️ flip/death sanity: death{price %.2f} matches NO level in this plan's list (orphan anchor).", d.DeathStructured.Price)

trader/auto_trader_planner.go:666 	if d.FlipStructured != nil && d.FlipStructured.Price > 0 && !levelPrice(d.FlipStructured.Price) {
trader/auto_trader_planner.go:667 		at.logWarnf("⚠️ flip/death sanity: flip{price %.2f} matches NO level in this plan's list (orphan anchor).", d.FlipStructured.Price)
trader/auto_trader_planner.go:668 	}
trader/auto_trader_planner.go:669 	if d.DeathStructured != nil && d.FlipStructured != nil && d.DeathStructured.Price > 0 && d.FlipStructured.Price > 0 {
trader/auto_trader_planner.go:670 		dd, ff := d.DeathStructured, d.FlipStructured
trader/auto_trader_planner.go:671 		if math.Abs(dd.Price-ff.Price) <= 0.01 && dd.Side == ff.Side && dd.Rule == ff.Rule {
trader/auto_trader_planner.go:672 			at.logWarnf("⚠️ flip/death sanity: flip == death (%.2f %s %s) — flip_to fires at the same tick the plan dies; void.", ff.Price, ff.Side, ff.Rule)
trader/auto_trader_planner.go:673 		}
trader/auto_trader_planner.go:674 		// death preempts flip when death sits closer in the same direction
trader/auto_trader_planner.go:675 		// with an easier/equal rule.
trader/auto_trader_planner.go:676 		easier := func(rule string) int {
trader/auto_trader_planner.go:677 			switch rule {
trader/auto_trader_planner.go:678 			case "15m_close":
trader/auto_trader_planner.go:679 				return 1
trader/auto_trader_planner.go:680 			case "5m_close":
trader/auto_trader_planner.go:681 				return 2
trader/auto_trader_planner.go:682 			default: // 2x5m
trader/auto_trader_planner.go:683 				return 3
trader/auto_trader_planner.go:684 			}
trader/auto_trader_planner.go:685 		}
trader/auto_trader_planner.go:686 		if dd.Side == ff.Side && easier(dd.Rule) <= easier(ff.Rule) {
trader/auto_trader_planner.go:687 			up := dd.Side == "above"
trader/auto_trader_planner.go:688 			if (up && dd.Price <= ff.Price) || (!up && dd.Price >= ff.Price) {
trader/auto_trader_planner.go:689 				at.logWarnf("⚠️ flip/death sanity: death (%.2f %s %s) preempts flip (%.2f %s %s) — flip likely unreachable.", dd.Price, dd.Side, dd.Rule, ff.Price, ff.Side, ff.Rule)
trader/auto_trader_planner.go:690 			}
trader/auto_trader_planner.go:691 		}
trader/auto_trader_planner.go:692 	}
trader/auto_trader_planner.go:693 }

api/handler_plan.go:248 // resolveRequestedSession applies an OPTIONAL ?session=NY|ASIA|LONDON to the
api/handler_plan.go:249 // plan lookup. W15.B — the card's session tabs used to be pure highlighting:
api/handler_plan.go:250 // clicking ASIA moved the chip and NOTHING else, because the request had no
api/handler_plan.go:251 // session dimension and always returned the ACTIVE session's plan. With the

api/handler_plan.go:254 // Absent or unrecognized → the live session, i.e. unchanged behavior. The

api/handler_plan.go:257 func resolveRequestedSession(reg kernel.SessionRegistry, want, activeName string, active *kernel.SessionDef) (string, *kernel.SessionDef, bool) {
api/handler_plan.go:258 	want = strings.ToUpper(strings.TrimSpace(want))
api/handler_plan.go:259 	if want == "" {
api/handler_plan.go:260 		return activeName, active, false
api/handler_plan.go:261 	}
api/handler_plan.go:262 	def, found := reg.SessionByName(want)
api/handler_plan.go:263 	if !found {
api/handler_plan.go:264 		return activeName, active, false
api/handler_plan.go:265 	}
api/handler_plan.go:266 	return def.Name, def, true
api/handler_plan.go:267 }

api/handler_plan.go:271
api/handler_plan.go:272 // planReadingFields derives the card's reading semantics FROM THE STORE (F7,
api/handler_plan.go:273 // 2026-08-30). The raw in-flight claim is only "the planner is writing" while

api/handler_plan.go:284 	}

api/handler_plan.go:536 // planLevelFacts computes per-level live facts from the latest 1m bars. Also
api/handler_plan.go:537 // returns the per-scenario fvg_entry live states (FVG ENTRY MODEL, 2026-08-26).
api/handler_plan.go:538 func planLevelFacts(traderID, symbol string, doc kernel.PlanDoc, now time.Time, rule string, owners map[int64]*store.OwnerLevelDB, birthMs int64) ([]gin.H, float64, []kernel.FvgScenarioState) {
api/handler_plan.go:539 	if market.FuturesBarsProvider == nil {
api/handler_plan.go:540 		return nil, 0, nil
api/handler_plan.go:541 	}
api/handler_plan.go:542 	bars := market.FuturesBarsProvider(symbol, "1m", kernel.AISVPBarCount)
api/handler_plan.go:543 	if len(bars) == 0 {
api/handler_plan.go:544 		return nil, 0, nil
api/handler_plan.go:545 	}
api/handler_plan.go:546 	nowMs := now.UnixMilli()
api/handler_plan.go:547 	price := 0.0
api/handler_plan.go:548 	for i := len(bars) - 1; i >= 0; i-- {
api/handler_plan.go:549 		if bars[i].CloseTime < nowMs {
api/handler_plan.go:550 			price = bars[i].Close
api/handler_plan.go:551 			break
api/handler_plan.go:552 		}
api/handler_plan.go:553 	}
api/handler_plan.go:554 	// Zero-reference guard (2026-08-24): right after a plan write, the only
api/handler_plan.go:555 	// bars may be forming ones, leaving price at 0 and every distance rendered
api/handler_plan.go:556 	// as the negative of the level price. Fall back to the latest bar close.
api/handler_plan.go:557 	if price == 0 && len(bars) > 0 {
api/handler_plan.go:558 		price = bars[len(bars)-1].Close
api/handler_plan.go:559 	}
api/handler_plan.go:560 	out := make([]gin.H, 0, len(doc.Levels))
api/handler_plan.go:561 	for _, l := range doc.Levels {
api/handler_plan.go:562 		dir := kernel.DirAbove
api/handler_plan.go:563 		if l.Price < price {
api/handler_plan.go:564 			dir = kernel.DirBelow
api/handler_plan.go:565 		}
api/handler_plan.go:566 		f := kernel.EvaluateLevelFacts(kernel.BarsSince(bars, birthMs), l.Price, dir, rule, 3, nowMs)
api/handler_plan.go:567 		// Zero-reference regression guard (2026-08-26, "dist wrong again"): right
api/handler_plan.go:568 		// after a re-plan, birthMs ≈ now → BarsSince is EMPTY → referenceClose
api/handler_plan.go:569 		// returns 0 → DistancePoints = 0 − level (the card showed −29095 etc.).
api/handler_plan.go:570 		// Distance is a SNAPSHOT fact — compute it against the zero-guarded
api/handler_plan.go:571 		// current price directly; the sweep/acceptance fields keep the
api/handler_plan.go:572 		// birth-scoped bars.
api/handler_plan.go:573 		distance := f.DistancePoints
api/handler_plan.go:574 		if price > 0 {

api/handler_plan.go:576 		}
api/handler_plan.go:577 		row := gin.H{

api/handler_plan.go:592 		}
api/handler_plan.go:593 		// OWNER PROVENANCE: the card renders 👤 / 📝 / an S-tag and the ⚡ conflict
api/handler_plan.go:594 		// chip from these three fields. They were typed as optional on the FE from
api/handler_plan.go:595 		// the start but never emitted, so the whole owner-attribution layer was
api/handler_plan.go:596 		// unreachable (design-conformance audit, D1/D6). Matched by price against
api/handler_plan.go:597 		// the sticky owner-level store, which is where note + scenario tag live.

api/handler_plan.go:608 		}

api/handler_plan.go:610 	}

api/handler_plan.go:712 // planDocDiff describes, in plain language, what changed between two plan
api/handler_plan.go:713 // versions. It is deliberately coarse — the point is for the owner to see at a
api/handler_plan.go:714 // glance WHY a re-plan happened, not to reconstruct a patch.
api/handler_plan.go:715 func planDocDiff(from, to *kernel.PlanDoc) []string {
api/handler_plan.go:716 	if from == nil || to == nil {
api/handler_plan.go:717 		return nil
api/handler_plan.go:718 	}
api/handler_plan.go:719 	var out []string
api/handler_plan.go:720 	if from.Bias.Direction != to.Bias.Direction {
api/handler_plan.go:721 		out = append(out, fmt.Sprintf("bias %s → %s", from.Bias.Direction, to.Bias.Direction))
api/handler_plan.go:722 	}
api/handler_plan.go:723 	if from.Bias.Conviction != to.Bias.Conviction {
api/handler_plan.go:724 		out = append(out, fmt.Sprintf("conviction %s → %s", from.Bias.Conviction, to.Bias.Conviction))
api/handler_plan.go:725 	}
api/handler_plan.go:726 	if from.DayType != to.DayType && to.DayType != "" {
api/handler_plan.go:727 		out = append(out, fmt.Sprintf("day type %s → %s", orNone(from.DayType), to.DayType))
api/handler_plan.go:728 	}
api/handler_plan.go:729
api/handler_plan.go:730 	had := map[int64]string{}
api/handler_plan.go:731 	for _, l := range from.Levels {
api/handler_plan.go:732 		had[roundKey(l.Price)] = l.Label
api/handler_plan.go:733 	}
api/handler_plan.go:734 	kept, added := 0, []string{}
api/handler_plan.go:735 	for _, l := range to.Levels {
api/handler_plan.go:736 		if _, ok := had[roundKey(l.Price)]; ok {
api/handler_plan.go:737 			kept++
api/handler_plan.go:738 			delete(had, roundKey(l.Price))
api/handler_plan.go:739 			continue
api/handler_plan.go:740 		}
api/handler_plan.go:741 		added = append(added, fmt.Sprintf("%s %g", l.Label, l.Price))
api/handler_plan.go:742 	}
api/handler_plan.go:743 	if len(added) > 0 {
api/handler_plan.go:744 		out = append(out, "added "+strings.Join(added, ", "))
api/handler_plan.go:745 	}
api/handler_plan.go:746 	if len(had) > 0 {
api/handler_plan.go:747 		dropped := make([]string, 0, len(had))
api/handler_plan.go:748 		for k, label := range had {
api/handler_plan.go:749 			dropped = append(dropped, fmt.Sprintf("%s %g", label, float64(k)/4))
api/handler_plan.go:750 		}
api/handler_plan.go:751 		sort.Strings(dropped)
api/handler_plan.go:752 		out = append(out, "dropped "+strings.Join(dropped, ", "))
api/handler_plan.go:753 	}
api/handler_plan.go:754 	if len(added) == 0 && len(had) == 0 && len(from.Levels) > 0 {
api/handler_plan.go:755 		out = append(out, fmt.Sprintf("same %d levels", kept))
api/handler_plan.go:756 	}
api/handler_plan.go:757 	if len(to.Levels) == 0 && len(from.Levels) > 0 {
api/handler_plan.go:758 		out = append(out, "NO LEVELS in the replacement")
api/handler_plan.go:759 	}
api/handler_plan.go:760 	if len(from.Scenarios) != len(to.Scenarios) {
api/handler_plan.go:761 		out = append(out, fmt.Sprintf("scenarios %d → %d", len(from.Scenarios), len(to.Scenarios)))
api/handler_plan.go:762 	}
api/handler_plan.go:763 	return out
api/handler_plan.go:764 }

api/handler_plan.go:768 		return "(none)"

api/handler_plan.go:771 }

api/handler_plan.go:847 // marketRef returns the last CLOSED price + the daily-ATR proxy from the live 1m
api/handler_plan.go:848 // bars (the B2 armor scale for owner-entered level prices). Both 0 when no bars.
api/handler_plan.go:849 func marketRef(symbol string, now time.Time) (lastPrice, dATR float64) {
api/handler_plan.go:850 	if market.FuturesBarsProvider == nil {
api/handler_plan.go:851 		return 0, 0
api/handler_plan.go:852 	}
api/handler_plan.go:853 	bars := market.FuturesBarsProvider(symbol, "1m", kernel.AISVPBarCount)
api/handler_plan.go:854 	if len(bars) == 0 {
api/handler_plan.go:855 		return 0, 0
api/handler_plan.go:856 	}
api/handler_plan.go:857 	nowMs := now.UnixMilli()
api/handler_plan.go:858 	for i := len(bars) - 1; i >= 0; i-- {
api/handler_plan.go:859 		if bars[i].CloseTime < nowMs {
api/handler_plan.go:860 			lastPrice = bars[i].Close
api/handler_plan.go:861 			break
api/handler_plan.go:862 		}
api/handler_plan.go:863 	}
api/handler_plan.go:864 	dATR = kernel.DailyRangeProxy(bars, now)
api/handler_plan.go:865 	return lastPrice, dATR
api/handler_plan.go:866 }
api/handler_plan.go:867
api/handler_plan.go:868 // mergedPriceViolations runs the B2 level armor over every price in the RESOLVED
api/handler_plan.go:869 // plan_final (level prices + scenario target-chain prices), so an implausible
api/handler_plan.go:870 // owner/planner price is rejected regardless of which patch shape introduced it
api/handler_plan.go:871 // (a whole-array /levels replace, a target_chain add, etc.) — armoring the result,
api/handler_plan.go:872 // not the patch text.
api/handler_plan.go:873 func mergedPriceViolations(doc *kernel.PlanDoc, lastPrice, dATR float64) []string {
api/handler_plan.go:874 	var out []string
api/handler_plan.go:875 	check := func(p float64) {
api/handler_plan.go:876 		if reason, bad := kernel.LevelPriceViolation(p, lastPrice, dATR); bad {
api/handler_plan.go:877 			out = append(out, reason)
api/handler_plan.go:878 		}
api/handler_plan.go:879 	}
api/handler_plan.go:880 	for _, l := range doc.Levels {
api/handler_plan.go:881 		check(l.Price)
api/handler_plan.go:882 	}
api/handler_plan.go:883 	for _, s := range doc.Scenarios {
api/handler_plan.go:884 		for _, t := range s.TargetChain {
api/handler_plan.go:885 			check(t)
api/handler_plan.go:886 		}
api/handler_plan.go:887 	}
api/handler_plan.go:888 	return out
api/handler_plan.go:889 }

api/handler_plan.go:891 // handlePlanOverlay POST /api/plan/overlay — append an RFC-6902 overlay to the
api/handler_plan.go:892 // active plan. test-op concurrency (§42): the patch is applied strictly to the
api/handler_plan.go:893 // current plan_final; a failed `test` or bad op → 409. Owner prices pass B2 armor.
api/handler_plan.go:894 // Origin defaults to "owner"; the Ask-Planner Apply passes "planner-revised".
api/handler_plan.go:895 func (s *Server) handlePlanOverlay(c *gin.Context) {

api/handler_plan.go:899 		Patch    string `json:"patch"`  // JSON array of RFC-6902 ops
api/handler_plan.go:900 		Origin   string `json:"origin"` // owner | planner-revised (default owner)
api/handler_plan.go:901 	}
api/handler_plan.go:902 	_ = c.ShouldBindJSON(&body)
api/handler_plan.go:903 	traderID := strings.TrimSpace(c.Query("trader_id"))

api/handler_plan.go:1261 // ── ITEM 2 — Ask-Planner context resolution ────────────────────────────────
api/handler_plan.go:1262 //
api/handler_plan.go:1263 // The thread used to be hard-gated on an ACTIVE plan (400 on night/disabled,
api/handler_plan.go:1264 // 404 when no row existed), which locked it at exactly the moment it is most
api/handler_plan.go:1265 // useful. It now degrades through three clearly-labelled contexts instead.
api/handler_plan.go:1266
api/handler_plan.go:1267 const (
api/handler_plan.go:1268 	askContextActive     = "active"     // a live plan for the live session
api/handler_plan.go:1269 	askContextHistorical = "historical" // the most recent stored plan, possibly dead
api/handler_plan.go:1270 	askContextNoPlan     = "no-plan"    // nothing stored — market facts only

api/handler_plan.go:1284
api/handler_plan.go:1285 // resolveAskContext never fails: worst case it returns a no-plan context carrying
api/handler_plan.go:1286 // only live market facts, which is still a useful thing to ask about.
api/handler_plan.go:1287 func (s *Server) resolveAskContext(traderID, symbol string, now time.Time) askContext {
api/handler_plan.go:1288 	reg := s.planRegistry()
api/handler_plan.go:1289 	ctx := askContext{kind: askContextNoPlan, tradeDate: now.In(planChicago()).Format("2006-01-02")}
api/handler_plan.go:1290
api/handler_plan.go:1291 	// 1) The live session's plan, if there is one.
api/handler_plan.go:1292 	// P1 — resolve enablement through the trader's sessionRunnable; the raw
api/handler_plan.go:1293 	// registry flag used to demote a strategy-enabled session to 'historical'.
api/handler_plan.go:1294 	if sess, ok := reg.ActiveSession(now); ok {
api/handler_plan.go:1295 		runnable := true
api/handler_plan.go:1296 		if s.traderManager != nil {
api/handler_plan.go:1297 			if at, aErr := s.traderManager.GetTrader(traderID); aErr == nil && at != nil {
api/handler_plan.go:1298 				if okR, _ := at.SessionRunnable(sess); !okR {
api/handler_plan.go:1299 					runnable = false
api/handler_plan.go:1300 				}
api/handler_plan.go:1301 			}
api/handler_plan.go:1302 		}
api/handler_plan.go:1303 		if !runnable {
api/handler_plan.go:1304 			return ctx
api/handler_plan.go:1305 		}
api/handler_plan.go:1306 		ctx.session = sess.Name
api/handler_plan.go:1307 		tradeDate := ctx.tradeDate
api/handler_plan.go:1308 		if d, okD := kernel.PlanChainTradeDate(sess, now); okD {
api/handler_plan.go:1309 			tradeDate = d
api/handler_plan.go:1310 		}
api/handler_plan.go:1311 		ctx.tradeDate = tradeDate
api/handler_plan.go:1312 		if row, err := s.store.Plan().GetLatestPlanForTraderSession(tradeDate, sess.Name, traderID); err == nil && row != nil {
api/handler_plan.go:1313 			ctx.row, ctx.planID = row, row.PlanID
api/handler_plan.go:1314 			if doc, okDoc := s.resolvePlanFinal(row); okDoc {
api/handler_plan.go:1315 				ctx.doc = doc
api/handler_plan.go:1316 				// A dead / no-trade row is NOT the live plan the executor follows.
api/handler_plan.go:1317 				if row.Lifecycle == "active" {
api/handler_plan.go:1318 					ctx.kind = askContextActive
api/handler_plan.go:1319 				} else {
api/handler_plan.go:1320 					ctx.kind = askContextHistorical
api/handler_plan.go:1321 				}
api/handler_plan.go:1322 			}
api/handler_plan.go:1323 		}
api/handler_plan.go:1324 	}
api/handler_plan.go:1325
api/handler_plan.go:1326 	// 2) Nothing for the live session → the most recent stored plan, even dead.
api/handler_plan.go:1327 	if ctx.row == nil {
api/handler_plan.go:1328 		if rows, err := s.store.Plan().ListRecent(1); err == nil && len(rows) > 0 {
api/handler_plan.go:1329 			row := rows[0]
api/handler_plan.go:1330 			ctx.row, ctx.planID = row, row.PlanID
api/handler_plan.go:1331 			ctx.session, ctx.tradeDate = row.Session, row.TradeDate
api/handler_plan.go:1332 			if doc, okDoc := s.resolvePlanFinal(row); okDoc {
api/handler_plan.go:1333 				ctx.doc = doc
api/handler_plan.go:1334 				ctx.kind = askContextHistorical
api/handler_plan.go:1335 			}
api/handler_plan.go:1336 		}
api/handler_plan.go:1337 	}
api/handler_plan.go:1338
api/handler_plan.go:1339 	// Label + context blocks.
api/handler_plan.go:1340 	switch ctx.kind {
api/handler_plan.go:1341 	case askContextActive:
api/handler_plan.go:1342 		ctx.label = fmt.Sprintf("ACTIVE PLAN — %s v%d", ctx.session, ctx.row.Version)
api/handler_plan.go:1343 		ctx.planBlock = kernel.RenderPlanBlock(ctx.doc, ctx.session)
api/handler_plan.go:1344 	case askContextHistorical:
api/handler_plan.go:1345 		lifecycle := ""
api/handler_plan.go:1346 		if ctx.row != nil {
api/handler_plan.go:1347 			lifecycle = strings.ToUpper(ctx.row.Lifecycle)
api/handler_plan.go:1348 		}
api/handler_plan.go:1349 		ctx.label = fmt.Sprintf("HISTORICAL CONTEXT — %s %s v%d (%s), NOT the live plan",
api/handler_plan.go:1350 			ctx.tradeDate, ctx.session, ctx.row.Version, lifecycle)
api/handler_plan.go:1351 		var b strings.Builder
api/handler_plan.go:1352 		fmt.Fprintf(&b, "# CONTEXT: THIS IS NOT A LIVE PLAN\nYou are answering about a STORED plan that is no longer active (%s %s v%d, lifecycle %s).\nThere is nothing to patch. Explain what happened; do NOT propose changes.\n",
api/handler_plan.go:1353 			ctx.tradeDate, ctx.session, ctx.row.Version, ctx.row.Lifecycle)
api/handler_plan.go:1354 		if r := strings.TrimSpace(ctx.row.TriggerReason); r != "" {
api/handler_plan.go:1355 			fmt.Fprintf(&b, "why this version was written: %s\n", r)
api/handler_plan.go:1356 		}
api/handler_plan.go:1357 		if r := strings.TrimSpace(ctx.doc.Reasoning); r != "" {
api/handler_plan.go:1358 			fmt.Fprintf(&b, "the plan's own reasoning: %s\n", r)
api/handler_plan.go:1359 		}
api/handler_plan.go:1360 		if r := strings.TrimSpace(ctx.doc.DeathCondition); r != "" {
api/handler_plan.go:1361 			fmt.Fprintf(&b, "its stated death condition: %s\n", r)
api/handler_plan.go:1362 		}
api/handler_plan.go:1363 		b.WriteString("\n")
api/handler_plan.go:1364 		b.WriteString(kernel.RenderPlanBlock(ctx.doc, ctx.session))
api/handler_plan.go:1365 		ctx.planBlock = b.String()
api/handler_plan.go:1366 	default:
api/handler_plan.go:1367 		ctx.label = "NO PLAN — market facts only"
api/handler_plan.go:1368 		ctx.planBlock = "# CONTEXT: NO PLAN EXISTS\nNo plan is stored for this session. Answer from the live market facts below.\nThere is nothing to patch; do NOT propose changes.\n"
api/handler_plan.go:1369 	}
api/handler_plan.go:1370
api/handler_plan.go:1371 	// Live facts are attached in EVERY context — they are the part that is always
api/handler_plan.go:1372 	// true, and the no-plan case has nothing else.
api/handler_plan.go:1373 	if market.FuturesBarsProvider != nil {
api/handler_plan.go:1374 		if bars := market.FuturesBarsProvider(symbol, "1m", kernel.AISVPBarCount); len(bars) > 0 {
api/handler_plan.go:1375 			price, dATR := marketRef(symbol, now)
api/handler_plan.go:1376 			rule, _, left := s.planRules(traderID, ctx.session, ctx.tradeDate)
api/handler_plan.go:1377 			ctx.liveStatus = kernel.RenderPlanStatus(traderID, symbol, ctx.doc, bars, price, dATR, rule, left, now.UnixMilli(), ctx.row.CreatedAt.UnixMilli())
api/handler_plan.go:1378 		}
api/handler_plan.go:1379 	}
api/handler_plan.go:1380 	if ctx.planID == "" {
api/handler_plan.go:1381 		ctx.planID = s.store.Plan().ResolvePlanID(ctx.tradeDate, ctx.session, traderID)
api/handler_plan.go:1382 	}
api/handler_plan.go:1383 	return ctx
api/handler_plan.go:1384 }

api/handler_plan.go:1474 		Role: "planner", Content: reply.Summary, Evidence: reply.Evidence,

api/handler_plan.go:1477 		CreatedAt:   now.Unix() + 1, // keep the reply after the question in id/time order
api/handler_plan.go:1478 	})

api/handler_plan.go:1530 // handlePlanAskApply POST /api/plan/ask/apply — apply a PROPOSE-MERGE patch as an
api/handler_plan.go:1531 // overlay (origin planner-revised) and mark the reply applied. Bare-disagreement
api/handler_plan.go:1532 // replies carry no patch, so there is nothing to apply (guarded here too).
api/handler_plan.go:1533 // handlePlanAskDecline POST /api/plan/ask/decline — the owner declines a
api/handler_plan.go:1534 // PROPOSE-MERGE proposal.
api/handler_plan.go:1535 //
api/handler_plan.go:1536 // W16/R2. Declining used to be pure local React state: the panel set the same
api/handler_plan.go:1537 // flag Apply sets, so the card claimed "Applied — card updated" while NOTHING
api/handler_plan.go:1538 // was persisted. Two things were wrong with that. The copy lied, and the KPI
api/handler_plan.go:1539 // series lost half its signal: a proposal the owner explicitly REJECTED and one
api/handler_plan.go:1540 // they simply never answered both sat at applied=false, indistinguishable.
api/handler_plan.go:1541 //
api/handler_plan.go:1542 // The decline is recorded as an OWNER-role row carrying the same plan/trigger
api/handler_plan.go:1543 // provenance as the planner reply it answers, with verdict DECLINED. Owner rows
api/handler_plan.go:1544 // are excluded from the planner-reply counters (VerdictStats filters
api/handler_plan.go:1545 // role='planner'), so DEFEND/CONCEDE/PROPOSE-MERGE keep their existing meaning
api/handler_plan.go:1546 // and the decline count joins the same series without redefining it.
api/handler_plan.go:1547 //
api/handler_plan.go:1548 // The plan is never touched — declining is the absence of a mutation, by design.
api/handler_plan.go:1549 func (s *Server) handlePlanAskDecline(c *gin.Context) {

api/handler_plan.go:1994 // resolvePlanFinal folds a plan row's overlays into plan_final (the doc the card

api/handler_plan.go:1996 func (s *Server) resolvePlanFinal(row *store.PlanDB) (kernel.PlanDoc, bool) {
api/handler_plan.go:1997 	var doc kernel.PlanDoc
api/handler_plan.go:1998 	if json.Unmarshal([]byte(row.Doc), &doc) != nil {
api/handler_plan.go:1999 		return kernel.PlanDoc{}, false
api/handler_plan.go:2000 	}
api/handler_plan.go:2001 	overlays, _ := s.store.Plan().ListOverlays(row.PlanID, row.Version)
api/handler_plan.go:2002 	if len(overlays) == 0 {
api/handler_plan.go:2003 		return doc, true
api/handler_plan.go:2004 	}
api/handler_plan.go:2005 	patches := make([]string, 0, len(overlays))
api/handler_plan.go:2006 	for _, ov := range overlays {
api/handler_plan.go:2007 		patches = append(patches, ov.Patch)
api/handler_plan.go:2008 	}
api/handler_plan.go:2009 	base, err := json.Marshal(doc)
api/handler_plan.go:2010 	if err != nil {
api/handler_plan.go:2011 		return doc, true
api/handler_plan.go:2012 	}
api/handler_plan.go:2013 	final, _ := kernel.ApplyOverlayPatches(base, patches)
api/handler_plan.go:2014 	var merged kernel.PlanDoc
api/handler_plan.go:2015 	if json.Unmarshal(final, &merged) == nil && kernel.ValidatePlanDocWithCaps(&merged, kernel.PlanHardMaxLevels, kernel.PlanHardMaxScenarios) == nil {
api/handler_plan.go:2016 		return merged, true
api/handler_plan.go:2017 	}
api/handler_plan.go:2018 	return doc, true
api/handler_plan.go:2019 }
api/handler_plan.go:2020
api/handler_plan.go:2021 // RealignDebounceSec collapses rapid saves (bulk-add rows, fast successive edits)
api/handler_plan.go:2022 // into ONE call.
api/handler_plan.go:2023 const RealignDebounceSec int64 = 20
api/handler_plan.go:2024
api/handler_plan.go:2025 // realignGateDecision is the pure auto-trigger gate: "" = proceed with the call,
api/handler_plan.go:2026 // "debounced" = an auto re-align already ran inside the window (this is what makes
api/handler_plan.go:2027 // a bulk-add batch ONE call), "capped" = the auto budget for this plan is spent and
api/handler_plan.go:2028 // the owner should use the manual button. A MANUAL re-align bypasses both — the
api/handler_plan.go:2029 // owner asked for it explicitly. lastAt < 0 means "no previous auto re-align".
api/handler_plan.go:2030 func realignGateDecision(manual bool, used, capN int, lastAt, nowUnix int64) string {
api/handler_plan.go:2031 	if manual {
api/handler_plan.go:2032 		return ""
api/handler_plan.go:2033 	}
api/handler_plan.go:2034 	if lastAt >= 0 && nowUnix-lastAt < RealignDebounceSec {
api/handler_plan.go:2035 		return "debounced"
api/handler_plan.go:2036 	}
api/handler_plan.go:2037 	if capN > 0 && used >= capN {
api/handler_plan.go:2038 		return "capped"
api/handler_plan.go:2039 	}
api/handler_plan.go:2040 	return ""
api/handler_plan.go:2041 }
api/handler_plan.go:2042
api/handler_plan.go:2043 // handlePlanRealign POST /api/plan/realign — re-examine the whole plan after an
api/handler_plan.go:2044 // owner edit. Always 200 with a `status` the FE renders:
api/handler_plan.go:2045 //
api/handler_plan.go:2046 //	proposal   → PROPOSE-MERGE + patch (the card slides in; Apply-gated)
api/handler_plan.go:2047 //	no-change  → the plan stands (quiet auto-fading chip)
api/handler_plan.go:2048 //	skipped    → no active plan / night / disabled / expired / day-plan off
api/handler_plan.go:2049 //	debounced  → an identical-window call already ran (bulk-add safety)

api/handler_plan.go:2258 // by construction — it exists so the owner can see the automation's running cost,
api/handler_plan.go:2259 // not for billing.
api/handler_plan.go:2260 func estimateRealignCostUSD(prompt, reply string) float64 {
api/handler_plan.go:2261 	const (
api/handler_plan.go:2262 		inPerMTok   = 0.28 // deepseek-v4-pro input, USD / 1M tokens
api/handler_plan.go:2263 		outPerMTok  = 0.42 // deepseek-v4-pro output
api/handler_plan.go:2264 		charsPerTok = 4.0
api/handler_plan.go:2265 	)
api/handler_plan.go:2266 	inTok := float64(len(prompt)) / charsPerTok
api/handler_plan.go:2267 	outTok := float64(len(reply)) / charsPerTok
api/handler_plan.go:2268 	return (inTok*inPerMTok + outTok*outPerMTok) / 1_000_000
api/handler_plan.go:2269 }

store/touch_outcomes.go:131 func (s *TouchOutcomeStore) RatesBy(column string) ([]OutcomeRate, error) {

store/touch_outcomes.go:162 			byGroup[r.Grp] = g

store/touch_outcomes.go:172 		}
store/touch_outcomes.go:173 	}

store/touch_outcomes.go:175 	for _, g := range order {
store/touch_outcomes.go:176 		out = append(out, *byGroup[g])
store/touch_outcomes.go:177 	}
store/touch_outcomes.go:178 	return out, nil

store/touch_outcomes.go:189 func (s *TouchOutcomeStore) DetectorReport() string {

store/touch_outcomes.go:191 		return "detector report: store unavailable"
store/touch_outcomes.go:192 	}
store/touch_outcomes.go:193 	var b []byte
store/touch_outcomes.go:194 	add := func(f string, a ...any) { b = append(b, []byte(sprintf(f, a...))...) }
store/touch_outcomes.go:195 	total := s.CountOutcomes()

store/touch_outcomes.go:197 	if total == 0 {

store/touch_outcomes.go:199 		return string(b)
store/touch_outcomes.go:200 	}
store/touch_outcomes.go:201 	for _, dim := range []struct{ label, col string }{
store/touch_outcomes.go:202 		{"ALL", ""}, {"by kind", "level_kind"}, {"by session", "session"}, {"by ordinal", "ordinal"},
store/touch_outcomes.go:203 	} {
store/touch_outcomes.go:204 		rates, err := s.RatesBy(dim.col)
store/touch_outcomes.go:205 		if err != nil {

store/touch_outcomes.go:212 			if g == "" {
store/touch_outcomes.go:213 				g = "(all)"
store/touch_outcomes.go:214 			}
store/touch_outcomes.go:215 			if r.N() == 0 {
store/touch_outcomes.go:216 				add("    %-14s n=0 — no resolved episodes (ambiguous=%d)\n", g, r.Ambiguous)
store/touch_outcomes.go:217 				continue
store/touch_outcomes.go:218 			}
store/touch_outcomes.go:219 			lo, hi := wilson(r.P(), r.N())
store/touch_outcomes.go:220 			note := ""
store/touch_outcomes.go:221 			if r.N() < TouchRateFloor {
store/touch_outcomes.go:222 				note = sprintf("  — n<%d, DESCRIPTIVE ONLY", TouchRateFloor)
store/touch_outcomes.go:223 			}
store/touch_outcomes.go:224 			ambShare := float64(r.Ambiguous) / float64(r.N()+r.Ambiguous) * 100
store/touch_outcomes.go:225 			add("    %-14s p(hold)=%.3f [%.3f, %.3f] n=%d · ambiguous=%d (%.1f%%)%s\n",
store/touch_outcomes.go:226 				g, r.P(), lo, hi, r.N(), r.Ambiguous, ambShare, note)
store/touch_outcomes.go:227 		}
store/touch_outcomes.go:228 	}
store/touch_outcomes.go:229 	return string(b)
store/touch_outcomes.go:230 }

store/touch_outcomes.go:235 func sprintf(f string, a ...any) string { return fmt.Sprintf(f, a...) }
store/touch_outcomes.go:236
store/touch_outcomes.go:237 // wilson is the 95% score interval — the same formula the detector uses, kept
store/touch_outcomes.go:238 // here so the report cannot quote a bare proportion.
store/touch_outcomes.go:239 func wilson(p float64, n int) (lo, hi float64) {
store/touch_outcomes.go:240 	if n <= 0 {
store/touch_outcomes.go:241 		return 0, 0
store/touch_outcomes.go:242 	}
store/touch_outcomes.go:243 	const z = 1.959963984540054
store/touch_outcomes.go:244 	nf := float64(n)
store/touch_outcomes.go:245 	den := 1 + z*z/nf
store/touch_outcomes.go:246 	c := (p + z*z/(2*nf)) / den
store/touch_outcomes.go:247 	h := z * math.Sqrt(p*(1-p)/nf+z*z/(4*nf*nf)) / den
store/touch_outcomes.go:248 	return c - h, c + h
store/touch_outcomes.go:249 }

store/touch_outcomes.go:253 // read of a session records everything it finds.

store/touch_outcomes.go:263 		return 0
